Arthaviniścayasūtra + Nibandhana
N. H. Samtani
OCR @2024, Dharmamitra project, UC Berkeley, Sebastian Nehrdich
arthaviniścayasūtram
namo buddhāya |
evaṃ mayā śrutam | ekasmin samaye bhagavān śrāvastyāṃ viharati sma pūrvārāme ṣmagārasādaḥ prāsāde mahatā bhikṣusaṅghena sārdhamardhatrayodaśabhirbhikṣuśataiḥ |
tatra ca bhagavān bhikṣūngambhīrasukhūrodārānanandambherīsvareṇa bhikṣūnāmantrayate sma—"dharmaṃ[2]
voṃ bhikṣavo deśayiṣyāmi, ādyau kalyāṇaṃ madhye kalyāṇaṃ paryavasāne kalyāṇaṃ svarthaṃ suvyañjanaṃ, kevalaṃ paripūrṇaṃ pariśuddhaṃ paryavadātaṃ brahmacaryaṃ saṃprakāśayiṣye—
yadutārthaviniścayaṃ nāma dharmaparyāyam |
tacchṛṇuta tādra ca śṛṇu ca manasikuru bhāṣiṣye' |
"sādhu bhagavan" iti te bhikṣavo bhagavataḥ pratyaśrauṣuḥ |
bhagavānastadavocat |
katamaścāsau bhikṣavo'rthaviniścayo nāma dharmaparyāyaḥ ?[3]
bodhyajñāni, (19) āyāryaśāsai mārge, (20) bodhyaśākārāyānasmṛtiḥ,
(21) catvāri sota ārya(2a)satyāni, (22) daśa tathāgatabalāni,
(23) catvāri vaiśāradyāni, (24) catasraḥ pratisaṃvidaḥ, (25) aṣṭādaśāveṇikāḥ
buddhadharmāḥ, (26) dvātriṃśanmahāpuruṣalakṣaṇāni, (27) aśītiranuvyañjanāni |
athaṃ śikṣate 'pratiniścayasya dharmaparyāyasyoheśaḥ |
(1) pañca skandhāḥ
tatra bhikṣavaḥ katame pañca skandhāḥ ?
tadyathā— (1) rūpaskandhaḥ, (2) vedanāskandhaḥ, (3) saṃjñāskandhaḥ, (4) saṃskāra-
skandhaḥ, (5) vijñānaskandhaśceti |
ime bhikṣavaḥ pañca skandhāḥ |
(2) pañcopādānaskandhāḥ
tatra bhikṣavaḥ katame pañcopādānaskandhāḥ ?
tadyathā--( 1 ) rūpopādānaskandhaḥ, ( 2 ) vedanopādānaskandhaḥ, ( 3 )
saṃjñopādānaskandhaḥ, ( 4 ) saṃskāropādānaskandhaḥ, ( 5 ) vijñānopādānaskandhaḥ |
ime bhikṣavaḥ pañcopādānaskandhāḥ |[4]
(3) aṣṭādaśa dhātavaḥ
tatra śiṣyaḥ: katame'ṣṭādaśa dhātavaḥ ?
tadyathā:— (1) cakṣurdhātuḥ, (2) rūpadhātuḥ, (3) cakṣurvijñānadhātuḥ, (4) śrotradhātuḥ, (5) śabdadhātuḥ, (6) śrotravijñānadhātuḥ, (7) ghrāṇadhātuḥ, (8) gandhadhātuḥ, (9) ghrāṇavijñānadhātuḥ, (10) jihvādhātuḥ, (11) rasadhātuḥ, (12) jihvāvijñānadhātuḥ, (13) kāyadhātuḥ, (14) spaṣṭavyadhātuḥ, (15) kāyavijñānadhātuḥ, (16) manodhātuḥ, (17) dharmadhātuḥ, (18) manovijñānadhātuḥ |
ime śiṣyatra ucyante'ṣṭādaśa dhātavaḥ |
(4) dvādaśāyatanāni
tatra śiṣyaḥ: katamāni dvādaśāyatanāni ?
tadyathā:— (1) cakṣurādhyātmikamāyatanam, (2) rūpaṃ bāhyamāyatanam, (3) śrotramādhyātmikamāyatanam, (4) śabdo bāhyamāyatanam, (5) ghrāṇamādhyātmikamāyatanam, (6) gandho bāhyamāyatanam, (7) jihvādhyātmikamāyatanam, (8) raso bāhyamāyatanam, (9) kāyādhyātmikamāyatanam, (10) sparśo bāhyamāyatanam, (11) mana ādhyātmikamāyatanam, (12) dharmo bāhyamāyatanam |
imāni śiṣyatra ucyante dvādaśāyatanāni |[5]
(5) dvādaśāṅgapratītyasamutpādaḥ
tatra bhikṣavaḥ katamo dvādaśāṅgaḥ pratītyasamutpādaḥ ?
yadyūta—avidyāṃ satīdaṃ bhavati, adhyonyādvidamutpadyate |
yadyūta—avidyāpratyayāḥ saṃskārāḥ, saṃskārapratyayaṃ vijñānaṃ, vijñānapratyayaṃ nāmarūpaṃ, nāmarūpapratyayaṃ ṣaḍāyatanaṃ, ṣaḍāyatanapratyayaḥ sparśaḥ, sparśapratyayā vedanā, vedanāpratyayā tṛṣṇā, tṛṣṇāpratyayamupādānaṃ, upādānapratyayo bhavaḥ, bhavapratyayā jātiḥ, jātipratyayā jarāmaraṇaśokaparidevaduḥkhadaurmanasyopāyāsāḥ saṃbhavanti |
evamasya kevalasya mahato duḥkhaskandhasya samudayo bhavati |
avidyānirodhātsaṃskāranirodhaḥ, saṃskāranirodhādvijñānanirodhaḥ, vijñānanirodhānnāmarūpanirodhaḥ, nāmarūpanirodhātṣaḍāyatananirodhaḥ, ṣaḍāyatananirodhātsparśanirodhaḥ, sparśanirodhād vedanānirodhaḥ, vedanānirodhāttṛṣṇānirodhaḥ, tṛṣṇānirodhādupādānanirodhaḥ, upādānanirodhād bhavanirodhaḥ, bhavanirodhājjātinirodhaḥ, jātinirodhājjarāmaraṇaśokaparidevaduḥkhadaurmanasyopāyāsā nirudhyante |
evamasya kevalasya mahato duḥkhaskandhasya nirodho bha( vi )ti |
tatra katamā avidyā ? yadyūta—pūrvānte'jñānam, aparānte'jñānam,[6]
praśyuddhe jñānam,
adhyātme jñānam,
bahirdhā jñānam,
adhyātmabahirdhā jñānam,
karmaṇyajñānam,
vipāke jñānam,
karmavipāke jñānam,
pūrvānte jñānam,
aparānte jñānam,
pūrvāparānte jñānam,
hetāvajñānam,
phale jñānam,
hetuphale jñānam,
hetusamutpanneṣu dharmeṣu jñānam,
pratītyasamutpādeṣu jñānam,
pratītyasamutpanneṣu dharmeṣu ajñānam,
buddhe jñānam,
dharme jñānam,
saṃghe jñānam,
duḥkhe jñānam,
samudaye jñānam,
nirodhe jñānam,
mārge jñānam,
kuśalākuśaleṣu dharmeṣu ajñānam,
sāvadyānavadyeṣu dharmeṣu ajñānam,
sevitavyāsevitavyeṣu dharmeṣu ajñānam,
hīnapraṇīteṣu kṛṣṇaśukleṣu dharmeṣu ajñānam,
prathur vā sarvathedaṃ[7]
yathāśrutamajñānamevetomamaniścayapramāḥ saṃgṛhīto'vidyāprakāram |
ithamucyate'vidyeti |
avidyāpratyayā: saṃskārā iti |
saṃskārāḥ katame ? trayaḥ saṃskārāḥ:—
kāyasaṃskāraḥ, vāksaṃskāraḥ, manaḥsaṃskāraḥ |
kāyasaṃskāraḥ katamaḥ ? āśvāsaḥ praśvāsaḥ |
kāyiko kṣepe dharmaḥ, kāyaniśritaḥ, kāyapratibaddhaḥ, kāya niśritya vartate |
tasmādāśvāsaḥ praśvāsaḥ kāyasaṃskāra ityucyate |
vāksaṃskāraḥ[8]
katamaḥ ? vinaśyartho vicāryo vācyaṃ bhāṣate, nāvinaśyartho nāvicāryo |
tasmādvinaśvaro vāksaṃskāra ityucyate |
manaḥsaṃskāraḥ katamaḥ ? svatantraśca yā cetanā, mūḍhaśca yā cetanā |
caitasiko dharmaḥ śubhaḥ, cittanimittaḥ, cittapratibaddhaḥ, citte niśritya pravartate |
tasmāccetanā manaḥsaṃskāra ityucyate |
ime bhaṃdavaḥ trayaḥ saṃskārā ucyante |
saṃskārapratyayaṃ vijñānamiti |
vijñānaṃ katamat ? ṣaṭ vijñānakāyāḥ
katame ṣaṭ ? tadyathā—cakṣurvijñānaṃ, śrotravijñānaṃ, ghrāṇavijñānaṃ, jihvāvijñānaṃ,
kāyavijñānaṃ, manovijñānam |
ime ṣaḍ vijñānakāyā vijñānamityucyante |
vijñānapratyayaṃ nāmarūpamiti |
tatra katamannāma ? catvāro'rūpiṇaḥ skandhāḥ
katame catvāraḥ ? vedanāskandhaḥ, saṃjñāskandhaḥ, saṃskāraskandhaḥ, vijñānaskandhaḥ[9]
idaṃ nāma | rūpaṃ katamat ? yadyakiñcit rūpaṃ tat—cakṣurādi mahābhūtāni,
catvāri ca mahābhūtānyupādāya | katamāni catvāri ? tadyathā—pṛthivīdhātuḥ,
abdhātuḥ, tejodhātuḥ, vāyudhātuśca | pṛthivīdhātuḥ katamaḥ ? yad gurutvaṃ
karkaśatvaṃ ca | abdhātuḥ katamaḥ ? yad dravatvamabhiṣyandanatvaṃ ca | tejodhātuḥ
katamaḥ ? yaduṣṇatvaṃ ca paripācanatvaṃ ca | vāyudhātuḥ katamaḥ ? yadātrubhyanamasāraṇa-
laghusamudīraṇatvaṃ ca | yadidaṃ rūpaṃ pūrvaṃ ca nāma tadubhayamekatrāmisaṃkṣipya
nāmarūpamityucyate |
nāmarūpamayaṃ pañcāyatanamiti | pañcāyatanaṃ katamat ? pañcaskandhakā-
nyāyatanāni | tadyathā—cakṣurāyatanaṃ, śrotrāyatanaṃ, ghrāṇāyatanaṃ, jihvāyatanaṃ,
kāyāyatanaṃ, mana āyatanam |
ityucyate pañcāyatanam |[90]
ṣaḍyatanandriyāḥ sparśa iti|
sparśe katamaḥ? ṣaṭ sparśakāyāḥ|
ṣaṭ? cakṣuḥsaṃsparśaḥ, śrotrasaṃsparśaḥ, ghrāṇasaṃsparśaḥ, jihvāsaṃsparśaḥ, kāyasaṃsparśaḥ, manaḥsaṃsparśa iti|
abhimucyate sparśaḥ|
sparśakāyayāḥ vedaneti| vedanā katamā? ṣaḍvedanākāyāḥ|
saṃsparśajā vedanā—sukhā, duḥkhā, aduḥkhāsukhā ca|
jihvākāyamanaḥsaṃsparśajā vedanā—sukhā, duḥkhā, aduḥkhāsukhā ca|
īyamucyate vedanā|
vedanākāyayāḥ tṛṣṇeti| tṛṣṇā katamā? ṣaṭ tṛṣṇākāyāḥ|
ṣaṭ? rūpatṛṣṇā, śabdatṛṣṇā, gandhatṛṣṇā, rasatṛṣṇā, sparśatṛṣṇā, dharmatṛṣṇeti|
tṛṣṇākāyayāupādānamiti| upādānaṃ katamam? catvāryupādānāni|
katamāni catvāri? kāmopādānaṃ, dṛṣṭyupādānaṃ, śīlavratopādānaṃ, ātmavādopādānaṃ ceti|[11]
upādānamayoye mantra iti|
mantraḥ kattamaḥ ? trayo mantrāḥ|
kattame trayaḥ ?
tadyathā—kāmabhavaḥ, rūpabhavaḥ, arūpyabhavaḥ|
tatra kāmabhavaḥ katamaḥ ?
tadyathā—uṣṇanarakā adho|
katame'dho ?
tadyathā—saṃjīvaḥ, kālasūtraḥ, saṃghātaḥ, rauravaḥ, mahārauravaḥ, tapanaḥ, pratāpanaḥ, avīciśca|
śītanarakā adho|
katame'dho ?
tadyathā—arbudaḥ, nirarbudaḥ, aṭaṭaḥ, hahavaḥ, huhuvaḥ, utpalaḥ, padmaḥ, mahāpadmaḥ|
pretāḥ, tiryañcaḥ, manuṣyāḥ ṣaṭ kāmāvacarāśca devāḥ|
katame ṣaṭ ?
cāturmahārājikāḥ, trāyastriṃśāḥ, yāmāḥ, tuṣitāḥ, nirmāṇaratayaḥ, paranirmitavaśavartino devāḥ|[12]
tatra rūpasaṃdhaḥ katamaḥ ? tadyathā—navakāyikāḥ, navapurohitāḥ, mahābrahmapariṣadyāḥ, apramāṇābhāḥ, ābhāsvarāḥ, parīttaśubhāḥ, śubhakṛtsnāḥ, anabhrakāḥ, puṇyaprasavāḥ, bṛhatphalāḥ, abṛhāḥ, atapāḥ, sudṛśāḥ, sudarśanāḥ, akaniṣṭhāśceti |
śābdamayaḥ katamaḥ | tadyathā—'śākyānantyāyatanaṃ, vijñānānantyāyatanam, ākiñcanyāyatanaṃ, naivasaṃjñānāsaṃjñāyatanam | arūpiṇāṃ devānāṃ viṣayāpratyayacaturviśeṣo'pi:' | 'dharmapratyaye śābdamayāśuddhaḥ' (sic) |
ime trayo mayāḥ'
bhavapratyayā cātitṛṣṭiḥ | jātiḥ katamā ? yā teṣāṃ teṣāṃ dharmajātānāṃ tatttvatā'nuttaraḥ sattvanikāyo jātiḥ, saṃjātiḥ, upapattiḥ, avakrāntiḥ, abhinirvṛttiḥ, prādurbhāvaḥ[13]
rūpapratikṣepaṃ; dhātupratikṣepaṃ; āyatanānāṃ pratikṣepaṃ; rūpeṇānāmabhinirvṛttaṃ;
jīvitendriyasaṃyogaṃ; niṣkāyaparamāṇunāṃ samavāyānam |
ityamucyate jāti: |
jātiṣadyaṃ jarāpraṇumiti | jarā katamā ? yatu khlāsthyaṃ, pālthicaṃ,
valīmukhurotāṃ, jīrṇotā, śuklatā, kubjagopānasīkṛtatā, khulukhuluniśvāsapraśvāsaḥ-
kūpṭatā, tilakālakopadūtagātratā, daṇḍāvalambanatā, purataḥ prāgbhārakāyatā,
indriyāṇāṃ paripākaḥ parihāniḥ; saṃskārāṇāṃ purāṇībhāvo jarjarībhāvaśca, ghanavatvaṃ,
mandavatvaṃ, hāniḥ, vṛddhāṇi: |
ityamucyate jarā |[14]
maraṇaṃ katamat ? trayeṣāṃ teṣāṃ sattvānāṃ tasmāttasmātsattvanikāyāccyutiḥ|
cyavanatā, bhedaḥ; śastrahāniḥ; maraṇaṃ kālakriyā, āyuṣo hāniḥ; ūṣmaṇo hāniḥ; jīvitendriyasya nirodhaḥ; rūpaskandhānāṃ nirodhaḥ|
idamucyate maraṇam|
yadetanmaraṇaṃ pūrvoktaṃ caṃ jarā tadubhayamekatrābhisaṃkṣipya jarāmaraṇamityucyate|
ayaṃ dvādaśo dvādaśāṅgaḥ pratītyasamutpādaḥ|
(6) catvāryāryasatyāni
tatra [ bhikṣavaḥ] katamāni catvāryāryasatyāni ?
tadyathā—duḥkhamāryasatyam, duḥkhasamudaya āryasatyam, duḥkhanirodha āryasatyam, duḥkhanirodhagāminīpratipadāryasatyam|
(1) tatra duḥkhamāryasatyaṃ katamat ? tadyathā —jātirduḥkham, jarāduḥkham, vyādhirduḥkham, maraṇaṃ duḥkham, priyaviprayogo duḥkham, apriyasaṃprayogo[25]
duḥkham, yadvīcchanna payoṣamaṇo na spate tadapi duḥkham, sakṣiptena pañcopādā-
rakkandhā duḥkham |
idamucyate duḥkhamāyarsatyam |
(2) duḥkhasamudayaṃ āryasatyaṃ katamat ? tadyathā—tṛṣṇā ponarbhavikī,
nandīrāgasahagatā, tatratatrābhinandinī |
idamucyate duḥkhasamudaya āryasatyam |
(3) duḥkhanirodha āryasatyaṃ katamat ? yadasyā eva tṛṣṇāyāḥ ponarbha-
vikyā nandīrāgasahagatāyā tatratatrābhinandinyā a(je) śeṣataḥ prahāṇaṃ, pratiniḥ-
sargo, vyantībhāvaḥ, kṣayo, virāgo, nirodho, vyupaśamo'staṃgamaḥ |
idamucyate duḥkhanirodha āryasatyam |
(4) tatra duḥkhanirodhagāminīpratipadāryasatyaṃ katamat ? ayameva samyagaṣṭāṅga-
dirāryamārgaḥ | tadyathā—samyagdṛṣṭiḥ, samyaksaṅkalpaḥ, samyagvāk,
samyakkarmāntaḥ, samyagājīvaḥ, samyagvyāyāmaḥ, samyaksmṛtiḥ, samyaksamādhiriti |[16]
idamucyate duḥkhanirodhamārgamitipratipadyatarām |
imāni bhikṣavaścatvāryāryasatyāni |
(7) dvāviṃśatirindriyāṇi
tatra bhikṣavaḥ katamāni dvāviṃśatiḥ(7.)rindriyāṇi |
yadutaṃ,—(1) cakṣurindriyam, (2) śrotrendriyam, (3) ghrāṇendriyam, (4) jihvendriyam, (5) kāyendriyam, (6) mana indriyam, (7) strīndriyam, (8) puruṣendriyam, (9) jīvitendriyam, (10) duḥkhendriyam, (11) daurmanasyendriyam, (12) sukhendriyam, (13) saumanasyendriyam, (14) upekṣendriyam, (15) śraddhendriyam, (16) vīryendriyam, (17) smṛtīndriyam, (18) samādhīndriyam, (19) prajñendriyam, (20) anājñātamājñāsyāmīndriyam, (21) ājñendriyam, (22) ājñātāvīndriyam |
imāni bhikṣavo dvāviṃśatirindriyāṇi |[17]
catvāri dhyānāni
(8) catvāri dhyānāni
tatra bhikṣavaḥ katamāni catvāri dhyānāni ?
(1) iha bhikṣavo bhikṣuḥ viviktaṃ kāmaiḥ viviktaṃ pāpakairakuśalairdharmaiḥ savicāraṃ savicāraṃ vivekajaṃ prītisukhaṃ prathamaṃ dhyānamupasaṃpadya viharati |
(2) sa vicarkavicārāṇāṃ vyupaśamādadhyātmasaṃprasādāccetasa ekotibhāvād-vicarkavicāraṃ samādhijaṃ prītisukhaṃ dvitīyaṃ dhyānamupasaṃpadya viharati |
(3) sa prītervirāgāddupekṣako viharati, smṛtaḥ saṃprajānan sukhaṃ ca kāyena pratisaṃvedayati ; yattadāryā ācakṣate "upekṣakaḥ smṛtimān sukhavihārī" iti tṛtīyaṃ dhyānamupasaṃpadya viharati |
(4) sa sukhasya ca prahāṇāt duḥkhasya ca prahāṇāt pūrvaiva ca saumanasya-daurmanasyayorastaṃgamādduḥkhāsukhamupekṣāsmṛtipariśuddhaṃ caturthaṃ dhyānamupasaṃpadya viharati |
imāni bhikṣavaścatvāri dhyānāni |[16]
(9) catvāra āhutyasamāpattayaḥ
tatra śiṣyaḥ: katamāścatasra āhutyasamāpattayaḥ ?
(1) iha śiṣyeṇo niṣṭhaḥ: sarvaśo rūpasaṃjñānāṃ samatikramāt‌ pratighasaṃjñānāmastaṃgamāccālaṃbanānāmananusmaraṇādanantamākāśamityākāśānantyāyatanamupasaṃpadya viharati |
(2) sarvaśo ākāśānantyāyatanaṃ samatikramyānantaṃ vijñānamiti vijñānānantyāyatanamupasaṃpadya viharati |
(3) sarvaśo vijñānānantyāyatanaṃ samatikramya 'nāsti kiṃcidi'ti ākiṃcanyāyatanamupasaṃpadya viharati |
(4) sarvaśo ākiṃcanyāyatanaṃ samatikramya naivasaṃjñānāsaṃjñāyatanamupasaṃpadya viharati
imā śiṣyava ucyante catasra āhutyasamāpattayaḥ |
(10) catvāro brāhmavihārāḥ
[tatra] katame [śiṣyavaḥ] catvāro brāhmavihārāḥ ?[16]
maitrīsahagatena cittenāviriṇāsampannotsāhācittena vipulena mahadgatenādvayenāpramāṇena chubhāvitena ekāṃ diśaṃ samabhiṣṭucya sphuritvā upasaṃpadya viharati |
tathā dvitīyāṃ, tathā tṛtīyāṃ, tathā caturthīṃ, ityūrdhvamadhastiryakca, sarvadhaḥ sarvātvatsamaṃ loke maitrīsahagatena cittenāviriṇāsampannotsāhācittena vipulena mahadgatenādvayenāpramāṇena chubhāvitena ekāṃ diśaṃ samabhiṣṭucya sphuritvā upasaṃpadya viharati |
evaṃ (1) karuṇāsahagatena (2) muditāsahagatena (3) upekṣāsahagatena cittenāviriṇāsampannotsāhācittena vipulena mahadgatenādvayenāpramāṇena chubhāvitena ekāṃ diśaṃsamabhiṣṭucya sphuritvā upasaṃpadya viharati |
ime bhikṣavaścatvāro brahmavihārāḥ |
catvāraḥ pratipadaḥ
tatra katamāścatvāraḥ pratipadaḥ ?
(1) asti bhikṣavo duḥkhā pratipad dhandhābhijñā ; (2) asti bhikṣavo duḥkhā pratipad kṣiprābhijñā ; (3) asti [bhikṣavaḥ] sukhā pratipad dhandhābhijñā ; (4) asti [bhikṣavaḥ] sukhā pratipad kṣiprābhijñā |[20]
(1) tatra katamā duḥkhā pratipad dhanyāmitrā ?
ihaikatyaḥ pudgalaḥ prakṛtyaiva tīvrāgo bhavati, tīvradveṣo bhavati, tīvramoho bhavati |
so'pīkṣaṇaṃ tīvrarāgatayā rāgajaṃ duḥkhadaurmanasyaṃ pratisaṃvedayati apīkṣaṇaṃ tīvradveṣatayā dveṣajaṃ duḥkhadaurmanasyaṃ pratisaṃvedayati, apīkṣaṇaṃ tīvramohatayā mohajaṃ duḥkhadaurmanasyaṃ pratisaṃvedayati |
tasyemāni pañca lokottarāṇīndriyāṇi dhanyāni bhavanti, yadyuttādīkṣaṇānya śīghravāhīnyāsavāṇāṃ kṣayāya |
katamāni pañca ?
yaduta---addhendriyaṃ, bodhyindriyaṃ, smṛtīndriyaṃ, samādhyindriyaṃ, prajñendriyam |
sa pañcānāṃ lokottarāṇāmindriyāṇāṃ yadutpādavṛddhirvihārātmaka dharmamaitrānandaṃ samājasmṛśati yaduttāvakṣaṇāṃ kṣayāya |
iyaṃ duḥkhā pratipad dhanyāmitrā |
(2) tatra katamā duḥkhā pratipad kṣiprāmitrā ?
ihaikatyaḥ pudgalaḥ prakṛtyaiva tīvrāgo bhavati, tīvradveṣo bhavati, tīvramoho bhavati |
so'pīkṣaṇaṃ tīvrarāgatayā rāgajaṃ duḥkhadaurmanasyaṃ pratisaṃvedayati, apīkṣaṇaṃ tīvradveṣatayā dveṣajaṃ duḥkhadaurmanasyaṃ pratisaṃvedayati, apīkṣaṇaṃ tīvramohatayā mohajaṃ duḥkhadaurmanasyaṃ pratisaṃvedayati |
tasyemāni pañca lokottarāṇi indriyāṇya adhimātrāṇi bhavanti, tīkṣṇāni śīghravāhīni |
katamāni pañca ? yaduta---[21]
śraddhendriyaṃ, vīryendriyaṃ, smṛtīndriyaṃ, samādhīndriyaṃ, prajñendriyam | saṃ pūrṇāṃ pañcānāṃ lokottarāṇāmindriyāṇāmabhijñātvāt tīkṣṇatvācca śīghravāhitvācca kṣipravedhānantvarthaṃ samādhiṃ spṛśati yadutāvadāṇāṃ kṣayāya |
iyaṃ duḥkhā pratipadu vidyābhijñā |
(3) tatra katamā sukhā pratipadu dhanyābhijñā ?
ihaikatyaḥ pudugalaḥ prakṛtyaivālagrāmo bhavati, alpaduḥkho bhavati, alpamoho bhavati | so'smagāmatayā nādhīkṣṇaṃ rāgajaṃ duḥkhadaurmanasyaṃ pratisaṃvedayati, alpa-devasayā nādhīkṣṇaṃ dveṣajaṃ duḥkhadaurmanasyaṃ pratisaṃvedayati, alpamohatayā nādhīkṣṇaṃ mohajaṃ duḥkhadaurmanasyaṃ pratisaṃvedayati | tasyemāni pañca lokottarāṇīndriyāṇi dhanyāni bhavanti, mṛdūni, na tīkṣṇāni, na śīghravāhīni | katamāni pañca ? yaduta śraddhendriyaṃ, vīryendriyaṃ, smṛtīndriyaṃ, samādhīndriyaṃ, prajñendriyam | sa pūrṇāṃ pañcānāṃ lokottarāṇāmindriyāṇāṃ dhanyatvādatīkṣṇatvāccāmṛdutvādaśīghravāhitvācca dhanyavedhānantvarthaṃ-samādhiṃ spṛśati yaduta āsravāṇāṃ kṣayāya |
iyathucyate sukhā pratipadu dhanyābhijñā |
(4) tatra katamā sukhā pratipadu vidyābhijñā ? ihaikatyaḥ pudugalaḥ prakṛtyaiva-lagrāmo bhavati, alpaduḥkho, alpamohaḥ | so'lparāgatayā nādhīkṣṇaṃ rāgajaṃ duḥkhadaurmanasyaṃ pratisaṃvedayati, alpadveṣatayā nādhīkṣṇaṃ dveṣajaṃ duḥkhadaurmanasyaṃ prati-saṃvedayati, alpamohatayā nādhīkṣṇaṃ mohajaṃ duḥkhadaurmanasyaṃ pratisaṃvedayati | tasyemāni pañca lokottarāṇīndriyāṇi abhimatrāṇi bhavanti, tīkṣṇāni śīghravāhīni | katamāni pañca ? yaduta—śraddhendriyaṃ, vīryendriyaṃ, smṛtīndriyaṃ, samādhīndriyaṃ prajñendriyam | sa pūrṇāṃ pañcānāṃ lokottarāṇāmindriyāṇāmabhimātratvāt tīkṣṇatvācca śīghravāhitvācca kṣipravedhānantvarthaṃ samādhiṃ spṛśati yadutāsravāṇāṃ kṣayāya |[32]
iyamucyate sukhā pratipat viśuddhimārgā |
imāṃ bhikṣavaḥ catasraḥ pratipadaḥ |
(12) catasraḥ samādhibhāvanāḥ
tatra bhikṣavaḥ katamāścatasraḥ samādhibhāvanāḥ ?
(1) asti bhikṣavaḥ samādhibhāvanā āsevitā bhāvitā bahulīkṛtā kāmarāgaprahāṇāyaṃ saṃvartate |
(2) asti bhikṣavaḥ samādhibhāvanā āsevitā bhāvitā bahulīkṛtā dṛṣṭadharmasukhavihārāya saṃvartate (3) asti bhikṣavaḥ samādhibhāvanā āsevitā bhāvitā bahulīkṛtā jñānadarśanapratilambhāya saṃvartate | (4) asti bhikṣavaḥ samādhibhāvanā āsevitā bhāvitā bahulīkṛtā prajñāpratilambhāya saṃvartate |[23]
tatra bhikṣavaḥ katamā samādhibhāvanā āsevitā bhāvitā bahulīkṛtā kāmarāgaprahāṇāya saṃvartate ?
iha bhikkho bhikṣurupraśāṃto vā jaghanyakālagato vā, dṛṣṭvāgāragato vā, imaṃ meva kāyamuddhvaṃ yāvaccādatalaśikhaḥ keśamastakān tvakuparyaṃtaṃ yathāvisthitaṃ yathāpraṇihitaṃ pūrṇaṃ nānāprakārasyāśucyarthaṃthāmūtaṃ samantakaṃ pratyavekṣate |
santi asmin kāye keśā, romāṇi, nakhā, dantā, tvaco, māṃse, snāyū, asthi,[24]
snādhu, śirā, mudgakā, hareṇuḥ, prahohā, kodravakaḥ, annāṇi, annaguṇā, dhānyāvarga
ṣakādayaḥ, uddardaṃ, yaṣkūt, purīṣam, athu, snehaḥ; lehaḥ; siṃhāṇako, vasa
chardikā, majjā, medaḥ, pittaṃ, śleṣmā, pūyaṃ, śoṇitaṃ mastakaṃ, mastakaluṅgamiti|
nānāprakārasyādyupaveṣṭuyutaṃ madhyavakro | tadyathā dvidhana uṣayato dvāraviniḥsṛta
koṣṭhagāraṃ paripūrṇaṃ nānāprakārasya sattvajātasya dhānyavikalpasyaṣaḍdurāvasanāṇām[25]
taccetuṣpādān puṣkaṣo mayakṣetrayam jātīyādisati sūkṣmajñānāni, hmāni ṭaṅka-dhānyāni |
evameva bhikṣavo bhikṣurimeva kāryaṃ yathā''stīrṇaṃ yathāpraṇihitaṃ yāvat pratyavekṣate |
imaṃ bhikṣavaḥ samādhibhāvanā āsevitā bhāvitā bahulīkṛtā kāmarāgaprahāṇāya saṃvartate |
(2) tarva bhikṣavaḥ katamā samādhibhāvanā āsevitā bhāvitā bahulīkṛtā dṛṣṭadharmasukhavihārāya saṃvartate ?
iha bhikṣavo bhi(30)kṣurupagato vā vṛkṣamūlagato vā śūnyāgāragato vā imameva kāyamadhyātmaṃ vivekajena samādhijena prītisukhenābhiṣyandati pariṣyandati parisevayati parisphurati |
tasya nāsti sarvaśaḥ kāyādaṅguṣṭaṃ bhavatyasaṃspṛṣṭamīva yaddūta adhyātmavivekajena samādhijena prītisukhena |
tathāpi nāma bhikṣavaḥ utpalāni vā padmāni vā kumudāni vā puṇḍarīkāṇi vā udake jātāni, udake vṛddhāni, udake samāni, sarvāṇi tāni śītalena vāriṇā abhiṣyanditāni pariṣyanditāni, paripūritāni pariprīṇitāni parisphuritāni; evameva bhikṣavo bhikṣurupagato vā vṛkṣamūlagato vā śūnyāgāragato[26]
vā imenaiva kāymadhyāsaṃ vivekena tamādhinā prītisukhena śamiṣyaddanti paripūrayanti pariśodhayanti parisphuranti |
tasya nāsti sarvataḥ kāyāvaraṇaṃ bhavasyaskuraṇā|
śaktu adhyāsaṃ vivekena prītisukhena |
iyaṃ samādhibhāvanā āsevitā bhāvitā bahulīkṛtā dṛṣṭadharmasukhavihārā saṃvartate |
(2) tatra bhikṣavaḥ katamā samādhibhāvanā āsevitā bhāvitā bahulīkṛtā jñānadarśanapratilambhāya saṃvartate ?
iha bhikṣavo bhikṣuṇā ālokasaṃjñā sādhu ca suṣṭhu ca udgṛhītā bhavati sumanasikṛtā suṣṭhudhā supratividdhā divāsasaṃjñāvihatā samapramāṇaṃ cittaṃ bhāvayati yathā divā tathā rātrau, yathā rātrau tathā divā, yathā pūrvaṃ tathā paścāt,[27]
yathā paścāt tathā puraḥ, yathā'ste'dra tathaivo'dya, yathaivo'dya tathā'grataḥ |
iti vidvattena cetasā'pyarvācīnena divasaṃjñābhihitaṃ samaprabhāsaṃ cittaṃ bhāvayati sarvakartṛmis ho'kṛtaṃ |
tatrāpi nāma nirbhaye bhīṣaṇānāṃ pṛṣṭhame māse traye hine vigatabahiraṅke namati madhyāhnakālasamaye yāvadālokapariṣuddho bhavati pūrvavṛttaḥ prabhāsvaraḥ, na cāsyāpyakāravikalpaṃ bhavati; evameva nirbhaye nirguṇā ālokasaṃjñā sādhu ca sukhadu ca sugṛhītā bhavati, sumanaskṛtā, sudṛṣṭā, supratividdhā divasa-saṃjñābhihitaṃ samaprabhāsaṃ cittaṃ bhāvayati, yathā divā tathā rātrau, yathā rātrau tathā divā, yathā pūrvaṃ tathā paścāt yathā paścāt tathā puraḥ, yathā'dya tathaivo'dya, yathaivo'dya tathā'grataḥ |
iti vidvattena cetasā'pyarvācīnena divasasaṃjñābhihitena samaprabhāsaṃ cittaṃ bhāvayati |
iyaṃ samādhibhāvanā āsevitā bhāvitā bahulīkṛtā jñānadarśanapratiṣṭhāyāḥ saṃvartate |[28]
(4) tatra śiṣyaḥ: katamā samādhisambhavanā ādauvitā bhāvitā bahulīkṛtā prajñāpratiṣṭhābhāya saṃvartate ?
iha śikṣako śikṣurupagato [vā] śṛkṣasukhyāto vā śṛtyāgāragato vā khullaka ca prahrāṇāt duḥkhecya ca prahrāṇāt pūrvameva saumanasyadaurmanasyayorastaṅgamād duḥkhāsukhamupekṣā(91)smṛtipariśuddhaṃ caturthaṃ dhyānamupasaṃpadya viharati |
iyaṃ samādhisambhavanā ādauvitā bhāvitā bahulīkṛtā prajñāpratiṣṭhābhāya saṃvartate ityabhyataḥ samādhisambhavanāḥ |
(13) catvāri smṛtyupasthānāni
tatra śiṣyaḥ: katamāni catvāri smṛtyupasthānāni ?
(1) iha bhikṣurvo bhikṣurupyātme kāye kāyānudarśī viharati, ātāpī saṃprajānan smṛtimān vinīya loke abhidhyādaurmanasye | bahirdhaṃ kāye kāyānudarśī viharati | adhyātmabahirdhyākāye kāyānudarśī viharati ātāpī saṃprajānan smṛtimān vinīya loke'bhidhyādaurmanasye |
(2) adhyātmavedanāsu, bahirvedanāsu, adhyātmabahirdhā vedanāsu vedanānudarśī viharati ātāpī saṃprajānan smṛtimān vinīya loke abhidhyādaurmanasye |
(3) adhyātmacitte, bahiścitte, adhyātmabahirdhācitte cittānudarśī viharatyātāpī saṃprajānan smṛtimān vinīya loke abhidhyādaurmanasye |
(4) adhyātmadharmeṣu bahirdharmeṣu, adhyātmabahirdhādharmeṣu dharmānudarśī viharatyātāpī saṃprajānan smṛtimān vinīya loke abhidhyādaurmanasye |
imāni śiṣyaścatvāri smṛtyupasthānāni |[25]
catvāri samyakprahāṇāni
(14) catvāri samyakprahāṇāni
tatra bhikṣavaḥ katamāni catvāri samyakprahāṇāni |
(1) iha bhikṣavo bhikṣuranutpannānāṃ pāpakānāmakuśalānāṃ dharmāṇāṃ mahāpāya chandaṃ janayati vyāyacchate vīryamārabhate cittaṃ pragṛhṇāti samyak praṇidadhāti |
(2) anutpannānāṃ ca pāpakānāmakuśalānāṃ dharmāṇāmanutpādāya chandaṃ janayati vyāyacchate vīryamārabhate cittaṃ pragṛhṇāti samyak praṇidadhāti |
(3) anutpannānāṃ kuśalānāṃ dharmāṇāmutpādāya chandaṃ janayati vyāyacchate vīryamārabhate cittaṃ pragṛhṇāti samyak praṇidadhāti |
(4) utpannānāṃ ca kuśalānāṃ dharmāṇāṃ sthitaye asammoṣāya abhyāsāya yaśo-bhāvāya vaipulyāya paripūrṇaṃ chandaṃ janayati vyāyacchate vīryamārabhate cittaṃ pragṛhṇāti samyak praṇidadhāti |
imāni bhikṣuścatvāri samyakprahāṇāni |[30]
(15) catvāra ṛddhipādāḥ
tatra bhikṣavaḥ katame catvāra ṛddhipādāḥ ?
(1) iha bhikṣavo bhikṣurachandasamādhipradhānasaṃskārasamannāgatamṛddhipādaṃ bhāvayati vivekaniśritaṃ virāganiśritaṃ nirodhaniśritaṃ vyavasargapariṇatam; ātmaśraddho nātiśraddho bhaviṣyati nātimūḍhītaḥ |
(2) vīryasamādhipradhānasaṃskārasamannāgatamṛddhipādaṃ bhāvayati vivekaniśritaṃ virāganiśritaṃ nirodhaniśritaṃ vyavasargapariṇatam; ātmaśraddho nātiśraddho bhaviṣyati nātimūḍhītam |
(3) cittasamādhipradhānasaṃskārasamannāgatamṛddhipādaṃ bhāvayati vivekaniśritaṃ virāganiśritaṃ nirodhaniśritaṃ vyavasargapariṇatam; ātmaśraddhaṃ nātiśraddhaṃ bhaviṣyati nātimūḍhītam |[31]
(4) mīmāṃsāmāniṣaddvāṇapariskārasamatrācaturddhedānāṃ bhāvayati vivekaniścite virāganiścite titīṣāniścite vyavasāyaniścaṇatvāt ; āsannīmīmāṃsā nā(10b)tilīnā bhaviṣyati nātimūḍhītā |
ime śikṣadvaścatvāra indriyātā: |
(16) pañcendriyāṇi
tatra śikṣadya: katamāni pañcendriyāṇi ?
yadyuta—(1) śraddhendriyam , (2) vīryendriyam , (3) smṛtīndriyam , (4) samādhīndriyam , (5) prajñendriyam |
(1) tatra katamat śraddhendriyam ?
yayā śraddhayā caturo dharmān śraddhadhāti | katamāṃścatura: ? saṃsārāvacarāṃ laukikīṃ samyagdṛṣṭiṃ śraddhadhāti | sa° karmavipākapratiśaraṇo bhavati | yadvṛttaṃ karma kariṣyāmi kuśalaṃ vā pāpakaṃ vā, tasyaiva karmaṇo vipākaṃ pratisaṃvedayiṣyāmi |
sa jīvitahetorapi pāpakaṃ karma nābhisaṃskaroti |[32]
idamucyate śraddhendriyam |
(2) tatra katamad vīryendriyam ?
yān dharmān śraddhendriyeṇa śraddhāsyati tān dharmān vīryendriyeṇa samudānayati |
idamucyate vīryendriyam |
(3) tatra katamat smṛtīndriyam ?
yān dharmān vīryendriyeṇa samudānayati tān dharmān smṛtīndriyeṇa vipuṇāśrayati |
idamucyate smṛtīndriyam |
(4) tatra katamat samādhīndriyam ?
yān dharmān smṛtīndriyeṇa na vipuṇāśrayati tān dharmān samādhīndriyeṇa ekāgrīkaroti |
idamucyate samādhīndriyam |
(5) tatra katamat prajñendriyam ?
yān dharmān samādhīndriyeṇa ekāgrīkaroti tān dharmān prajñendriyeṇa pratividhyate |[33]
sa teṣu ṣamaṃgu pratyavekṣaṇajātīyo bhavati |
idamucyate prajñendriyam |
imāni śikṣakaḥ pañcendriyāṇi |
(17) pañca balāni
tatra śikṣakaḥ kṛ (11a) tamāni pañca balāni ?
yaduta—(1) śraddhābalam, (2) vīryabalam, (3) smṛtibalam, (4) samādhibalam, (5) prajñābalam |
imāni śikṣakaḥ pañcabalāni |
(18) sapta bodhyaṅgāni
tatra śikṣakaḥ katamāni sapta bodhyaṅgāni ?
tadyathā—(1) smṛtisaṃbodhyaṅgam , (2) dharmapravicayasaṃbodhyaṅgam , (3) vīryasaṃbodhyaṅgam , (4) prītisaṃbodhyaṅgam , (5) praśrabdhisaṃbodhyaṅgam , (6) samādhisaṃbodhyaṅgam , (7) upekṣāsaṃbodhyaṅgam |[34]
(1) iha śikṣādyo nidraḥ; smṛtisaṃbodhyaṅgaṃ bhāvayati vivekaniśritaṃ virāganiśritaṃ, nirodhaniśritaṃ, vyavasargapariṇatam |
(2) evaṃ dharmapravicayaṃ saṃbodhyaṅgaṃ bhāvayati vivekaniśritaṃ, virāganiśritaṃ, nirodhaniśritaṃ, vyavasargapariṇatam |
(3) vīryasaṃbodhyaṅgaṃ bhāvayati vivekaniśritaṃ, virāganiśritaṃ, nirodhaniśritaṃ, vyavasargapariṇatam |
(4) prītisaṃbodhyaṅgaṃ bhāvayati vivekaniśritaṃ, virāganiśritaṃ, nirodhaniśritaṃ, vyavasargapariṇatam |
(5) praśrabdhisaṃbodhyaṅgaṃ bhāvayati vivekaniśritaṃ, virāganiśritaṃ, nirodhaniśritaṃ vyavasargapariṇatam |
(6) samādhisaṃbodhyaṅgaṃ bhāvayati vivekaniśritaṃ, virāganiśritaṃ, nirodhaniśritaṃ, vyavasargapariṇatam |
(7) upekṣāsaṃbodhyaṅgaṃ bhāvayati vivekaniśritaṃ, virāganiśritaṃ, nirodhaniśritaṃ, vyavasargapariṇatam |
ityāni bhikṣavaḥ sapta bodhyaṅgāni |
(19) āyogākṣo mārgaḥ
tatra bhikṣavaḥ katama āyogākṣo mārgaḥ ?[35]
āryagaiṅko mārgaḥ
tadyathā—(1) samyagdṛṣṭiḥ, (2) samyaksaṃkalpaḥ, (3) samyagvākku,
(4) samyakkarmāntaḥ, (5) samyagājīvaḥ, (6) samyagvyāyāmo (11b) taḥ,
(7) samyaksmṛtiḥ, (8) samyaksamādhiśca |
(1) tatra śikṣavaḥ samyagdṛṣṭiḥ katamā ?
astyaya lokaḥ | asti paralokaḥ | asti mātā | asti pitā |
asti dattam | asti hutam | asti iṣṭāniṣṭakuśalākuśalānāṃ karmaṇāṃ
phalavipākaḥ | tasti loke samyagjñātāḥ, samyakpratipannāśca iti |
iyaṃ śikṣavaḥ samyagdṛṣṭiḥ | tadviparītā mithyādṛṣṭiḥ |
(2) samyaksaṃkalpaḥ katamaḥ ?
buddhakarmādipariṇāmitaṃ śīlaṃ dānaṃ ca, naṃ cakṣurmaṃsādipariṇāmitam |
ayaṃ śikṣavaḥ samyaksaṃkalpaḥ |
(3) samyagvākku katamā ?
iha śikṣavaḥ pāruṣyādanṛtapaiśunyasambhinnapralāpaviratā vākku |
iyamucyate śikṣavaḥ samyagvākku |[36]
(4) saptakramanti: katama: ?
kāyavāṅmanasāṃ daśakaśaleṣu karmasaveṣu vyāpāra: |
tatra kāyikaṃ trividham—prāṇātipātādattādānakāmamithyācāranivṛtti: |
vācikaṃ caturvidham—anṛtavacanapaiśunyapāruṣyasaṃbhinnapralāpād virati: |
mānasaṃ trividham—anabhidhyāvyāpādamithyādṛṣṭibhyo virati: |
(ka) tatra prāṇātipāta: katama: ?
prāṇī ca bhavati, prāṇisaṃjñī ca bhavati, vadhakacittaṃ cotpādayati | upa
kramaṃ ca karoti | jīvitādapi vyaparopayati |
ayamucyate niścaya: prāṇātipāta: | anyatra pramādādahaṅkārad abhimānaviṣaya: |
(kha) tatrādattādānaṃ katamat ?
steyacittasya paradravyasvīkaraṇamadattādānam | anyatra mātṛpitṛśrāddhubana
mitrajñātyādi svahastādanuproṣiṇo grahaṇaṃ nādattādānam |
(ga) tatra kāmamithyācāra: katama: ?
parastrīparapuruṣagamanaṃ sarve rājamārgapratiṣiddhātānagamyā asthānāgamanādeśā[37]
athamucyate kāmaniṣṭhācāraḥ| | kārttikamāsevaṃ trividham|
(ka) anṛtaṃ katamam‌?
śāśvapraśne yathābhūtānaṃ vitathavacanamanṛtam‌| anṛheto vā arhannasmīti parihāsavarjam‌| ityamucyate anṛtam‌|
(kha) paiśunyaṃ katamat‌?
abhūtena satyena vā sedakaraṃ vacanaṃ paiśunyaṃ tat‌|
ityamucyate paiśunyam‌|
(ga) 'vak‌ pāruṣyaṃ katamat‌?
paraduḥkhābhisaṃyogenāvapriyavacanaśrāvaṇaṃ pāruṣyam‌| duḥkhasukhaṃ bhāvatikaścaṇayā
buddhyā yadapriyavacanaśrāvaṇaṃ kriyate tadapāruṣyam‌|[38]
idamucyate pāribhāṣyam |
(gha) sammiśrakathāḥ katamaḥ ?
tadyathā—rājakathā, corakathā, yuddhakathā, madyakathā, śrṛtakathā, strīka-
thāsyāmiṣakathā kathayaṃ vā |
ayamucyate sammiśrapralāpaḥ | nāryaṃsevetabahuvidham |
(ka) vajārniśrayā katamā ?
paradravyājikaraṇocchā bhikṣyā | 'dhānyasya dravyāṇi tāni mama syu'riti
iyamucyate'bhidhyā |
(kha) tatra vyāpādaḥ katamaḥ ?
parajīvitasamapariṇāmāvacchedanīḍāhivikṣepanam |
ayamucyate vyāpādaḥ |
(ga) tatra mithyādṛṣṭiḥ katamā ?
nāstyayaṃ lokaḥ; nāsti paraloka iti pūrvavat | "etanmātaṃ trividham"
(5) tatra sampragājoḥ katamaḥ ?[39]
siddhautsavādi kuhenā chaṇanā naimittikaṃ saṃ naiṣṭhikikaṃca chābhena lābhapratikāṅkṣā ca|
(ka) tatra kuhenā katamā?
siddhutsavādi dṛṣṭvā paryaṅke navadvūṣā pāthi sūcyamāne cā nivedayati—"dhyāyī siddhuraṃsaṃgamahi"ti lābhasatkāre me bhaviṣyaṃti| evamādikā kuhenocyate|
(kha) tatra chaṇanā katamā?
iha siddhulaubhasatkāranimittameva "tvaṃ me mātā, tvaṃ me pitā, tvaṃ me bhaginī, tvaṃ me duhiteti evamanyānyapi priyavacanāni vadīti| evamādikā chaṇane-tyucyate|
(ga) tatra naimittikaṃsaṃ katamat?
siddhutsavādi piṇḍapātaṃ pariśuddhya akṣudraṃ prate—"yādṛśo'yaṃ piṇḍa-pātanādṛśo'styeṣāmakāmṛddheṣu na labhyate"| alpasatkāranijasya tu vadato'saṃbhaḥ|
ityucyate naimittikaṃsam|
(gha) tatra naiṣṭhikikaṃsaṃ katamat?[40]
bhikṣustāvat yatra gṛhe piṇḍapātaṃ na labhate, dāyavibhaktabhasma bhavati, tatra brūte-
"adānapakkho 'hi nirayaṃ gacchanti | yuyamapyadānapakkho bhavatha nirayagāmino"
iti |
nairarkṣamyaprītāḥ piṇḍapātamanuprayacchanti |
taṃ ca labdhvā paribhuñjate |
ityamucyate naiṣādikavṛttam |
(ḍa) tatra lābhena lābhamatikrāṃkṣā katamā ?
bhikṣustāvadārāmīyena ghanenaṃ śobhanāni cīvarāṇi caulakīya upāsakebhyo
darśayati—'īdṛśāni vayaṃ vastrāṇi labhāmahe' iti |
te lajjitā yāni vastrāṇi prayacchanti tāni paribhuñjate |
ityamucyate bhikṣorvo lābhena lābhamatikrāṃkṣā |
ayaṃ bhikṣurvo mithyājīvaḥ |
"dharmādūratidūratiḥ samyagājīvaḥ" |
(ca) iha khalu (13a) bhikṣavaḥ (Sic) upāsakasya mithyājīve |
viṣavikrayaḥ, śastravikrayaḥ, sattvavikrayaḥ, madyavikrayaḥ, māṃsavikrayaḥ, agradaveśita-
tilasarṣapavikrayaṃ mithyājīvaḥ |
adhyārddhaśatiḥ |[41]
athamucyate bhiṣuvaḥ sampragalbīḥ |
(6) tatra sampragalvyāyāḥ katamaḥ ?
iha bhiṣuvo'bhicāranaivedyanānusthānāṅgalisāmīcīkarma karaṇīyam |
athamucyate bhiṣuvaḥ sampragalvyāyāḥ |
(7) tatra bhiṣuvaḥ sampragalputiḥ katamā ?
iha khalu bhiṣuvo bhiṣuṃ striyaṃ dṛṣṭvā utsave roge bāhyābhyantarikayoḥ
śarīrayoranubhākaroti yathābhūtadarśī bhavati—tatsyabhittūnū kārye keśā,
romāṇi, nakhā, dantā, rajo, malaṃ, tvaṅ, māṃsam, asthi, snāyuḥ, śirā, vṛkkā,
hṛdayaṃ, phlīhā, yakṛnmakkā, āmāśayaḥ, pakvāśayaḥ, antrāṇi, antraguṇā,
jīvarīyakaṃ, yakṛt, purīṣam, śaṅkhaḥ, svedaḥ kheṭaḥ, siṃhāṇako, vasā, laśikā,
majjā, medaḥ, pittaṃ, śleṣmā, pūyaṃ, śoṇitaṃ, mastuluṅgaṃ, mastakaśchurdaṃ, uccāraprasrāve,
pūrṇaṃ nānāprakārasyāśuceriti |[52]
ityupacyate samyaksmṛtiḥ |
(8) samyaksamādhiḥ katamaḥ ?
catvāri dhyānāni | iha bhikṣavo bhikṣurviviktaṃ kāmairviviktaṃ pāpakaira-
kuśalairdharmaiḥ savitarkaṃ savicāraṃ vivekajaṃ prītisukhaṃ prathamaṃ dhyānamupasaṃpadya viharati |
sa vitarkavicārāṇāṃ vyupaśamādadhyātmasaṃprasādāccetasa ekotībhāvādavitarkamavicāraṃ
samā(13b)dhijaṃ prītisukhaṃ dvitīyaṃ dhyānamupasaṃpadya viharati | saṃ prītervirāgā-
dupekṣako viharati smṛtaḥ saṃprajānan sukhaṃ ca kāyena pratisaṃvedayati | yattadāryā
ācakṣate "upekṣakaḥ smṛtimān sukhavihārīti" tṛtīyaṃ dhyānamupasaṃpadya viharati |
sa sukhasya ca prahāṇād duḥkhasya ca prahāṇāt pūrvameva ca saumanasyadaurmanasyayorastaṃ-
gamādduḥkhāsukhamupekṣāsmṛtipariśuddhaṃ caturthaṃ dhyānamupasaṃpadya viharati | imāni khalu
bhikṣavaścatvāri dhyānāni |
ayamucyate samyaksamādhiḥ |
ayaṃ bhikṣava āryāṣṭāṅgo mārgaḥ |
(20) ṣoḍaśākārā ānāpānasmṛtiḥ
tatra bhikṣavaḥ katamā ṣoḍaśākārā ānāpānasmṛtiḥ ?[43]
iha bhikṣavo bhikkhū| spṛśaṃ śrāśravan spṛśa āśravāmīti yathābhūtaṃ prajānāti|
spṛśa: vā praśravan spṛśa praśravāmīti yathābhūtaṃ prajānāti|
(1) dīrghamāśravan dīrghamāśravāmīti yathābhūtaṃ prajānāti| dīrghaṃ vā praśravan dīrghaṃ praśravāmīti yathābhūtaṃ prajānāti|
(2) hrasvamāśravan hrasvamāśravāmīti yathābhūtaṃ prajānāti| hrasvaṃ vā praśravan hrasvaṃ praśravāmīti yathābhūtaṃ prajānāti|
(3) sarvakāyasaṃskārapratisaṃvedī āśravan sarvakāyasaṃskārapratisaṃvedī āśravāmīti yathābhūtaṃ prajānāti| sarvakāyasaṃskārapratisaṃvedī praśravan sarvakāyasaṃskārapratisaṃvedī praśravāmīti yathābhūtaṃ prajānāti|
(4) prītipratisaṃvedī āśravan prītipratisaṃvedī āśravāmīti
[14a]
yathābhūtaṃ prajānāti| prītipratisaṃvedī praśravan prītipratisaṃvedī praśravāmīti yathābhūtaṃ prajānāti|[44]
(5) sukhapratisaṃvedī vā āsravan sukhapratisaṃvedī āsravāmīti yathāsūtraṃ prajānāti|
sukhapratisaṃvedī praśvasan sukhapratisaṃvedī praśvasāmīti yathāsūtraṃ prajānāti|
(6) pratipraśrambhayan kāyasaṃskārānāśvasan pratipraśrambhayan kāyasaṃskārānāśvasāmīti yathāsūtraṃ prajānāti|
pratipraśrambhayan kāyasaṃskārān praśvasan pratipraśrambhayan kāyasaṃskārān praśvasāmīti yathāsūtraṃ prajānāti|
(7) cittasaṃskārapratisaṃvedī āśvasan cittasaṃskārapratisaṃvedī āśvasāmīti yathāsūtraṃ prajānāti|
cittasaṃskārapratisaṃvedī praśvasan cittasaṃskārapratisaṃvedī praśvasāmīti yathāsūtraṃ prajānāti|
(8) pratipraśrambhayan vā cittasaṃskārānāśvasan pratipraśrambhayan vā cittasaṃskārānāśvasāmīti yathāsūtraṃ prajānāti|
pratipraśrambhayan vā cittasaṃskārān praśvasan pratipraśrambhayan vā cittasaṃskārān praśvasāmīti yathāsūtraṃ prajānāti|
(9) cittapratisaṃvedī āśvasan cittapratisaṃvedī āśvasāmīti yathāsūtraṃ prajānāti|
cittapratisaṃvedī praśvasan cittapratisaṃvedī praśvasāmīti yathāsūtraṃ prajānāti|
(10) abhipramodayan me cittamāśvasan abhipramodayan me cittamāśvasāmīti yathāsūtraṃ prajānāti|
abhipramodayanne cittaṃ praśvasan abhipramodayanne cittaṃ praśvasāmīti yathāsūtraṃ prajānāti|
(11) samādadhan cittamāśvasan samādadhan cittamāśvasāmīti yathāsūtraṃ prajānāti|
samādadhan cittaṃ praśvasan samādadhan cittaṃ praśvasāmīti yathāsūtraṃ prajānāti|
(12) vimocayan me cittamāśvasan vimocyanne cittamāśvasāmīti yathāsūtraṃ prajānāti|
vimocyanne cittaṃ praśvasan vimocyanne cittaṃ praśvasāmīti yathāsūtraṃ prajānāti|[44]
catvāri srotoapyaṣṭāṅgāni
(13) pañcaniṣṭhānudarśanāṃ, (14) virāgānudarśanāṃ, (15) nirodhānudarśanāṃ,
(16) pratiniḥsargānudarśanāṃ āhuranantaṃ pratiniḥsargānudarśanāṃ āhuratsāmīti yathābhūtaṃ prajānāti |
pratiniḥsargānidarśanāṃ vā pracchannaṃ pratiniḥsargānudarśanīṃ pracchannasāmīti yathābhūtaṃ prajānāti |
iyaṃ bhikṣavaḥ poṭṭhapākāśa ānāpānasmṛtiḥ |
(21) catvāri srotoapyaṣṭāṅgāni
tatra katamāni [bhikṣavaś]catvāri srotoapyaṣṭāṅgāni ?
(1) iha bhikṣave āryaśrāvako buddhe'vaiśaradyādine saṃpratyāyato bhavati—
ityapi sa bhagavāṃstathāgato'rhan samyaksaṃbuddho vidyācaraṇasaṃpannaḥ sugato lokavidanuttaraḥ puruṣadamyasārathiḥ śāstā devānāṃ ca manuṣyāṇāṃ ca buddho bhagavāniti |
(2) dharme'vaiśya prasādena saṃpratyāyato bhavati—svākhyāto bhagavatā dharmaḥ sāṃdṛṣṭiko nijjaro ākāliko opanāyiko aihipaśyikaḥ pratyātmavedyo[46]
vidveḥ yaduta—madanirmardanaḥ, pipāsāprativinayaḥ, śālayomumūrcchātaḥ, bhamopacchedaḥ, dūmaniryapacchamaḥ, tṛṣṇāghātaḥ, virāgaḥ, nirodhaḥ, nirvāṇam |
(2) saha utterapramādaina samprajāno bhavati | supratistro bhagavatuḥ śrāvakaḥ sahaḥ, myāsampratipadaḥ, myujuṭipratipadaḥ, sāmīcīpratipadaḥ, dharmānudharmapratipadaḥ |[47]
śrutarbhacārī | santi ṣaḍbhe 'śrotraśāptasahasrākṣākriyāyai prativacanakā:, santi ṣaḍbhe 'śrotraśāptāḥ ; santi ṣaḍbhe sahasragāmiphalasākṣākriyāyai prativacanakā:,
santi ṣaḍbhe sahasragāminaḥ ; santi ṣaḍbhe anāgāmiphalasākṣākriyāyai prativacanakā:,
santi ṣaḍbhe anāgāminaḥ ; santi ṣaḍbhe arhatphalasākṣākriyāyai prativacanakā:,
santi ṣaḍbhe arhantaḥ ; yadyūta—catvāri puṣpamudgāni, aṣṭo puṣpapudgalāḥ |
eva bhagavataḥ śrāvakasaṃghaḥ; śrotrasampanna:, samādhisampanna:, śraddhāsampanna:, śrutasampanna:,
vimuktisampanna:, vimuktijñānadarśanasampanna:, āhavanīya:, prāhavanīya:, añjalikaraṇīya:,
sādhīcīkaraṇīya:, anuttaraṃ puṇyakṣetraṃ dakṣiṇīyaṃ lokasya |
(4) āyaikānte: dārṣṭe: samprajñāto bhavati | yāni ca tāni dārṣāni
śrakṣaṇḍāni, śrancchadrāṇi, aśraśaṃśani, akalpyāni, atulyāni,
aprarūr̤hāni, kumārāni, kusārakṣāni, vijñapraśastāni, thagāhitāni ca
nirdeśiti |
imāni śrikṣatrayasthāni lokrottarapañcaskāni |[48]
(22) daśa tathāgatabalāni
tatra katamāni bhikṣavo daśa tathāgatabalāni ?
(1) iha bhikṣavastathāgataḥ sthānaṃ ca sthānato yathābhūtaṃ prajānāti, asthānaṃ cāsthānataḥ|
(2) atītānāgatapratyutpannānāṃ karmasamādānānāṃ vipākaṃ yathābhūtaṃ prajānāti|
(3) sarvatragāminaṃ pratipadaṃsopaśamamanekādhimuktikānāṃ nānābhi-muktikānāṃ yathābhūtaṃ prajānāti|
(4) anekadhātukaṃ lokaṃ nānādhātukaṃ lokaṃ yathābhūtaṃ prajānāti|
(5) sarvasattvānāmindriyaparāparajñatāṃ yathābhūtaṃ prajānāti|
(6) sarvetrāgrāminīṃ pratipadaṃ ca yathābhūtaṃ prajānāti|
(7) sarvasattvānāmindriya-[69]
bal̤amoṣṭhaprakṛṣṭānāmimo'vamābhisamāpattīnāṃ saṅkṣepavyavedanāthamaśrayāne yathāsūtraṃ prajñāpati |
(8) punaraparaṃ sākāraṃ soddeśaṃ sanimittamanekavidhaṃ punarvibhāvaṃ samanu-smarati, pūrvakāmapi cātti samanusmarati he titvabhavato vā yāvadanekāpyadhi cātikoṭi-nirvutasahasrāṇi anusmaratīti vistaraḥ |
(9) punaraparaṃ sa dvikena cakṣuṣā viśuddhenātikrāntamānuṣyakeṇa sattvān paśyati cyavamānānupapadyamānān, kāyavāṅ-manasā sucaritaduścaritaiḥ sugatidurgatiṣupapadyamān (16 a)jānāti vistaraḥ |
(10) āvarjanāprātyāhāraṃ cetovivṛttiṃ prajñayā yathāsūtraṃ prajñāpati |
imāni bhikṣavo daśa tathāgatabalāni |
(23) catvāri vaiśāradyāni
tatra katamāni bhikṣavastathāgatasya catvāri vaiśāradyāni |
(1) iha bhagavāṃstathāgato'rhan samyaksaṃbuddhaḥ ityātmānaṃ pratijānīte |
"ime sadā dharmā nābhisaṃbuddhā" ityatrāsya kaścid vadamāno vedet, sadevake[50]
loke samārūḍhe sanābhake sapramaṇātrāṇikāyāṃ prajñāyāṃ sarvebhamānucārāyāṃ nimitteneva samanudraṣṭāmi |
nimittamasamanudraṣṭeścāpyātaḥ kṣemamāto viharati apramādaḥ |
ādarśaṃ sthānaṃ prajānāti |
samyaksamuddeśataḥ siṃhanādaṃ nadati trāhmaṇaṃ pracodayati, apracoditaṃ apraṇītaṃ trāhmaṇena vā, kenacidvā punaroṣitaṃ sahadharmeṇoti |
(2) ye vā punarbhordaṃtaraśikā dharmā ākhyātāḥ tānu pratisevamānasya nāśmanaraśā yestatrārthe kṛṣṭibhūti vistaraḥ |
(3) yā vā punaryogā ākrānāṃ pratipadyatyāta āryāṃ nairyāṇikīṃ, tatpratisevamānasya na niryāṇāt tatkarasya samyagduḥkhakṣayāya hatyatrārthaṃ kṛṣṭibhūti pūrvavat |[51]
"śrīṇāmasya tataḥ, ātmānaṃ pratijānāsaḥ, 'ime te' ātmāna na parikṣīṇāḥ' ityavārthaṃ kathitadvamāno vadet, sarvevaike loke' samārūke samanuke saptamaprābrāṇikāthe prajāyāṃ sadaivānugṛhṇātu rāyāṃ nimittametavaṃ samnupasyati|"
nimittamanugamanupaśyanta yathāgataḥ kṣemaprāpto viharati akṣamaprāptaḥ|
ādimeṃ sthāne prajānāti, sāthamanuṃdragataḥ siṃhanādaṃ nadati, nāhaṃ cakraṃ pravartayati, apravartitaṃ śramaṇairna brāhmaṇena vā, kenacidvā punaraloke sahadharmeṇeti|
imāni catvāri vaiśāradyāni|
(24) caturaḥ pratisaṃvidaḥ
tatra katamāstathāgatasya catasraḥ pratisaṃvidaḥ?[52]
tadyathā* :— (1) arthapratisaṃvid, (2) dharmapratisaṃvid, (3) niruktipratisaṃvid, (4) pratibhānapratisaṃvid |
(1) arthapratisaṃvid katamā ?
yadutta-"paramārthaṃ yadavaivartyajñānam" |
(2) dharmapratisaṃvid katamā ?
anājñeyaṃ dharmaṃ yadavaivartyajñānam |
(3) niruktipratisaṃvid katamā ?
abhivyāhāre yadavaivartyajñānam |
(4) pratibhānapratisaṃvid katamā ?
yuktamuktamabhivyāpitāyāṃ samādhivaśe saṃpṛcchaneṣu yadavaivartyajñānam |
ityākṣarataḥ pratisaṃvidaḥ |
(25) athādaśa āveṇikā buddhadharmāḥ
tatra katame'ṣṭādaśāveṇikā buddhadharmāḥ ?[53]
yadyūte—(1) nāsti tathāgatasya skhalitam, (2) nāsti ravitam, (3) nāsti muṣitasmṛtitā, (4) nāstyasamāhitaṃ cittam, (5) nāsti nānātvasaṃjñā, (6) nāstyapratisaṃkhyāyopekṣā, (7) nāsti chandasya hāniḥ, (8) nāsti vīryasya hāniḥ,
(9) nāsti smṛterhāniḥ, (10) nāsti samādherhāniḥ, (11) nāsti prajñāyā hāniḥ
(12) nāsti vimukterhāniḥ, (13) atīte'dhvani apratihataṃ jñānadarśanam,
(14) anāgate'dhvani apratihataṃ jñānadarśanam, (15) pratyutpanne'dhvani apratihataṃ jñānadarśanam, (16) sarvakāyakarma jñānapūrvaṅgamaṃ jñānānuparivartaṃ,
(17) sarvavākkarma jñānapūrvaṅgamaṃ jñānānuparivartam| (18) sarvamanaḥkarma jñānapūrvaṅgamaṃ jñānānuparivartam|
ime aṣṭādaśāveṇikā buddhadharmāḥ|
(29) dvātriṃśanmahāpuruṣalakṣaṇāni
tatra katamāni tathāgatasya dvātriṃśanmahāpuruṣalakṣaṇāni?[48]
yadvat—(1) ghṛtanirdigdhagātratā, (2) abhyaktātyādaratyano'thakāntimatpādatvam|
(3) śyāvarṇaśmaśrusankṣapādatā, (4) dīrghabāhutvatā, (5) cāruhastapādatā, (6) mṛdutaraṇahastapādatā, (7) sutotsaṅgadarīratā, (8) pṛthubāhutā, (9) kośagarbhaviśuddhagātratā, (10) siṃhapūrvārdhakāyatā, (11) nimnāharīratā, (12) samacaturasraskandhatā|
(13) dhanaghanasusaṃhatagātratā, (14) viśuddhamājanatā, (15) kambugrīvatā, (16) siṃhahanutā, (17) samacatvāriṃśaddantatā, (18) samāvarakadantatā, (19) ghanadantatā, (20) mabhūrlanirbhatā, (21) rasasāmatā, (22) brahmasvarakatā|
nikṣvāṇatvatā, (23) abhinīlanetratā, (24) gopakṣmanetratā, (25) puṣkalagaṇḍatā|
chavitā, (26) śucirṇacchavitā, (27) vyaktakarmaprabhutatā, (28) añjanaprabhaviṣaṇatā|[55]
vatīyamatā, (29) indranīlakeśatā, (30) suguptamukhabāntarālebhyaṣṭatā,
(31) ujjvalaśiraskatā, (32) smeghoghanaparimaṇḍalatā |
imāni dvātriṃśanmahāpuruṣalakṣaṇāni |
(1) supratiṣṭhitapādatā—tathāgatasyaivaṃ mahāpuruṣasya mahāpuruṣala
kṣaṇaṃ pūrvaṃ dahasamādānatayā nirvṛttam |[56]
(2) athastātpadyattajo'thaprakṛtidvayapādanaktā—tathāgatasyaivaṃ mahāpuruṣasya mahāpuruṣalakṣaṇaṃ tatra pūrvaṃ vicitradattopacayena nirdṛṣṭam |
(3) āyatepāṣṇyurusmṛpādatā—tathāgatasyaivaṃ mahāpuruṣasya mahāpuruṣalakṣaṇaṃ pūrvaṃ parasattvābhijanakaraṇatayā nirdṛṣṭam |
(4) dīrghāṅgulitā—tathāgatasyaivaṃ mahāpuruṣasya mahāpuruṣalakṣaṇaṃ pūrvaṃ sattvānāṃ dharmedānāvaraṇaguptikaraṇatayā nirdṛṣṭam |
(5) jālahastapādatā—tathāgatasyaivaṃ mahāpuruṣasya mahāpuruṣalakṣaṇaṃ pūrvaṃ parayaṃvirodhitanatayā nirdṛṣṭam |
(6) mṛdutaruṇahastapādatā—tathāgatasyaivaṃ mahāpuruṣasya mahāpuruṣalakṣaṇaṃ pūrvaṃ vividhamānurāgānupradānatayā nirdṛṣṭam |
(7) saptotsadaśarīratā—tathāgatasyaivaṃ mahāpuruṣasya mahāpuruṣalakṣaṇaṃ pūrvaṃ[57]
dvātriṃśanmahāpuruṣalakṣaṇāni
viśuddhaniryānānumeyatānathā nirdṛṣṭam |
(8) eṇeyajaṅghatā—tathāgatasyaivaṃ mahāpuruṣasya mahāpuruṣalakṣaṇaṃ pūrvaṃ buddha-bhaveṣamṛhaṇatayā nirdṛṣṭam |
(9) kośagatavastiguhyatā—tathāgatasyaivaṃ mahāpuruṣasya mahāpuruṣalakṣaṇaṃ pūrvaṃ guhyamantrasaṃrakṣaṇatayā maithunadharmaviratinirdṛṣṭanethā ca nirdṛṣṭam |
(10) siṃhapūrvārdhakāyatā—tathāgatasyaivaṃ mahāpuruṣasya mahāpuruṣalakṣaṇaṃ pūrvaṃ śubhakarmānuvṛtticaraṇatayā nirdṛṣṭam |
(11) citāntarāṃsatā—tathāgatasyaivaṃ mahāpuruṣasya mahāpuruṣalakṣaṇaṃ pūrvaṃ duḥkhadharmoddhāracaraṇatayā nirdṛṣṭam |
(12) samavṛttaskandhatā—tathāgatasyaivaṃ mahāpuruṣasya mahāpuruṣalakṣaṇaṃ pūrvaṃ pareṣāmapyāśāsadānatayā nirdṛṣṭam |[56]
(13) ananamnataṃmūlamantravāhutā—tathāgatasyedaṃ mahāpuruṣasya mahāpuruṣalakṣaṇaṃ pūrve parakiṅkaraṇīgoṣṭhukakanyā nivedyam |
(14) trikūṭāgāmitā—tathāgatasyedaṃ mahāpuruṣasya mahāpuruṣalakṣaṇaṃ pūrve dakṣiṇakālakūmebhāgyāsamṛddhātmanā nivedyam |
(15) kūrmapṛṣṭhavatā—tathāgatasyedaṃ mahāpuruṣasya mahāpuruṣalakṣaṇaṃ pūrve rajñaiṣu tritridharmeṣu sthānapradānatayā nivedyam |
(16) siṃhahanutā—tathāgatasyedaṃ mahāpuruṣasya mahāpuruṣalakṣaṇaṃ pūrve kuśalamūlakṣepopapādapūrṇaṃ nivedyam |
(17) samacatvāriṃśaddantatā—tathāgatasyedaṃ mahāpuruṣasya mahāpuruṣalakṣaṇaṃ pūrve sarvasaṃsyāśayasamodhānena nivedyam |
(18) samāviral̤adantatā—tathāgatasyedaṃ mahāpuruṣasya mahāpuruṣalakṣaṇaṃ pūrve niravadyasaṃvarapratipattyā nivedyam |[59]
(19) sukumāradattā—tathāgatardhedaṃ mahāmudrasya mahāmudralakṣaṇaṃ pūrvaṃ svāraṃsikayampradhāntskandheṣu nirdeṣṭam |
(20) prabhūtinidratā—tathāgatardhedaṃ mahāmudrasya mahāmudralakṣaṇaṃ pūrvaṃ sadyavacanesaṃrakṣaṇatāyā nirdeṣṭam |
(21) rasarasāgratā—tathāgatardhedaṃ mahāmudrasya mahāmudralakṣaṇaṃ pūrvaṃ uṣamāṇupuṇyaskandhopetaviddho[mmatāyā parānupradattayā nirdeṣṭam |[60]
(22) brahmasvarekaliṅgahṛdahṛtsatratā—tathāgatasyedaṃ mahāpuruṣasya mahāpuruṣalakṣaṇaṃ pūrve simbhavacannatayā satyāyalvenatayā ānandavacanathāvaṇatayā nirdiṣṭam |
(23) abhinīlanetratā—tathāgatasyedaṃ mahāpuruṣasya mahāpuruṣalakṣaṇaṃ pūrvaṃ meghavat sarvadharatvaṇatayā nirdiṣṭam |
(24) gopakṣmanetratā—tathāgatasyedaṃ mahāpuruṣasya mahāpuruṣalakṣaṇaṃ pūrve'ṅganiveśayatayā nirdiṣṭam |
(25) sūkṣmachavitā—tathāgatasyedaṃ mahāpuruṣasya mahāpuruṣalakṣaṇaṃ pūrve dharmasaṃgīticittakarmodayayatayā nirdiṣṭam |
(26) suvarṇacchavitā—tathāgatasyedaṃ mahāpuruṣasya mahāpuruṣalakṣaṇaṃ pūrve śayyāsanavastrapradānatānupradānatayā nirdiṣṭam |
(27) ekaikaromakūpatā—tathāgatasyedaṃ mahāpuruṣasya mahāpuruṣalakṣaṇaṃ pūrve saṅgaṇikāparivarjanetayā nirdiṣṭam |
(28) ūrdhvāgrapradakṣiṇāvartaromatā—tathāgatasyedaṃ mahāpuruṣasya mahā[61]
puruṣalakṣaṇaṃ pūrvaṃ dhyānayogeṇdhyakkaraṇāminānukīrtanamadvidhamāhitathā nirdiṣṭam |
(29) indranīlaikegatā--tathāgatasyedaṃ mahāpuruṣasya mahāpuruṣalakṣaṇaṃ pūrvaṃ saṃyogaṇanukramatayā nirdiṣṭalakṣyapraśnasañcayā ca nirdiṣṭam |
(30) sukukummusāntaraugallalattā--tathāgatasyedaṃ mahāpuruṣasya mahāpuruṣalakṣaṇaṃ pūrvaṃ vaṇārhiṇā varṇabhāṣatayā nirdiṣṭam |
(31) uṣṇīṣaśiraskatā--tathāgatasyedaṃ mahāpuruṣasya mahāpuruṣalakṣaṇaṃ pūrvaṃ guragauravaṇātamayā nirdiṣṭam |
(32) nyagrodhaparimaṇḍalatā--tathāgatasyedaṃ mahāpuruṣasya mahāpuruṣalakṣaṇaṃ[62]
pūrvaṃ vyākhyaiḥ parasaṃscanāṃ ca samāse nirbhedanavayā nirddeśeṣu |
apramāṇe: kṛśālaṃmūleṣu ṣameṣu vaśravṛttiāt tathāgatasya dvātriṃśanmahāpuruṣa-
lakṣaṇāni kāye nirddeśāni |[63]
aśīti anuvyañjanāni
