imānyavaśītinuguṇastanāni |
yaduktaṃ bhagaṃvatā—"bhagne vo bhikṣavo deśayiṣyāmi, ādyo kalyāṇaṃ madhye kalyāṇaṃ paryavasāne kalyāṇaṃ, svarthaṃ suvyañjanaṃ, kevalaṃ paripūrṇaṃ pariśuddhaṃ paryavadā-te brahmacaryaṃ saṃprakāśayiṣyāmi" yaduta atharyaviniścayaṃ nāma dharmaparyāyamupa"—[67]
iti me yaduktamidaṃ tatte'nukamp|
"etāni vo bhikṣavo'raṇyāyatanāni vṛkṣamūlāni śūnyāgāraṇi parvatakandaragiriguhāpmaśānavanaprasthāni abhyavakāśaśmaśānāni vanaprasthānāni śathanīyāni adhyāvasata|
dhyāyata, bhikṣavo, mā pramādata|
mā paścādvipratisāriṇo bhaviṣyatha"|
idamanuśāsanam|
bhaviṣyantu khalu punastamevāye bhāgyamāṇe pañcāt vidyuścalānāmanuvādāyā-viśeṣyabhijñāni vighuṣkāni|
idamavocad bhagavān|
āttamanāste bhikṣavaḥ sā ca sarvāvatī parṣat,[68]
sadaivamānuṣāṅgarāgamarṣaṃśca dvokro bhagavato bhāvitamadhyamantravidhi|
arthocittiścayarṣaprapañcāya: samāpta:|
ye dharmā hetuprabhavā hetusteṣāṃ tathāgato hyavadat|
teṣāṃ ca yo nirodha evaṃvādī mahāśramaṇa:|
likhitamidaṃ saṃvat 319 caitra śukla 9|vīryaśrīdattakṛtam
karṇābhiniścayasūtranibandhanamI
oṃ namo buddhāya |
kandapūrvavadevalanaṃ praṇipatya sūjanāṃ bauddhottamaṃ gurusadāśivapādapadmam |
śuklā guro: sugatasūtrapadārthavijñān
sūtraṃ tadarthamanusammaraṇaṃ kariṣye ||
sūtraśarīram—nidānam, uddeśyavāta:, prayojanam, uddeśa:, nirdeśa:, anusambandha: |
tatra nidānam—evaṃ mayā śrutamityaya yāvadevaṃpratyayodeśanirmiṣṭamuttarīti |
uddeśyavāta:—evaṃśabdanirdeśāya prastāvabandhanaṃ | abhastāvaṃ śuśrūṣamāṇama-
nubhagaṃ syāt | tannibandhāya prastāvabandhanam | sa ca tatra bhagavānityārabhya
te bhikṣavo bhagavata: pratyaśrauṣu: | bhagavāṃstānidamavocaditi yāvat |[72]
prayojanaṃ pravakṣyārthaṃ|
stūri vyākhyānukramenaivakramenaiva vaktavyaṃ śrotṛ-rādareṇa śravaṇādviṣu pravartanārthaṃ|
aviditaprayojanā: prekṣāvanto na kañcit pravartante|
tathā ca āha—
śrutvā stutvā māhātmyaṃ śrotṛrādarakāritā|
śravaṇodyamahetuśca syādityādyau vācā prayojanam|| iti|
tatsyātharninirdhāraṇapravṛttasya dharmavinayādhigamadeśaḥ|
na hi vinā sūtropadeśena śiṣyaḥ śakto dharmaṃ pravivecum|[73]
tadanuktamo'pi naikṣakrapadaprayoge bhavatītyaveyam |
prayojanaṃ cāsyaiva sūtre nirdiṣṭaṃ yattūryeniniścaya iti |
athānāṃ viniścayo dharmāṇāṃ pravicaya ityarthaḥ |
dharmapravicayaḥ prayojanamityuktaṃ bhavati |
uddeśaḥ punaḥ śiṣyamukhavivarto'yaṃ kriyate |
avabuddharasamudāyārthaṃ bāhyavārthaṃ duṣṭena pratipadyate |
daṣṭabhūminiḥsaṃgaprakarṣaṇodvṛtti |
samāropāya vāhyapurīhārārthaṃ vā |
sa ca kṛtamātrasātisthārasya yadvṛtta pañcaskandhayā śabdavayaṃ bhikṣavo'śmaitaniniścaya itya dharmaparyāyaprayodeśa iti |
nirdeśo'pi saṃkṣepenāhṛtanirdeśanajanārthaṃ kriyate, vṛttipadmadurlabhāt |
vṛttiyuto hi nirdeśaḥ |
sūtraśruta uddeśaḥ |
tadyathāsattvoheśāsya piṇḍīkṛto'rthau vṛttiyatena nirdeśena viṣampramāṇaḥ sukhaṃ gamyate |[74]
evaṃ varhiṃ nirdeśa eva kartavyo noddeśa iti | ucyate |
nirdeśasyālpaṃ sakhāvacāraṇārthaṃ ca | yataḥ sāmānyavacanaṃ viśeṣavacanākāṅkṣam, ato nirdeśo'śakyavacanaṃ kartavyamiti; sāmānyasya viśeṣasmṛtihetutvāt |
dvividho doṣanirdeśo cedamucyate | dvividho doṣo yathākramamamativicāraḥ spṛtivikāra-doṣaśceti |
viparyasto'nugato'nugatāśeṣakathā vā nirdeśo'śakyavacanamiti | taspunaḥ svatra bhinnavaḥ kvacit pṛthakskandhā ityārabhya yāvadidamaghocuddhiḥ parisamāptiḥ |
anugatiḥ pūrveṇottarasya padasya saṃbandhapradarśanam |
uktaṃ saṃgrahaślokaḥ-
ādivākyamudāhṛtāt uddeśaḥ sayojanaḥ |
nirdeśaḥ sānugatiśca sarvatrārthasaṃgrahaḥ ||
iti saṃgrahaślokaḥ | idaṃ punasya śarīram |
sūtrapadānugatā vyākhyāyante | enamiṣyantaṃ tiṇīto yathāpraśnā-[75]
nāviduṃ bahumanyeṣu vartate tathāpīdārthapekṣāt prativacane boddhavyam |
katham ?
sakrotikāle pañcabhirahaṅkṛtaḥ sakṣāduddeśenāyamannado'pīṭho yathā tvayā śrutaṃ tathā
bhāṣaseti | tat āha evamiti |
yathā uṣmānirodhidravyeṣu kriyate ityarthaḥ |
mayotpadyamānacchede kurven sākṣācchuti darśayati |
mayaiva bhagavataḥ
sakāśācchutaṃ na tu śrāvapairyaparamparātisaṃdheḥ |
śrutamiti śrotrendriyāno-
dgṛhītatvam |
anena tasmin samaye'jimāyārthaṃ darśayati |
paścādapimā(2b)-
sādamane'pi vakṣyetārasādīnāṃ ududuṣpabhṛtimātraviśeṣanāmanivasathāt śrutamiti na
virudhyate |
ekasmin samaye ekasmin kāle; śravaṇakālasya prakaraṇenāvicchedāvekṣaḥ[76]
kālaḥ|
anena ca bāhuśravasāmanaḥ kathyati|
idaṃ tāvadekasmin samaye śrutam|
anyadapi anyadā śrutamiti saṃsargenāt|
ekasmin samaye bhagavān viharatītyupakrameṇa vā sambandhanīyam|
bhagavāniti|
māranuṣṭhupthaṃ devanuṣatreśaikṣupyamanuṣyakṣaṇaṃ bhagavāniti|[77]
kṛtvā trairettanaṃ nyāyena bhagavān |
aiśvaryādiguṇayogādya, yathoktam—
aiśvaryasya samagrasya rūpasya yaśasaḥ śriyaḥ |
jñānasyātha prayatnasya ṣaṇṇāṃ bhaga iti śrutiḥ || iti |
śrāvastyāmiti |
śravastasya nṛperaśapakrameṇa nirmitatvāt śrāvastīnagarī |
tadupalakṣito'pi deśo viśeṣaḥ |
śrāvastī |
viharatīti caṅkramaṇādilakṣaṇe-[76]
śruturirīyo'pi; muditātihṛṣṭapramodāṃ caturiramagaṇaḥ|
smeti sadākālaṃ darśayati|
āvassīnāro suvistīrṇo vijñāyate|
kutreyat āha—puvārirāye|
āyassāḥ puvārisam bhāge ārāmaḥ puvārirām; jetavanādvā pubbārāmaḥ|
ārambhenyasmini-vyārāmaḥ|
puvārirāmo'pi catuḥsthānānuyuktastatrāpi kutreyat āha—mugāramātu:
prāsāde|
mugāramātā nāmopāsikā haṭṭagharā, taṅkārite prāsāde|
prasīda-snehadvaṣṭināt sarvaṃ vicimiti prāsād ucyate naiṣkaina vivinā|
kimetakāyenaiyat āha sahetesyāhi|
guṇasaṃsthāmahattvācyāṃ bhikṣusaṅghasya mahattvam|
guṇamahattvamudyotayannāha—bhikṣusaṅghena sārdhamiti|
bhikṣā-gāviśeṣekhāpād bhikṣuḥ, prātimokṣāhito'dhapratyābyiśrā(3a)saṃbhupraśnabdāhu|
sārādviyamanīkāmadhetvāt|
teṣāṃ samūhaḥ saṅghaḥ ityucyate|
sārthaṃ sahaikyam|[79]
saṃkhyāmahasvamāha—akṣatrayodaśabhirityādi |
arthānityatvamanirdeśakāle pravaktavyaprativijñaptanāṃ sambhavāt |
vyākhyāte nidānam |
tadanantarasugatebrūvāca |
taṣāmakṣakūṣāha—takṣaisthādi |
tatra śabdo vākyo-nyāyo |
bhūtavāditi pravedat |
dhāresyādi |
dhīraśca gambhīraśca madhuraścodāraśceti viśeṣaḥ |
kiṃso svaraḥ ?
tena svareṇa |
tatra pakṣākṛpekṣavādīraḥ |
brahmasvaravādu gambhīraḥ |
nekamadhopadiṣṭamanodanananabhmaṇṛguraḥ |
na tu prāmyasropa-saṃhitamananāt |
tathā cāha—
śaṇīkāntamadhuraṃ saṇīkāntanirghuṣṭaṃ |
na ca mādhuryameṇadoṣaiyuktaṃ vacastave ||
dūramagamadvāraḥ |
tathāhi asau sarvaguṇarutiṃ |
dharmaṃ no bhikṣava ityādi |
dharmeti evādikaṃ deśanāṅgaṃ nyāyapari-dīpanārthaṃ |
na hi yuktamarthaṃ yuktamuktirapekṣayathuḥ |
bhikṣava ityākhyānamantraṇamadbhut |
etaca bhikṣuṇāmanyatracārāt tatprabodhīkaraṇārthaṃ ca |
tadyuktaṃ syādṛśairviniścayasūtra-deśanābhimukhakaraṇārthamiti |
kasmāc bhikṣuṇovāmantraṇaṃ nānyeṣāṃ bhikṣuṇyādi-[60]
parvedyuḥ ? śravācāryerhuṅgambhadraiḥ—"aśrāntānū, śro hṛṣṭānū, śāsanajñānū, sadā ca sānnidhyātū, grahaṇe ca śaktimaccāt, bhikṣutvamantravāyāmāsu"|
adhvārtheḥ—aśrāntaṃ veśepūrvamitvamitvatvāt|
ato jyeṣṭhaiputrabhṛtvādyopekṣāmantraṇaṃ nyāyyam|
śreṣṭhatvaṃ tu niryogāccharitarātmāṃ, prāśāśena vā sarveṣuṇāmaṃtīrṣāramatvāt|
śāsanajñamprabhavāccoddhavāca tadanyebhyaḥ pṛrato nirvedanāca|
sadā ca sānnidhyaṃ puruṣabhavajitasāmānadhyā vaittiryāgunudvahatvāt|
grahaṇe ca śaktimaccanyā|
supatsyavṛttitvād puruṣaviṣākārṣaṃ parena ca prajñātisaṃśrayogāditi|
kiṃ tatriṣṭamityāha—ādyo kalyāṇamityādi|
ādyo kṛtsnāgami[59]
śīlaśikṣāśrotenāc |
prajñe karañyānubhicakṣiśikṣāśrotenāc |
paryavasāne kalyāṇamabhiprajñāśikṣāśrotenāc |
ādimadhyaparyavasāneṣuniḥsṛtasaṃvādyaḥ tathocyate |
svarthevivṛtotattvenābhipretārthasaṃpadānāc |
sukhapradarśanaṃ gamamaktenābhidhānatapaḥ kṣapāc |
anena ca deśanādharmo brahmacaryaṃ saṃprakāśayiṣye |
brahmacaryaṃ nirvāṇaprāpako'nāsavo mārgaḥ |
nirvāṇaṃ nakṣocyate, paramapadhānakhāc |
kuta etat ?
"icchati sa bhagavān śāstā śītībhūto brahmībhūta" iti sūtrāt |[62]
tathaiva carati prāsunavṛtti brahmacaryaṃ taṣṇakkhayādhimutte|
ki viśiṣṭaṃ brahmacaryamityāha—kaivalyamiti|
atreyāṃ brahmacaryānāmabhāvāt|
kaivalyamiha dhārmikūṇāmiva vidyate|
ato'sādhāraṇatvāt kaivalyamityucyate|
sūyo nirvignaṃ paripūrṇamiti|
sārvabhūmakaleśapratipakṣatvāt paripūrṇam|
laukike hi mārge bhavābhikleśapratipakṣatvāt paripūrṇaṃ iti|
pariśuddhaṃ svabhāviśuddhasya viśuddhatvāt|
paryavedātasāmagrīsaṃjñānodaye'pi|
aupadhimanātviśuddhasya pariśuddhamiti kṛtvā|[63]
yadṛcchateri nipāta upadarśanārthaḥ |
artho'viniścaya iti |
athaiṣya vinipākāreṇa niścayo bhavatyetat avaṇāt sattvānāmityartho'viniścaya ityanugatārtho saṃjñā |
dharmeparyāye iti |
dharmaikadeśaḥ, dharmasamūha iti yāvat |
tat bhṛguriti |
tadiṣyarthaṃ nipātasamāveśe |
yasmāddṛśadiṣyāṇi tasmāttacchabdurucyate darśayati |
trividhaścopadhiḥ(44) pakṣabhāvena caitaduktam |
prabhedena viśadadvodheṇa, vyañjana-syāśravaṇāt, yadāha bhṛguriti |
etena ca śrotrāvaśane vineyāt niśeṣavati |
prajñādoṣeṇa vā dvitīyena, viparītavyañjanāśravaṇāt, yadāha sādhu ceti |
aviparītāśravaṇe mandacchandadoṣeṇa vā tṛtīyena, anākarodvṛddhaistyāśaṃsāraṇāt,
yadāha suṣṭhu ca manasikuruteti |
ādarasaṅgṛṇe etacca yathākramamadhoṅgatā-guruśiṣyabhājanopaniviṣṭautannanuviśeṣopaniṣatkam |
bhāṣiṣye iti |
evaṃ bhāṣiṣye |
anyathā'nyonyakhaṇḍanicchayā janeṣu
varṣevarṣe vyarthameva gharṣayeyuḥ syāditi bhāvaḥ |
bhāṣiṣye'haṃ kevalaśca |[68]
eṣayā tu yuktyāgiraiva samyagdvīpaniti darśayati |
'sthāne saṃgamatā catra niyojitā' iti saṃcāhāroktau bhikṣavo bhāṣate sādhukumāradṛṣṭe: |
rodhe gatāyaiṣu |
vyākhyāta upodghātaḥ |
tadanantarasūtreṣu | tabhibhūkṛdvāha | kṛtaiṣāmāśābhirarṇāni subodham |
yadvṛtteyureśanapareśanaurtha draṣṭavyam | pañca rūkathayā iti | pañce rūkathayā irathāratha yāvadvaśautyuktacañjanatānīti sakaladṛśoparisamāpiriti sūcitaṃ syāt |
uddeśaṃ nigamayavāha—aye bhikṣavaśa ityādi |
yasmāt saptaviṃśatireśau nyāyātiriktaretāḥ sūtraśataura tasmādvarya dharmethoyaśogheśa iti sambhavaḥ |
uddeśo vyākhyātaḥ |[116]
(1) pañca skandhāḥ
uddiṣṭanidāno nirdeśamāha— tatra bhikṣavaḥ ityādi praśnaḥ |
tatraithetyādinā visarjanam |
tatra śāstrendro rūpādīnāṃ skandhānāmupadarśanārthaṃ prayuktaḥ |
anyārtho'pyambhet |
ataevāṇāṃ ca rūpādīnāṃ skandhānāmatra nirdeśaḥ |
tathā cānantarameva vakṣyati tatra bhikṣavaḥ katame pañcopādānaskandhā ityādinā |
ato jñāyate upādānaṃ rūpādayaḥ skandhā atra nirdiṣṭā iti |
āryeṣāṃ samāptasya yogino yadanātmārthaṃ rūpamavidyāmūlam(4b)pramaso rūpaskandhaḥ |
yā vedanā tasmin kalāpe sa vedanāskandhaḥ |
yā saṃjñā tasmin [kalāpe] sa saṃjñāskandhaḥ |
vedanā-saṃjñe varjayitvā śestre caitāḥ śraddhādayastasmin saṃyuktā viprayuktāśca jātyādyāḥ[86]
saṃskārāḥ saṃskāraskṛbhyaḥ|
vijñānaṃ cātra kathyate vijñānaskṛbhyaḥ iti|
vijñānaskṛbhyaśceti|
nacedandaḥ saṅgacchate|
iti śabdaḥ samāpyate|
teṣām yannādya—ime bhiṣajaḥ pañca skṛbhyā iti|[116]
(2) pañcopādānaskandhāḥ
upādānaskandhanirdeśārthaṃ tatra siddhatra ityādinā praśnaḥ | tatra śabdo vākyopakramārthe | siddhatra ityāmantraṇe | pañceti nyūnātiriktaniṣedhanātsāstīya-grahaskandhapradarśanārthaṃ saṃkhyāvacanarāṇam | prāyeṇā''hustattvānāṃ vijñānaskandha evāsaṃ-grahaḥ | roṣeṣu rūpādiṣvānyīpragrahaḥ | ataḥ pañcaiva skandhāḥ |
upādānaskandhā iti | upādīnāni vakṣyām, tatsaṃjñatāḥ skandhā upādānaskandhāḥ | madhyapadalopāt druṇavṛṣagimavat | madhyapadalopamaṅgākṣā bahu-samāsenānye sādhayanti | upādānānāṃ skandhā upādānaskandhāḥ | evaṃ ca niruktaṃbhavati—tṛṣṇānāmanityatṛṣṇāniriti | guktaṃ, vai sattvasaṃkhyāśāstre upādāna-skandhāḥ; vakṣyāmi|athāntranukramehetutvāt; vākyāttu bhāvāḥ kathamupādānaskandhāḥ ?
te 'pyupādānāmisaṃhṛṣṭāḥ | ''karmajaṃ lokavaicitryamiti'' varṇanāt | upādāna-[77]
viśeṣabhedādvā, rājapuruṣavat |
yathā rājño viśeṣaḥ puruṣo rājapuruṣaḥ |
evamupādānānāṃ viśeṣaḥ rūpasya upādānaskandhayoḥ |
taddhetorlakṣaṇaṃ gamyate vā pravṛtteḥ |
teṣāṃ sparśaśca |
rūpopādānaskandhayo yānindriyāṇopādānaskandhaśca iti |
tatra rūpaskandhaścakṣurādīni pañcendriyāṇi |
rūpādayo viṣayāḥ pañcaviṃśatisaṃkhyakāḥ |
vedanāskandhaḥ sukhaduḥkhāduḥkhāsukhaśca |
sa punardvijñānaḥ ṣaḍvedanākāyāḥ cakṣuḥsaṃsparśajā yāvanmanaḥ saṃsparśajeti |
saṃjñāskandhayo'pi "nīlaghrāṇa(5a)dīrghairhasvādinimittaparicchedaḥ |
sa ca vedanāvat "ṣaṭ saṃjñākāyāḥ" |
saṃskāraskandhaśca kuśalākuśalāvyākṛtāḥ saṃyuktā viprayuktaśca prāpyamaprāpyamiti kāya-[89]
samāsato'tisaṃkṣiptaśabdāḥ saṃskārāḥ |
rūpādiviṣayoparaktavijñānasvarūpam |
sa punarvijñānaḥ ṣaḍvijñānakāyāḥ cakṣurvijñānaṃ yāvanmanovijñānamiti |
kaḥ skandhārthaḥ ? rāsyarthaḥ | kuta etat ? sūtrāt |
"yatkiṃcidupacitottānagatamupacayanam , ādhyātmikaṃ bāhyaṃ vā, "audārikaṃ vā"
sūkṣmaṃ vā, "hīnaṃ "vā praṇītaṃ vā, yadvā dūre yadvā'ntike, tatsarvamekadeśamabhisaṃkṣipya
rūpaskandha iti saṃkhyāṃ gacchatī"ti vacanāt |
ato rāsyarthaḥ skandhārtha iti[30]
siddham | evaṃ caitanātrayo'pi veditavyāḥ |
athaṃ kasmāt 'rūpamātramevo'nubhūtaḥ' ?
(1) yathodārikatena | kathaṃ ? rūpaṃ hi samavinaśvāt sarvodārikam |
alpiṇo vedanā pracārodarikatāyā | tathāhi upapadiṣṭāni 'haste me vedanā, pāde me vedane'ti | vijñānasaṃskārātthāmīnadārikatā saṃjñā | vijñānāt saṃskāra iti ato yathodārikaṃ tatpūrvakam |
(2) yathāsaṃkleśato vā | tathāhyanādimati saṃsāre klīṣṭeṣu anyonyaṃ rūpamimāmāḥ | te ca caitanātvānusaṃke | tatsṛṣṭiḥ saṃjñāviparyāsāt | saṃjñā viparyāsaḥ vastvaiḥ | śeṣaṃ ca taiḥ saṃkliṣṭamiti yathāsaṃkleśaṃ kramaḥ |
(3) bhājanārthena vā | bhājanabhūtaṃ rūpaṃ caitanyādyāśrayatvāt | bhojanabhūtā vedanā āsvādatvāt | svayaṃbhūtā saṃjñā nimittodgrahaṇena caitanādīnāṃ vyavastha-[11]
tīti ktvā |
saṃskāraḥ pakkūsūtā viṣākramanisaṃskṛtya vijñānopapadanāt |
bhoktṛsūrtaṃ vijñānaṃ tadyuyogāditi |[116]
(3) aṣṭādaśa dhātavaḥ
pañcopādānaskandhāntaraṃ dhātum nirdiśāha— tatra katame'ṣṭādaśa dhātava iti |
atra ca bhagavatā āśrayāśritalakṣaṇapṛthaktvenodāharaṇa dhātavo nirdiṣṭāḥ|
tatrāśrayabhūtaṃ cakṣurdhā(51|)turvijñānadhātuḥ | āśritabhūtaṃ cakṣurvijñānadhāturyāvanmanovijñānadhātuḥ |
āśrayanabhūtaṃ rūpadhāturyāvaddharmadhāturiti |
cakṣurādiṣātanamanakatī dvaṣṭavyāḥ | tadanukrameṇaiva tadviṣayavijñānadhātava
manukramaḥ siddha ityucyate— (1) manodhātordhruvādīnāṃ pañcānāṃ pūrvanirdeśo
vartamānaviṣayatvāt| manasastatriyato viṣayaḥ | kiñcid vartamānaviṣayaṃ kiñcit sa
ñcānaścaviṣayam| kāyadhātoḥ punaśckṣurādīnāṃ caturṇāṃ pūrvanirdeśo bhautikaviṣa
tvāt| kāyasya tvaniyato viṣayaḥ | kvacidvṛt bhūtāni, kvacidvṛt bhautika
kvacidvabhayam | śrāṇajihvācakṣuḥotreṣu pūrveṣu triviṣayatvāt| śrāṇasa
jihvāyāśca gataviṣayaprahaṇādūre itinaitiḥ| śrotrādapi cakṣuṣo dūratarā vṛttiḥ|[93]
tathāhi pañcamo'pi nardata tacchabdaśravaṇāt | dvātastanu pūrvamuktam | jihvāto drāṇaṃ punaruktamuktam, āghrātavarjitatvāt ; tathāhi jihvāmāghrātaśceva mocayet
(2) athavā'smin śarīre cakṣuṣaḥ sthānamupariṣṭāt vyavasthitam | tasmādadhaḥ śrotrasya, tasmādadho ghrāṇasya, tasmādadho jihvāyāḥ; tasmādadhaḥ kāyasya bāhulyena |
manaḥ punastātryeva niścayanirdeśasya ceti |
cakṣurādīnāṃ lakṣaṇamucyate | cakṣuścakṣurvijñānāśrayo rūpālocanārthaḥ
rūpaprasādaḥ | etena śrotrādīni pratyuktāni | etāvānatra viśeṣaḥ |[14]
nāśrayatvadiṣaśālocanakriyo rūpapasādā iti godhammam ?
kaḥ punaścittacakṣuḥ ? gotrādyaiḥ |
yathaikasmin pavate bahūni rūpyavṛkṣādi-gotrāṇi dhātavaḥ ucyante, evamekasmin santāne āśraye vā adhādaśa gotrāṇyaṣṭādaśa dhātava ucyante |
natuṃ cākāratattva gotrāṇyucyante |
tadviśe cakṣurādayaḥ kathyākarāḥ ? cittacaitānām |
tathā hi cittacaitānyetānihetupratyayārādayo bhavantītya pi dhātava ucyante |
uktā dhātavaḥ |[116]
(4) dvādaśāyatanāni
tadanantarāyapravadanīyamāha—katamāni dvādaśāyatanānīti praśnaḥ |
cakṣurādhyātmikamāyatanamiti visarjanam | nanvātmā nāsti | tadbhāvāt
kathamādhyātmikaṃ cakṣuriṣyate ? nahi śīrṣasthe'pi śiśirau śirā vyavasthāpyeta |
āśrayevad | kintvahaṅkārasanniśraya āropyamaṇavāditaḥ kathyante | taccatantu
cittahaṅkārasya tatriśraya ityāropyapadayate | tatimātramadhyadhyacāñyakam |
adhyāmevedhyātmikam | āsattenopacarite citte utpāye cakṣurāśrāyatateṃ
heturādhyātmikarucodhyate | vijñānasyāśraya iti yāvdakta bhavati | vyātyā-
tāni cakṣurādīni |
viṣayā ucyante | rūpaṃ dvidhā | varṇāsaṃkaṃ saṃsthānātmakaṃ ceti |[96]
śabdau dvividhaḥ |
upādānaheturūhetuko yathā itsalakṣaṇam, anupādānaheturūhe-tuśca yathā vāyuvṛkṣapatinadīśabdaḥ |
rāṣṭraścihnadhiḥ |
dyuguṇo durguṇaḥ samānapaśretriṃ |
rasaḥ ṣaṭprakāraḥ |
madhurāmlalavaṇakaṭutiktakaṣāyaprabhedāt |
spṛṣṭavyamekādaśavidhyamidam |
draṣṭiśvaturviṃdhodhātupāvakaśvasāraḥ; kṣudhaścāpi
karkaśādi rūkṣaṃ laghunaṃ śītaṃ nijarasaṃ pipāsā ceti |
dharmopadeśanaṃ vedanā-saṃśāsanakāraskandhaḥ, ākāśaṃ pratisaṃkhyānirodho'pratisaṃkhyānirodho'ciṃtyavikṛti sapta dravyāṇi |[97]
ka āyatanāni? cijñeyāyadvaitānyatha|
tathāhi cijñeyacāmāyadvaismi tanvantīti āyatanānīti niruktiḥ|
tanvahiṃ vistṛṇvantītyarthaḥ|
dhyākhyā- nātrāyatanāni|[116]
etadanantaraṃ pratītyasamutpādanirdeśaḥ | tatrābhidhīyate—tatra katamaḥ
pratītyasamutpādaḥ ityādi | atha pratītyasamutpāda iti ko'rthaḥ ? ucyate
pratibaṃdhasthāpaḥ | ito bhāvadha ityarthaḥ | avasthāpīnena ityarthaḥ | sa samavāyārthaḥ
ucyate; pratiḥ prādurbhāvārthaḥ | etaduktaṃ bhavati | tāṃ tāṃ kāraṇasāmagrīṃ prati ityānāṃ
samavāyenotpādaḥ pratītyasamutpāda iti |
kathamālambībhāvādiṣu pravṛttiḥ sthāpititi pravṛttisamuhebhyo nivṛttibhyaḥ
kramasaṃdarśanārthamāha—avidyāpratyayāḥ saṃskārā iti | avidyā pratyayo heturyeṣāṃ
te'vidyāpratyayāḥ| saṃskārā avidyāhetukā ityarthaḥ| evamanyeṣvapyevaṃprakāreṣu yo'pyap|[99]
atra ca pratītyasamutpāde trīṇyaṅgāni kleśāstravabhāvāni'—avidyārājaṇīpādānāni ; akṣuddhaṃ karmeṣṭamādyaṃ—saṃskārā bhavaśca ; saptāṅgāni vastusvabhāvani—vijñānanāmarūpaṣaḍāyatanasparśavedanājātijarāmaraṇātmāni, kleśakarmahetutvāt |
tatra cāvidyāyā aturvainivehetukalpadāhimān saṃsāraḥ prādurbhoti ; jarāmaraṇasya cāturvedhe'phaltvādavasānam |
aṅgāntaraṃ vā punarutsṛṣṭasyāptamyam |
tasyāpyanyadityanavasthā |
nobhayasyātamyam |
yato bhagavatā trāṇa darśitāni |
kleśāt kleśa ucyate—tṛṣṇāyā upādānam |
kleśātkarma—upādānādbhavaḥ, avidyāyāḥ saṃskārāḥ |
karmaṇo vastu—saṃskārebhyaḥ vijñānaṃ, saptāca jātiḥ |
vastuno'pi vastu—vijñānātmasthaṃ, jāteśca jarāmaraṇam |
vastuno'pi kleśaḥ:—vedanāyāstṛṣṇohi |
yasmādvaiva trayo bhavāṅgānāṃ savavistaraḥ, tasmādviyāpi kleśasambhāvā vastunaḥ kleśāditi vijñāpitaṃ bhavati |
vedanāvaṃ tṛṣṇāca jarāmaraṇahetutaḥ punaḥ kleśo bhavati |
tātra kicidajgamastyaṃ tasyam—"evamasya kevalasya sa mahato duḥkhaskandhasya[100]
samudayo bhavatī'ti vaktavyam | anyathā hi kimasya sāmarthyaṃ syāt |
ayoniśomanaskāreñjukā avidyoktā sūtrāntare—"avidyāhetukaṣkleśoniśo manaskāraḥ" iti | sa copādāne'nyabhedasadyukto bhavatītyevaṃ pūre |
atra ca pañcājñāni hetubhūtāni | avidyāsaṃskārastu(7a)ṇopādānabhāvājñāni yathāyogaṃ karmakleśa-svabhāvayoḥ | śeṣāṇi sahajāni phalasvabhāvāni |
nanvatra pūrvapakṣaṃ saṃtsāntvabyūhaḥ pratītyasamutpāda uktaḥ | "pratītyasamutpādaḥ katamaḥ | sarve saṃskṛtā dharmāḥ" iti | idaṃ tu sūtre sattvākhya evoktaḥ |[101]
abhiprāṇikacādasya mudrānirdeśasyopaniṣaḥ |
īśvarāviṣṭitaḥ saṃsāre saṃsaratīti kasyacideva vineyasya nisyakāraṇāminiveśasamohanivṛttaye |
avicāraikagamyabhūmāsthānamādharya tattvākhyaṃ pada muktave deśitaḥ|
lākṣaṇikatvadṛṣṭimūrdhaṃ lakṣaṇānirmuktatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
śokaparidevaduḥkhadaurmanasyopāyāsāḥ sambhavantīti tatra satyāvastvākhyasya viṣayasya vipariṇāmo'dvaitabhāvavapariṇāmānnabhavate bhavanti|
manādidṛśyaviṣayopāśrayaitarī'sadāhaṃḥ śokaḥ |
śokastuśrau vāṣpaśleṣaḥ paridevaḥ |[102]
paścād vyākhyāsyate|
śokaparidevasūcakaṃśramaḥ upavāsaḥ iti|
evamanyeṣvapyanusaṃ rokena nyāyena|
kaivalye kevalyāsmītyarahitasya|
mahttu ityagyādharatvam duḥkhaskandhasyetyatra duḥkhasamūhasya|
samudayo bhavatīti prāḍbhāṣaḥ|
ityevaṃ saṃsāramavṛttimākhyāya cittavṛttimāha|
avidyādibhiryogāt saṃskārādinirodhād iti vistaraḥ|
tadevamavṛttaupratipattaugataḥ pratītyasamutpādavedinaiyuktaṃ bhavati|
kasmāt punarbhedāt dvābhyāṃ prakārābhyāṃ pratītyasamutpādavedanāmāsādate?
ucyate|
vineyeṣvayaṃ bhedāt|
āśayo hi vineyānāṃ bhedavattaḥ kaścitkasyāpyadhikṛtanāyāḥ yogyaḥ|
tatrānulomikaḥ kathaṃ tasya saṃsārasya pravṛttiriti pravṛttikamasamuddeśyo vineyeṣvyavasthitaḥ|
pravṛttikamasaṃdarśanārtham|
pratilomikastu prativedhakamasamuddeśyaḥ prativedhakamasaṃdarśanārtham|
tasmādubhayathāpi deśayamāno vineyānāmadhimuktimāpnuyādityupasaṃharati yathā deśanā|[103]
kimavidyetyaṃ kevalā'vidyā uvāho sarvathaiveṣvayākaraṇairiti ?
ata: praśnagati tatrāvidyā katameti |
yadutteṣvapadakarthānāṃ visarjanam | punarukte'jñānamiti |
avidyāyā vibhāgenālambana: paryālambaṃ prabhedaṃ darśayati |
punaruktādītyātmanam ,
avidyādīni paryāyā: |
punaruktamatītaṃ janma | tatrājñānaṃ 'ki tvahamabhūvam'tīte'dhvanītayemādi vicikitsā: |
aparāntamanāgataṃ janma | tatrājñānaṃ 'ki nvahaṃ bhaviṣyā-m'nāgate'dhvanītayemādi vicikitsā: |
pratyutpannaṃ vartamānaṃ janma | tatrājñānaṃ 'ki svidadam', 'ke dhanta' ityevamādi vicikitsā: |
puthivyāṃ pṛthivīcikitsāsaṃmūḍhekā|vivekā |
etenājñānamevāvivekā |
vidyāvipakṣabhūtama-[404]
mitrādyevat |
nirveśena ca nidhānāditi darśitaṃ bhavati |
puroparastādeḍajñānamiti |
abhedena pravṛttitvaduḥkhasya yadajñānam |
pūrvaṃ vā kalpayato nāsti pūrvokto nāstyarthaparāptaḥ |
etāvāneva puruṣo yāvānindriyagocara iti |
adhyātmeḍajñānamiti |
svaṃsaṃtānesaṃcatuṣṭayato yadajñānam |
satkāyartheṣṭasvapramuktā vaiyathāviśokā |
vahirtheḍajñānamiti |
vahireva vahirtho |
nipātāntarasaṃvedyamuṣṭam |
svasaṃtānādanyatra yadajñānam |
āveṇikī vaiyathāviśokā |
iyamadhyavidyā satkāyartheṣṭamukteryantre |
tadanyakam |
yathāmārasabhūtānāmaṣṭameva satvaṣṭiḥ[405]
satkāyadṛṣṭiḥ | mithyājñānaṃ punareteṣukṛtamarya pañcamāḥ | āyakṣetano vā svaparasā-
ntānika cakṣurādivyātikam | "dvādaśāyatanikā" iti vacanāt | rūpādiviṣaya
pañcakendriyasantānajaṃ vājñānaṃ | pūrvādāmnānā vāṅaḍiviśeṣakā | adhyātmabahirdhā''jñā-
namiti | svaparasaṃtānayorabhedeneva nirātmatāmaprajānato yadjñānaṃ taccādhyātmabahi-
rdhā''jñānam | āveṇiko ceyamaviśeṣakā |
(95) karmeṇyajñānamiti | puṇyāpuṇyāstitvavicikitsātatto yadjñānam |
puṇyāpuṇyāstitvāpavādināṃ vā yadjñānam | saiva karmeṇyajñānamityuktā | pūrva-
smin pakṣe vicikitsātadnuyuktā, dvitīye tu mithyādphṭisaṃyukteti viśeṣaḥ | vipā-
ke''jñānamiti | vipākāstitvavicikitsātatsamathavādināṃ cetiṃ pūrvavaddeva vyākhyā-
nam | karmavipāke''jñānamiti | karmaṇi tahipāke cobhayatra | īśvarādisaṃprajñaptista-
jināṃ yadjñānam | saiva dīrghakālaparamedīsaṃyuktā'viśeṣakā | ahetau hetudphaṣṭiriti[106]
huktvā | tadidamaikamevājñānamupamavekoktam | anena hi vipāke 'pi saṃmūḍhā bhavanti |
tasmānyamaihetukaraṇāditi | ahetujñagṛhādināṃ vā | yo hi hetumapaśyati sa vipā-
kasya hetupūrvakatāṃ na prajānāti | tenāṃ mithyādṛṣṭerabhuktajitvādupabhogānugrahi-
spṛśyate |
sa evaṃ karmalakṣaṇo hetuḥ sukṛtādibhedenocyate yadvidhivaiśadyagrahaṇārthaṃ ca |
sukṛte karmaṇi daśakuśalakarmapathalakṣaṇe | duṣkṛte daśākuśalakarmapathalakṣaṇe |
sukṛteduṣkṛte iti śāstroktamanorathasamudācāreṇa sattvayogavṛttibhede subhāśubhakarmapatha-
lakṣaṇe | evamevāstvayaṃ vicikitsāvatāṃ tattvayādināṃ vā yattvajñānaṃ tathyākarma vici-
kitsāmithyādṛṣṭimabhuktaṃ sukṛtādāvajñānamityucyate | hetāvajñānamiti vipāke
hetāvā| phale'jñānamiti vipākaphalāvā| ahetujñagṛhādināṃ vā yadvajñānam | yaśca
hetumapaśyati sa vipākasyāpi hetupūrvakatvaṃ nābhyupapadyate | ato hetuphalāpravā-[107]
liṅgānāṃ mithyāhetusaṃyuktā 'vidyoktā|
hetuphale 'jñānamithyātvenaiva pratākṣyaṃ|
hetusaharūpatvenaivati saṃskṛteṣu dharmavikāreṣva|
pratītyasamutpādastiṣṭhati hetuphalamvicādyakramaṃ pratītyasamutpāda:, samutpannate'pyarthati kṛtvā|
pratītyasaharūpatvenaivati|
phalapratyamarṣa pratītyasamutpannam|
evaṃ ca kṛtvā saṃgīryantāni ubhayeṣāpi niṣprati hetuphalabhāvāt|
na caivaṃ satyapravatsthā hetuphalavat,
pitṛputratveneti|
evatannayobhagato vā viniścisato vā'pravṛtto vā yadjñānaṃ te'pi yathākramamārgāṅkiko viniścisato'prayuktā mithyāhetusaṃyuktā vā'vidyoktā|
buddhe dharme saṃghe vā'jñānamiti|
buddhānāṃ nārthi, dharmasya svākhyātatā, saṃghasya supratipattāṃ yathākramaṃ śraddhā tadanayobhagato vā viniścisato vā'pravṛtto vā yadjñānamiti pūrvavat|[108]
duḥkhe'jñānamiti | duḥkhasamudayasyānityaduḥkhasunyānātmalakṣaṇaṃ śrutvā tadanantaraṃ bodhanaṃ ityādi pūrvavat |
samudaye'jñānamiti | samudayasamudayasya hetusamudayamanaśca pratyayalakṣaṇaṃ śrutvā pūrvavat |
nirodhe'jñānamiti | nirodhasamasya nirodhaśāntapraṇītaniḥsaraṇalakṣaṇaṃ śrutvā pūrvavat |
mārge'jñānamiti | mārgasatyasya mārganyāyapratipattyādhigamalakṣaṇaṃ śrutvā pūrvavat |
kuśalākuśale'jñānamiti | bodhipakṣe savipakṣe |
tatra bodhipakṣyāḥ smṛtyupasthānasamyakprahāṇarddhipādasamādhibodhyaṅgāni |
tadvipakṣā ajñānakausīdyacapalatasmṛtivikṣepāsamprajanyāni darśanabhāvanāheyāścaturdaśayāḥ |
te bodhipakṣyāḥ kuśalāḥ |
teṣāṃ yete kuśalāstat |
tadvipakṣāścākuśalāḥ |[109]
teṣadhānaṃ tadanubandhodayo vā viniścikitsato vā'pavadato vā pūrvavac | sāvadyāvadye-
ṣviti | sāvadyā bodhipakṣāṇāṃ yathoktā viṣayāḥ | prāṇivadhādindra hi kuśala-
svādavadhāyiṣyucyate | tacca tadimakṣaṇāṃ kāryeṣṭu | ataste sāvadyāḥ | tadimakṣe-
ṇānavadyāḥ | ta eva bodhipakṣāḥ; vihṛdayastastvāt |
sevitavyāsevitavye(9a)ṣviti | ta eva bodhipakṣāḥ punaḥ punaḥ
saṃmukhīkartavyāpekṣayā hetutvāt sevitavyā ityucyante | hetuṃ hi punaḥ punaḥ sevanā-
mahetīti kṛtvā | asevitavyāstadimakṣāḥ | ahitutvāt na sevanāmahenti |
hīnāste viṣayāḥ kāyacittāpīṇanārthanidhīrṇatvāt | praṇītā bodhipakṣāḥ kāya-
cittāpīṇanārthatvāt | samāhitasya hiṃ prabaddhaviryaviṣayopadeva te bodhipakṣāḥ
kāryatvaṃ ca prīṇayanti | kṛṣṇāḥsadiṣaśyāḥ sāvadyatvādanirmokṣārthatve | śukrā
bodhipakṣā arthatvāt tanmokṣārthatve | ālavaṃ hi cittacetanāṃ maśravyānīyāsteṣāṃ
ca teṣu vipāma iti |[110]
pravṛttu vā sparśāyataneṣu caturdvārādiṣu yathābhūtamajñānamityamativiśeṣaḥ|
so'yamavidyāgrā ākramavaneṣeṣṭhaḥ ṣaṭtriṃśad nivṛttipakṣe cārabhya veditavyaḥ|
yā ca pravṛtterupagitanī pravṛttinibandhanā, yathā'tra saṃcāravyāyāmaḥ svaprabodhasambandhateḥ, yathā ca karmaṇo vipākānibandhanamaya ṣaṭtriṃśaḥ|
yo nivṛtterāśrayaśīlā śaraṇāni|
yadvātmanaṃ cānātmīyatvāni samāgataḥ|
dhyeyataḥ punardvādaśāyatanapratītyasamutpādāḥ|
yaḥ saṃmukho bodhipakṣāḥ|
ye cāntarāyāstadvipakṣāḥ|
yaḥ svabhāvaḥ pravṛttu sparśāyata-neṣu yathā''saṅganiveśaḥ, ahaṃ paśyāmi yāvadvijñānamīkṣyāsambandhiṣṭhakṣāyāḥ sakkā-yadṛṣṭeḥ pratipakṣātvena nivṛttipakṣaḥ|
ajñānamadarśanamityevamādi saprapañcīcārārthaṃ kimarthaṃ tu|
kasyacit kartha-cittasyaivamo'rthaṃ tatkālavihitānāṃ payāyeṇa tadarthaśravaṇārthaṃ|
tenaivābhidhānenā-syevāmavakro˅(ī) syādityevamādīni bahūni prayojanāni mantrabhāṣaṇabhāvat tocyante|
nanu cābhisamaye ukto'sti sarve'śubhapradvācārāpathyā'bhiśabdyucyate|[111]
svavidheyaṃ kaivalyaṃ abhavyavṛttinī cetyā|
vidheyāśeṣavyatirekeṇa tathādhigatasvatātvimāvikau nirdeśyaḥ, na vyākhyāvikaḥ|
vyākhyāvikastvabhidheyaṃ|
ukta-savidheyāṅgam|
(saṃskārāḥ:—2)
abhidheyāṃ vyākhyeyaṃ saṃskārāñca nirdeśyaṃvāha—abhidheyāmrayāgāḥ saṃskārā
iti|
saṃskārāḥ katame|
sandehayogād praśnaḥ|
yataḥ saṃskāraśabdo'yaṃ sarvasaṃskṛtodbhavi iṣṭaḥ "sarvasaṃskārā anityāḥ" iti|
tadekadeśe'pi yathā "saṃskāraṃ kuryya" iti|
caitanyāmapi|
"saṃskāropādānaskandhaḥ katamaḥ ?
ṣaḍevetīkāyā iti|
jīvitendriye'pi "jīvitasaṃskārā āyuḥ saṃskārā" iti|
teṣāṃ samudāyārambha|[112]
na jñāpyate ki punarihābhisaṃdhā iti |
tattriyādhāraṇamāha—naśra: saṃskārā iti |
te'pi na jñāpyante ityāha—kāyasaṃskārā vāgmanaḥsaṃskārā iti |
atra prabhedarairviniyogibhedaśca bhagavatā saṃskārāṇāṃ kāyādibhedena prabheda ukta:, na tu svabhāvaḥ |
athavā teṣāṃ svabhāvakathanārthaṃ praśnaḥ kāyasaṃskārā: katama iti |
āha—ādhyāsaṃprajñātā iti |
nanu cyānatra bhagavatā pūrvaṃ svabhāvanirdeśa kṛtvā paścāt prabhedanirdeśaḥ kṛtaḥ
yathā:—"vedanā katamā | yo 'nubhavaḥ |" sa ca "sukho duḥkho'duḥkhāsukhaśca" |
iha tu prabhedanirdeśaṃ kṛtvā paścāt svabhāvanirdeśa iti |
vidheyakaramaniyedāddvayamapyatra tathānirdiṣṭamityadoṣaḥ |
ata evocyate—
'bahūni bahurūpāṇi vacāṃsi caritāni ca |
vineyāśayabhedena tatra tatra gatāni te' ||
ādhyāsaṃ praśvāsā iti |
kasmādityāha—kaṣiko chopa dharma iti vistaraḥ |[113]
tadevaṃ tatprātibaddhacittena kāyābhisaṃskṛtatvāt |
kāyasya cādhyāsamadhyāsanimittavṛttitvāt kāyamabhisaṃskurvantīti kāyasaṃskārā ādhyāsamadhyāsā ucyante hṛdayaṅkaṃ bhavati |
vāksaṃskārān prāha—vāksaṃskārā kṛtam iti |
āha—vivakṣāṃ vicārya vācaṃ bhāṣata iti | evaṃ caivaṃ ca ( 10 a ) bhāṣiṣyata ityevaṃ | vāksaṃskārān vyākhyāya nigamayati vivakṣāvicāra iti |
tadevaṃ sati vāco'bhisaṃskaraṇābhedeti jñātavyaṃ bhavati |
manaḥsaṃskārānāha—raktaceto'yādi |
raktasya yā cetanā rāgasaṃyuktā |
evaṃ dviṣṭasya yā cetanā dveṣasaṃyuktā |
mūḍhasya yā cetanā mohasaṃyuktā |
manaḥsaṃskārān nigamayanāha—tasmādityādi |
atra ca rāgādisaṃyuktacetanāgrahaṇaṃ kuśalānijñāvedanopakṣeṇārtham |
anyathā kuśalādicetanāyāḥ saṃskāragraheṇa grahaṇaṃ na syāt |
tathā ca smṛtyantararipoḥ prasajyeta |
na caitadiṣyate |
tasmādupakṣeṇārthamiti vyākhyeyam |
tadevaṃ mano'bhisaṃskaraṇāyaina punarabhisaṃskaraṇāyaina cetiṃ jñātavyaṃ bhavati |
atra cocyate |
atrantra bhāgavatā kāyavāṅmanaḥkarmaviśeṣāḥ kāyādisaṃskārā uktāḥ |
iha svādhyāsamadhyāsāyaḥ kāyādisaṃskārāḥ |
tat kimihāpi te karmaviśeṣā gṛhyante utādhyāsamadhyāsādaya eveti ?
ubhaye'pi gṛhyante | ādhyāsādayaḥ[114]
kāyādyavisaṃskṛtāsthānaṃ saṃskāra ucyate |
pradeśāntare karmaviśeṣāḥ punarmaivābhisaṃskaraṇārthenaitaṃ |
kathaṃ jñāyate ?
sūtrāt yukteśca |
uktaṃ hi bhagavatā kūrmayoginā mama—"taṃ puṇyānapi saṃskārānabhisaṃskāro'bhiviśāpayatyanupuṇyānapi ; śāntisaṃskārānapi saṃskāro'ti avidyāmayathāni" iti |
na cākṣāsusamakṣāḥ puṇyapuṇyānijñā hṛṣyante ; mahābhūtasamāveśāt |
mahābhūtāni cānivṛttīnākṛtānīti[115]
siddhāntaḥ | punaścoktam-"puṇyabhedeṣu saṃskārānamisaṃskṛteti puṇyopamardasya vijñānaṃ bhavati | evaṃ yāvadānijñopagamanam" iti| saṃskārasyayaṃ caiṣaṃ vijñānamuktam | tasmādayasaṃskāropagamaṃ vijñānaṃ tatu saṃskārassvayamideheti nirṇīyate |
punaścoktam-"tattvātmānaṃ kāyasaṃskārānamisaṃskṛtya yāvat sattvātmane loka upaśrute" iti | na cātrāsaṃśleṣatā upaśrutehetuḥ | mahābhūtasamavetanaikātmyānirvṛttyādyuktatvāt | anirvṛttyādyuktānāṃ ca pūrvavipakṣadṛṣṭānte vipākādiṣāsamarthyatvāt | yadyevaṃ kathaṃ te kāyasaṃskārā śrotrecyante ? kāyābhisaṃskṛṇātraityādiviviktayogonnateṣu | vivakṣitavāgatapi vākyamudretyapaktvātrāvamisaṃskṛtatta iti kṛtvā tāveva nirdiṣṭo | na tu tau vākyasamayau | manaḥsaṃskāraḥ punarathecchati tayā'stu | naṃ viruddha iti |[116]
yatraivaṃvidhe sūtre kāyādyāḥ saṃskārāḥ karmalakṣaṇāḥ kasmān deśitatāḥ ityucyate |
pratyupasthitavineyaviśeṣāpekṣayā |
te hi kāyādisaṃskāraṇāṃ karmaviśeṣalakṣaṇānāmiha deśanāmantareṇātmaca evātmasaṃvedena kāyādisaṃskārān karma-lakṣaṇān pratipadyante |
anātmavineyāpekṣayaivaṃ yo'pi noktam |
tadanukūlenaiva tattvakathanapratipadyanta iti kṛtvā |
deśanāyā ekakṛtvāditi |
yuktirapyasti |
vijñānapratyayabhāvena saṃskārā iṣyante |
āśayasamuddhāsayośca kāyasaṃskāratve'ṅgīkṛtyāśayasamuddhāsayogān nobhayavikalpitāpratyayamātraṃ syāt |
na cānirdeśāvyākṛtarodhāśayasamuddhāsayorvijñānapratyayabhāvo yujyate |
yuddhinīvṛttajñānanāthayogāt |
tasmāt puṇyādilakṣaṇāḥ kāyādikarmaviśeṣāḥ kāyādisaṃskārā abhimatā iti |
uktaṃ saṃskārālakṣaṇam ||[117]
i(11a)damuktaṃ paḍurvijñānakāyā iti|
atra ca bhagavatā cakṣurādidravyabhedena vijñānasya prabheda ukto na tu svabhāvaḥ|
pradeśamātrā kālaviśeṣaviśeṣāpekṣayā|
tathāhi ṣaḍvihijñānasya viśeṣo'pi noktaḥ|
uddeśamātrametoktam|
paḍu vijñānakāyā iti|
anena ca kāyaśabdena rūpādibāhyamaneṣu cakṣurvijñānādīnāṃ vṛttibhedo'pi darśitaḥ|
nīlabāhyajñānabhedena hi cakṣurvijñānādīnāṃ bāhyaviṣayasthāpanāt|
anyathā hi ṣaḍu vijñānānyevābhedamat, kāyaśabdasya ca prayojanābhāvāditi|
saṃkhyāmātreṇa pratipāditaṃ dṛḍviti|
na tu nirdeśata iti āha—cakṣurvijñānaṃ yāvanmanovijñānamiti|
atha kasmāt nirdeśo'pi noktam—cakṣurvijñānakāyo yāvanmanovijñānakāya iti|
atrocyate|ḍa hetorihāśabdyaihṛttiyetyasya saṃvarṇitatvāt|
nāmno vijñānāt kāya iti ṭīkānārtha ceti|
kiṃ punaridaṃ cakṣurvijñānaṃ yāvanmanovijñānamityasya sūtre noktam, athapratra tūttaram—"cakṣurāśrayā rūpaprativijñaptiścakṣurvijñānam"|[116]
vyadharmetinirjñātaḥ śrotrādivijñānamiti ? tadevātra vaiditavyamitīti |
tanu cātmana pratisaṃvijñānaṃ saṃskārapratyayaprakam—"tadicchakramaprāptu vijñānami"ti vacanāt |
yathākramaṃ janmāntareṣu pratisaṃvijñānabhāvati |
smṛterapyuktam—"vijñānaṃ caiva tānanda yāhuḥ kuddhiṃ nāvakāmeneva tu tanmāpuraṃ kathañcanāyāmisaṃsṛjchedi"riti |
"no bhavanti" |
vijñānaṃ cātra manovijñānamabhimatam |
"chedaṃ suṣamaniraṃśehanicyukhupapanno manovijñāna eveśraḥ" iti varṇanāt |
ihaṃ tu vijñānaviśeṣe vijñānakṛtamatra |
paṭ vijñānakāyā ityuktam |
manovijñānena ca pratisaṃvijñānaṃ na manuṣiriti |
kathaṃ na syādirodhaḥ ? nāsti virodhaḥ |
vijñānamabhūtaprasaṅgena hi bhagavatā sakale vijñānamastin sūtre vibhaktam |
saṃskārapratyaye tu pratisaṃvijñāne yathāsambhavaṃ veditavyam |
yathā nāmarūpaviśeṣe nāmaṃ vitastareṇoktam—"rūpaṃ katamat ? yad kiṃcidrūpaṃ sarvaṃ tacca tvāri mahābhūtāni, catvāri ca mahābhūtānyupādāye"ti sakalaṃ rūpaṃ vibhaktam |
vijñānapratyaye tu nāmarūpe[119]
vyavasthāpyamāne yathāsambhavaṃ vyavasthāpyatva iti|
tadvṛt|
tasmādvijñānamūrtimadhya-nāmiṣyate śabdairvā nirdeśo na lākṣaṇikaiḥ|
lākṣaṇikaistatsvayamiti|
sautrāntikamatena svavirodha eva|
yasmāttasya saṃskāroparibhāvinaḥ ṣaṭ vijñānakāyāḥ saṃskārasyaiva vijñānamiṣṭaṃ na pratisandhivijñānameveti vijñānātmakavyākhyā||
(nāmarūpam—5)
vijñānāṃśaṃ vyākhyāya nāmarūpāṃśamāha—vijñānapratyayaṃ nāmarūpamiti|
vijñānapratyayaṃ nāmarūpamityuktam|
nāmaśabdaścāyamadhivacane viprayukte'pi rūh,
caturvedaviṣu skandheṣu|
ataḥ prasaṅgaḥ tatra kṛtamalābha|
viprayuktanirākārthamāha—cakṣurādīndriyāṇi rūpebhya iti|
sautrāntikamatena bāhyasyabhāvasyād-vijñaptiṃ rūpamantaram|
vaibhāṣikamatena tu viprayuktatvād saṃskārarūpamantaram|
samastaṃ caiva dvaye vijñānapratyayaṃ nāmabhimataṃ nādhivacanamātram|[120]
cedanātmavaiyathyāt | sukhāduḥkhamabho vedanā | nimittodgrahaṇaṃ saṃjñā | tadanvaitasikā praśāntiścāniścayajñāsya saṃskāraskandhayaḥ |
viṣayīkaraṇavijñānāsini karasācakṣāraḥ skandhā nāmetyucyante ?
tṛṣṇādimadhyakrindanakrameṇaparivartanamanāda, upapattyantaraṃ rāmanādisyathaḥ |
rūpādidravyeṣu namatīti vā nāma bālakanākāragrahaṇāditi yo°ūthaḥ |
pravacane mūtahaṃ bhautikaṃ cetyuktam |
ato rūpaṃ kratamahiti prasraḥ viṣajīnamāha—yatkiñcidrūpam |
patena yatkiñcidrūpamiti nirdeśena bhūtabhautikam[121]
tiviktaṃ rūpaṃ nāstīti darśitaṃ bhavati | upādāyeti hetukṛtetyarthaḥ |
yathaivamanupādāyānirūpitasya ityamahetuko'niriti gamyate, evaṃ mahābhūtānupādāyeti mahābhūtāni hetukṛteti gamyate | ta ete pañcaskandhāḥ kalalalarbudaghanapeśīśākhāmahāśākhādirūpā atisthūlapañcakanakā vijñānasaṃcayaṃ nāmarūpaniṣpannaṃ bhavati |
bhūtātyucyante | bhūtāni kṛtamānītyāha—tadyathā pṛthvīdhāturiti |
dhātuprakaraṇaṃ varṇotsthānākṛtyutpattinirmāṇārtham | svalakṣaṇapṛthagadāpralakṣaṇādad
dhātuḥ | vāyudhāturdehamadhāraṇādyarthakāraḥ | tvagādaye mahābhūtāni | na trīṇi[123]
na paśyati | vyarthaivādāsādhyatvācca paryaṃkapādakamiv | svarūpānavabodhāt pṛcchati-praśnīpātraṃ karomi iti | āha-yadu gurutvamiti | gurutvaṃ nāmopādāya-rūpamadhyati tasya pratipattirnā bhavati payomāha-kaṅkaṭeṭamiti | kaṭhinaṃ-misarathaṃ | laghuriti | laghutvalakṣaṇo vāyudhātuḥ | tehṛlpasaṃyutāvāsteṇayastīti śataḥ karmaṇārthaṃ vāyudhātoḥ svabhāvo'bhiṣyaktaḥ | yadīraṇātmakaṃ laghutvaṃ na vāyudhātuḥ |
laghveva tu caivaśya tadupādāyarūpamiti | idaṃ rūpaṃ pūrvake ca nāma tadaikathamatisaṃ-kṣipteti tadubhayaikārthaṃ darśayati |
nannu copapattiṇāmādṛṣṭaṃ nāmarūpaprayayenaiva nāmarūpaṃ bhavatīti kathaṃ tarhi tān-pratyayamiti ? ucyate | na svajātidṛṣṭaṃ nāmasmaraṇamātra etannimittānāṃ vicāritaṃ |
kiṃ tarhi viśiṣṭaviśiṣṭadṛśyāntarapratyayabhāvo vicāritaḥ | itarathā'vicāryavicā-rapratyayo vaktavyaḥ, vijñānaṃ ca vijñānapratyayamiti | uktaṃ nāmarūpam ||[123]
nāmarūpajñaṃ vyākhyāya ṣaḍāyatanamāha—nāmarūpapratyayaṃ ṣaḍāyatanamityādi |
nāmarūpapratyayaṃ ṣaḍāyatanamityuktam |
tatra ṣaḍāyatanaṃ dvividham |
bāhyamādhyātmikaṃ ceti|
ato bāhyānirāsārthaṃ bravīti—ṣaḍadhyātmikānyāyatanā(12b)-nīti vistaraḥ |
āyatanaśabdo vyākhyeyaḥ |
nanu yadā pratisandhikṣaṇāt prabhṛti nāmarūpābhyāṃ kāyamanaāyatanayoḥ saṃbhedo'sti tatkathaṃ nāmarūpapratyayaṃ ṣaḍāyatanamityucyate ?
tatpratyayaṃ caturāyatanamiti vaktavyaṃ syāt |
āstetadevaṃ |
kintu śāśrotrapraṇidhigocaraiṣuktam |
kāyāśritacakṣurādayasto sattvo kāyāśrayanaṃ paripūrṇaṃ bhavati |
tadā tasyāśrayadvārasmānmanaāyatanaṃ yaṃ paripūrṇaṃ bhavati |
ṣaḍvijñānaguhītatvayaṃ manaāyatanasyāśrayapratyayādayattatvāt paripūrṇaṃ iti |
sakalāśrayasamavāyiti bhāvaḥ |
vyākhyātaṃ ṣaḍāyatanam ||[124]
etadanantaraṃ sparśoṃ vyākhyeya ityāha— ṣaḍ|yatanasparśayaḥ sparśo iti
ṣaḍ|yatanasparśayaḥ sparśo ityuktam |
sparśo'pyāyaṃ pravartane spṛṣṭe'pyapi dṛṣṭaḥ
saṃgatirapyatro'pi cetasikaṃ |
ato na niyamyate kramaḥ ṣaḍ|yatanasparśayaḥ sparśa iti |
tatriṣpāṇaśāstremāha— ṣaṭū sparśakāyāḥ iti |
prabhede'pi paridṛśyamatikatame ṣaṭū |
āha—cakṣuḥsaṃsparśo yogyamanaḥsaṃsparśa iti |
atra ca bhagavataścakṣurādyāyatanena sparśasya prabheda ukto na svabhāvaḥ |
tatkāśaviniye'viśeṣapekṣayaivasaṃśabdaprāptyātimetat |
kratasya svabhāvaḥ ? aṣṭayatroktaṃ—"ṣaḥ kaścidvedanāskandhaḥ saṃjñāskandhaḥ saṃskāraskandhaḥ sarve sparśo pratītyo'pi" |
nanvatrau vedanādivannamatā na paricchidyate |
kathamasau pratyetavyaḥ cakṣurādivat , āsavakṣayāt |
yathā—"cakṣuḥ karamamaṃ ? cakṣurvijñānāśraya rūpasādaḥ" |
na ca praśārakṣaṇaḥ paricchidyate |
vijñānakāyīpañcamaduktvācchinnamato'pi svadṛṣṭeranugamyate |
tadvat |[125]
nanu tasya svabhāva: kathita eva bhagavatā-"bahuḥ pratītya rūpāṇi cotpadyate cakṣurvijñānamiti|
ya evaṃ trayāṇāṃ saṃgatiḥ saṃgatiḥ sannipātaḥ saṃ sparśa"
iti vacanāt|
kāraṇe kāryopacāra eṣaḥ|
buddho'niruktir yathā|
yasya tu sannipātāñcchattasparśaḥ sparśestattvayeṃ parihāraḥ|
yasya tu sannipāta eva sparśastasya svabhāvo nirdiṣṭa iti goddhavyam|
atra ca "cakṣuḥsaṃsparśoṃ yāvanmanaḥsaṃsparśa" iti|
sarvavijñacakṣurindriyaśrotrādīni pañca pratipatsaṃsparśā ucyante|
manaḥsaṃsparśo'dhivacanasaṃsparśa iti|
yasmājjanamatikramyārthaiṣu manovijñānasya pravṛttiḥ, na pañcānāṃ vijñānakāyānāmavataraḥ|[136]
vāṣivacanam | tena saṃprayuktaḥ sparśo manaḥsaṃsparśa iti | prathama āśrayaprabhāvito dvitīyaḥ saṃprayogaprabhāvita iti | uktaḥ sparśaḥ ||
(vedanā–7)
vedanā vaktavyā | tatsamprayuktakāyidyāha–sparśopasthayā vedeti |
sparśamasyā vedanetyuktam | vedanā ca trividheti | katamā vedanā sparśapratyayā'-
stītyabhipretetyāha–ṣaḍū vedanākāyāṃ iti | katame ṣaṭ? ityāha–cakṣuḥsaṃsparśajā
śrotrasaṃsparśajeti | pūtena tātkālikaniyatāṃ bhedasaṃmātravedanād vedanāyāḥ
prabheda eṣa darśito na tu svabhāva iti | svabhāvastvekatvāt | vyākhyātam–
"cakṣuḥ pratītya rūpāṇi cotpadyate cakṣurvijñānam | trayāṇāṃ saṃ nipātaḥ
sannipātaḥ | tato jātā vedanā cakṣuḥsaṃsparśajā vedanā" i sā ca tridhā |[127]
sukheti sātaṃ |
indriyamahābhūtānugrāhiketi yotheṃ |
duḥkheṣyamātā mahābhūtopaghātiketyarthaḥ |
aduḥkhāsukhā punaretadubhayaviparītā |
evaṃ trīṇāmapi yogam |
manaḥsaṃsparśajā sukheti |
sātakṣaṇāt somanasyaṃ sukhabrahmanīkam |
duḥkheti daurmanasyamasātakṣaṇāt |
yadi vitarko vedanā, yaduktaṃ bhagavatā sutrāntare—"yakiñcidduṃ veditamidamatra duḥkhasya"iti taddraṣṭavyate |
nai virudhyate |
abhiprāyakramo'nyatra |
ko'trābhiprāyaḥ ?
"saṃskārāḥ(139)svatāmānandaṃ mayā sandhāya bhāṣite saṃskāraviṣayiṇāmatāṃ ca—yakiṅcidveditamidamatra duḥkhasya"iti |
gāthāyāmapyuktam—
"saṃskāranimpatāṃ jñātvā atho vipariṇāmatām |
vedanā duḥkhabaḥ proktā saṃbuddhena prajānatā" ||[128]
iti bhagavadvede cābhiprāyo darśitaḥ |
tasmāt ṣaḍdvāradvāruddhayorvedanayoḥ svabhāvikaṃ duḥkhatvaṃ nāstītyabhiprāyikaṃ duḥkhatvaṃ darśitamiti |
uktā vedanā ||
vedanārhaṃ tṛṣṇācasara ityāha-vedanāpratyayā tṛṣṇeti |
vedanāpratyayā tṛṣṇotyuktaṃ |
sā cāśrayabhedena ṣaḍvidhā |
ṣaḍdvāreṣu punaḥ kāyādibhedena trividhā |
ālambanabhedena punaḥ ṣaḍvidheti |
ato nāvayavate vedanāpratyayā tṛṣṇā katham |
ityāha-yadyu tṛṣṇākāyā iti |
atrāpi bhagavatā rūpādiviṣayābhivyaktabhedena tṛṣṇāyāḥ prabheda uktaḥ, na tu tasyāḥ svabhāvaḥ |
viṣayaviśeṣopekṣayā |
svabhāvaḥ punaḥ sarveṣāṃ abhiṣvaṅgalakṣaṇamiṣṭakammeva |
abhiṣvaṅgaḥ punastṛṣṇāprakāraḥ rūpādau viṣaye cittasantaterabhīkṣṇaṃ pravṛttiḥ |
na cāntareṇābhiṣvaṅgaṃ tathā pravṛttirupapadyate grahīṇatṛṣṇānāṃ tadabhāvāt |
yadi vedanāpratyayā tṛṣṇā, sarveṣāmasti vedanāsyathairte'pi tṛṣṇāprasaṅgaḥ syādityāśaṅkya tvāvat |
katham ?
yathaivapratyayā vṛṣṭirityucyate, kiṃ saiva sati avaśyaṃ[129]
vṛṣabhairneti ? evaṃ satyamapi vedanānāṃ nāvaśyaṃ tṛṣṇā bhavati |
kasmāt ? pratipakṣopadeśakatvāt |
pratipakṣaviśeṣeṇa hi tṛṣṇājīvitaduḥkhaṃ bhavati venādvaito vedanopadrave'pi nirvāṇā nopapadyate |
yathā saskṛtapi kṣīrodakādiṣu pratyayeṣu na nirvāṇāṅkurādibhāvi iti |
virodhinaṃ cātmatra bhagavatā—"avidyāsaṃsparśajaṃ veditaṃ pratītyotpavateḥ tṛṣṇā" iti |
avidyāsaṃsparśajavedanābhāvāt tadbhāvaḥ, pratipakṣopetena na īśaṇatvāt | tasmāddvaito nopapadyate tṛṣṇā |
ihāpi tahiṃ tathāṃ kiṃ na virodhitam—"avidyāsaṃsparśajaveditapratyayatṛṣṇā iti ?
avidyāyāḥ saṃskārasāmānyapratyayatvenārtharthaṃtvācca jñāpitatvāt |
yadi vedanāpratyayā tṛṣṇā, kimarthaṃ kvacid dravyādiṣu tṛṣṇāyāḥ aviśeṣoktaḥ pratyayaḥ—"pṛṣṇāyāḥ bhikṣavo'vidyā heturavidyā pratyayo'vidyā nidānaṃ" iti |
kvacit sparśo'pyuktaḥ "ṣaṣṭhaḥsaṃsparśo tṛṣṇe" iti |
iha punarvedanāpratyayavaṃ bodhakiti | kathaṃ na syādvirodhaḥ ? na syāt, abhisambandhemātrāt |
avidyā hi tṛṣṇāyāḥ sāmānyapratyayaṃ samprayoktā sparśaśca | yasmāt sarveṣāṃ tṛṣṇānāṃ dhātu-[130]
bhūmigatimitrānāmanyeṣāṃ ca kleśānāmavidyā sādhāraṇo hetuḥ; evamavidyāprayoktrṛtvaṃ
"tṛṣṇāyā vipradoṣo'vidyā heturi" ti|
sparśo'pi tṛṣṇāyāmāmānye'kṣeya eva
sarvecaitetasmātsamānvayavyavyāt—"ṣaṭ cakṣurādisaṃsparśajā tṛṣṇā" iti|
atastṛṣṇāyāḥ sparśo heturityuktam|
vedanā punarvi śeṣaheturanumisandhīyoktā|
tadyāhi kāmabhūmikāyāḥ tṛṣṇāyāḥ kāmabhūmikaiva vedanā hetuḥ; prathamadhyānabhūmikāyāḥ prathamadhyānabhūmikaiva|
evamuttarāsu bhūmī vedanā prajñādibhedena tṛṣṇāviśeṣasyahetvamupādāyāt vedanā viśeṣahetvayaṃ tṛṣṇāyāḥ sandhyaukteti gamyate|
tṛṣṇoktā||[131]
daśaṃ ca paryaṃvasthānoni kāmopādānam |[132]
iṣṭiparāmaśaॅtreṅgāṅkā daṇḍyāpādānam | śīrendraparāmaśaॅtreṅgāṅkaḥ śīrendrato
pādānam | śīre śīśoṣṭhambitasriḥ | vrataṃ vaiśāraṅkārāsaṅgrahāyātrāñca kāya-
vākṣaṇoḥ kāyavāksamṛtiniyamaḥ |[133]
karamādidaṃ śītaghnatoṣadānaṃ iṣṭibhyo niṣkṛtya pṛthagyuktam | ucyate |
mārgaśidṛṣṭihṛdakhān | sāṃkhyayogajñānavijñāneṇa mokṣaprāptisaṃdarśanāt , pṛṣṭipraja-
jighṛkṣupathavipraṣṭamanākha | pṛṣṭiṇo'pyanena vipralabdhā anajānādibhiḥ svagomārgasaṃjñā,
pravartitā api ihaviṣayaparivartanena śuddhipratyāyāmanāt | etenāvacanādeyaḥ śītaghna-
tasṛṭṭītā iti |[137]
rūpālambyāvacarā rāgamānādikyā darśanamānavāheyāḥ vicikitsādayo'pi darśane
heyā eva |
idamupādānamūlatvenopādānamiti |
upādānamuktam ||
(bhavaḥ––10)
upādānaṃ vyākhyāya bhavāṅgaṃ nirdiśannāha––upādānapratyayo bhava iti
bhavaḥ kramaḥ |
upādānapratyayo bhava ityuktam |
bhavaścāyameva pañcopādānaskandha
iti nirdiṣṭam |
sthānāntare punarekamevābhipretena tama bhedā ityuktam |
ato ma
jñāyate bhavaścedena ka ucyate ityāha––caityo bhavā iti |
katame caitya ityā
––kāmabhava ityādi |
kāmadhātusaṃyukto bhavaḥ kāmabhavaḥ |
madhyapadalopād[135]
marīcagānakavade| evaṃ rūpapratisaṃyukto bhava:, ālambapratisaṃyukto bhava:, yathāyogaṃ rūpamana ālambamabhyasyatīti|
pramedaṃ eva kāmasañjñānāmukto na tu svabhāva iti| ata: svabhāvanirdeśārthaṃ praśnayati—kāmaprama: katama iti| nirdiśyati—uṣṇanarakā aṣṭāviti| śītanarakā a( 15 a )pi santi tadvyabhañce'nuśṛṇuṇāgṛhaṇāt|
te tu śītanarakā: paścād vakṣyante|
tatra nīryete'smin saṃjñā apuṇyavedetīti naraka:| sañjīva ityādi|
yatra sattvān vidyamāṇān vāyava: saṃjīvayanti sa saṃjīva:| yatra kālasūtrāṇi[136]
pātacitrāḥ sattvāstadbhuyate tatkālamanugam|
yatra meghākulam: parvato ubhayata vyāptavantaḥ sattvāḥ pīḍyanti sa saṃśoka:|
yatra sattvāḥ pālyamānā: paramahṛṣṭakaṃ svatantra[137]
sa raudraḥ|
yatra tu viśeṣeṇa sa mahāraudraḥ|
yatrāgnyādimistulyatvena
sattvaḥ sa taptanaḥ|
yatra tu viśeṣeṇa sa pratāpanaḥ|
yatrātipākādipañcakleśāgnibhi-
stānāṃ sattvānāṃ sukhabodhiratnaṃ dānaṃ tīkṣṇāgnisaṃstṛtaṃ|[236]
śrīmanuṣkā atho | kai punasate | arddhudvativaṃ hṛthādi |
arddhadrohmbānāṃ sattvānāmanudvekārā: kāce piṭakāḥ śītena jāyante |
nirudvudve skhadudvā-tikāntamānāṇā jāyante |
aṭeṭe śīteto'proṣaviśeṣeṇa jihvayā śabdaṃ kurvanti |[139]
hṛhadvṛddhayogīrjihvāyā apyapaśvādantarīṣṭhakena |
utpalodidṛgvalkṣaṇākāreṇa kāye māṃsātiśaye'ro jāyate |
ataḥ kāśyapaśabdavikāraśyātīte evaināmārgaṃ iti |
pretā iti |
bhūśamito'punarbhūteritā iti pretāḥ |
piśācayā vā pretā iti pretaiḥ |
tivegamanāttiṣya itre |
manaujjhuṅvenāmanucchayā iti pravacane |
mano'paśyānīti laukikaiḥ |[140]
pūr devā iti|
dīrghādīti daivīḥ|
cāturmahārājiko iti|
catvāro mahārājāno lokapālā:—virūḍhako virūpākṣo dhṛtarāṣṭro vaiśravaṇasteti|
teṣu bhavāścāturmahārājikā:|
tanmadhyagatā ityartha:|
tathā tenaivopapattaye devamadhya upavaṣṭavya ityartha:|
cāturmahārājasthāne bhavāścāturmahārājikā:|
madhyamalokāt|
gauraścaturdikpure|
naṣaṭtriṃśā iti|
sahaṅkṛtacaturdaivai: karmabhisteṣūpapattate iti|
naṣaṭtriṃśā:|
atho vasava:, dvādaśādityamāro, ekādaśa rudrā dvādaśādityā:|
evoktamanubhūyatalād naṣaṭtriṃśā iti lokikā:|
tadanusāreṇa ca pravacane tathā vyavahāra iti|
yāmā iti|
yātāmtava pṛṣṭheti yāma:|[141]
yāmo yāmo sro hulamaho hulamicamudrātraścanoti vai yāmā: | tuṣitā iti | tuṣeṇa
tuṣṭyā hutā: yuktā iti tuṣitā: | tuṣā vā saṃjñāto pūrṇomiti tuṣito: |
nimmāṇarataya iti | svayaṃ kṛtānāṃ bhogānāṃ nirmāṇe ratireṣāmiti nirmāṇarataya: |
paranirmitavaśavartinaśceti | paranirmitān bhogān vaśe vartayaṃ śīlameṣāmiti
paranirmitavaśavartina: |
tathayā brahmakāyikā iti | tatra druhukukauśalamūlanirvṛttatvād brahmā |
kathyāsau ? yo mahātratasaṃyukte | dhyānāntaramāpād | paścādvṛttiścaturthapati-
bhāvāt | pūrvebhyasya sahalo kāyastatra nivāsa evaṃ vijñaya iti brahmakāyikā: |[142]
brahmapurohitā iti|
brahmaṇaḥ puro dhīyante iti brahmapurohitāḥ|
mahābrahmāṇa iti|
āyurvarṇeśvariddhiviśeṣaviśiṣṭo mahābrahmā eṣāmiti mahābrahmāṇaḥ|
parītā eṣāmābhā svarguptimadhyeti parītābhāḥ|
nābhābhāḥ prabhāṇo nābhāprabhāṇaḥ|
prabhānupetānihapraprabhāṇābhāḥ|[143]
sukhaṃ śubhamityucyate | tadeṣāṃ svayaṃramiṇīdhrama ( sic ) parītaniti parītaśubhāḥ|
aśrāṇāṃ śubhabhogamiti aśubhāśubhāḥ | hṛstena śubhabhogamiti śubhaṃkṛhastāḥ |
na tadkuchadhanaṃ sukhamanyaśāstrīyaṣaḥ | abhraveśāṃ bhūmisambandho nāstītyuna-
bhrakāḥ | "saha satvena tadbhimāno'dyapyathāpra"ti vacanāt | ānirjikarmasaṃdhṛta-
tvāt puṇyebhyaḥ pralaya eṣāmiti puṇyapralayāḥ | sthānāntaravinnaṃ pātheyanikaṃ
sarvo'ṅkhaṃ vṛhadeṣāṃ phalamudrāvatīti vṛhatphalāḥ | anudrā iti | anudrāhārasya
yāvadukṛtiṣṭā iti pakṣe śudrāvāsayutāḥ | ( 16 a ) pṛthagjanānāmaśravān śudra
āvāsa eṣāmiti śudrāvāsāḥ| śudrāvāsatvareśo'pyukkuṣṭīlāva vṛttitā ityanūrāḥ |[144]
nāśena vā kālenāsamanaḥ sthāne buddheti jahatītyabuddhe||
viśiṣṭasamābhiśvāsābhyāṃ caleśvāḥ saṃtāpayantītyanupāyāḥ|
parichuddhadvaitanenāvāc saṃtuṭṭa paramparīti suduṣṭāḥ|
śrīmanadvaitanenāvāc mūḍharnenāḥ|
etatprakartiṣṭāḥ jyeṣṭhaprakṛtāvi|
ākāśānantarāyatananirayādi|[124]
samāpattiprayogakāle'kāratvātkāśānvayam | tasya tadevāvedanam | oṃnenaiva vijñānamananena
vijñānamiti samāpattiprayogakāle'kāratvāt tasya tadevāvedanam | nāsti
kiñciditi samāpattiprayogakāle manasikārādau tasya tadevāvedanam | saṃjñā gaṇḍaḥ[146]
saṃjñā śabdaḥ, akṣikamapi saṃmoha ityavaḥ saṃjñāmātrotsavaḥ; naivasaṃjñānā-
masaṃjñāyatanam | ayamṛṇādānasatyayo bhava ucyate |
atra ca bhaviṣyatsaṃśayākṛmavakūle karma sūtraśabdenoktam | bhavasenenaivatat
punarbhava iti sūtraṃ; yathā vṛhaspateneti vṛhaḥ | uktaṃ ca sūtrāntare—"yadutthānami[140]
kamāśrayāṃ punarbhevābhinirveṣṭakamimañca bhavetye"iti vistaraḥ |
prakrameṇetyuktam—
"kāmabhavaḥ katamaḥ ? yatkarma kāmapratisaṃyuktamupādānapratyayamāyatyāmabhinirvarttakami"ti vistaraḥ |
"rūpabhavaḥ katamaḥ ? yatkarma rūpapratisaṃyuktami"ti vistaraḥ |
"ārūpyabhavaḥ katamaḥ ? yatkarmārūpyapratisaṃyuktami"ti vistaraḥ |
tasmād bhaviṣyadvṛttaphale karmeveha vaiśeṣikaḥ kāmādibhedo'bhipretaḥ |
upapattisthānanirdeśastveva upapattikramevaitaddarśanārthamiti |
yadyatra bhavaḥ karmetyabhāvaḥ, saṃskārāṇāṃ sabhava ca ko viśeṣaḥ ? atītānantaryaṃ karma saṃskāraḥ, vartamānāntaryaṃ karma bhava ityabhayorviśeṣaḥ | uktaṃ bhavāṅgam ||
bhavaḥ (15 |) itya vyākhyāya jātiṃ nirdiśannāha—bhavapratyayā jātiḥ |
teṣāṃ teṣāṃ bhāvupavinidhyānāttatsaṃbhāvabhedabhinnānām |
tadristastubhyaṃ sarvanikāye iti |
yo yasya saṅgahaḥ sarvasaṅgraho devādikaḥ |
jātiḥ saṃjātiriti |
avasthāviśeṣatardaśanārthaṃ paryāyocāraṇam |
yathāśikṣasya hi nāmarūpādivedanotpattya[116]
bhavati | jātasya nāmassamparipūriḥ saṃjñātiḥ | yathā saṃparipūrṇapaṃcaḥ saṃjātavastu ityucyate loke |
tadvat | saṃpūrṇanāmarūpasya viśiṣṭakāyāvasthāyāmihopapattiḥ |
upapannasyāvaśiṣṭendriyāvasthāpamanasaṃkāntiḥ | avakrāntapūḍḍhātanasya mātuḥ kṣetramabhiniṣkramato viśeṣotkarṣavyāptimūlā nirvṛttirnimittavṛttiḥ |
yonidvārasaṃghaṭṭaḥ prasavādṛṣṭe bhāvaḥ prādurbhāvaḥ | evaṃ jñātvā paripūrṇaṃ janma saṃpṛṣṭovai bhavati | idaṃ jāteravasthābhedalakṣaṇam |[479]
svalakṣaṇaṃ rūpajātirasabhedaiḥ saṃjñānām |
ātmasāṃve daraśanavāha—
skandhapratilambha ityādibhiḥ |
eva hi saṃjñānāṃ trividha ātmalambhaḥ |
skandhādisaṃjñāne saṃsvasaṃjñābhiniveśo'pi "trividhākṣakalpanāccutāsādyaṃ skandhādiprayamaha-
ṇam" |
'ekko' 'dehī' 'viṣayopabhoktrā' cāsmīti |
rāśibhedo hi skandhārtha iti "skandhamahaṇe'kāmakalpanāccutāsādyam" |
dharmamātraṃ buddhvā(sic) dhātu riti nivecanāt dhātugrahaṇaṃ deśātmakalpanāccutāsādyam |
cakṣurādauni viṣayopalambhe-rāyatanāni |
taiṣiḍakhāyatane kuvidurūpīti āyatanagrahaṇaṃ nadītaṭakalpanāccutāsādyam |
evaṃ vistareṇāvasthābhede jātilakṣaṇaṃ nirdiśya punaḥ saṃkṣepeṇa nirdiśati—skandhyā-yatanadhātubhiriti liṃga, jīvitendriyasya grāhyabhāvaṃ iti |
evaṃ ca saṃvedyabodhapudgalānāṃ matiṃ saṃjñānāṃ jātilakṣaṇamabhihitaṃ bhavati |[140]
tatrābhinavā nivṛtterabhinivṛttiḥ|
jau vinotkṛṣṭaṃ sattvān
pūrvekabhāvedaṃ samāyāsattānuṣaṃgane samarthe yena tasmin nikāyasabhāge nivṛtta
rūpaṇyāḥ pravattaidravyā viśiṣṭen|
sā ca sthitijātiviśiṣṭayāmityucyate|
nikāyasabhāge samāsena pratisaṃdhipratyayaḥ samabandho jātivisyuktaṃ bhavati|
yuktaṃ tāvat pañcavidhāyāṃ jātau prathamā jātirbhedapratyayeti|
tatpuruṣattarapratyayeti kathaṃ saṃpratyayetyucyate?
bhavena hi prathamāyāṃ jātau tadvat
sāmarthyamādhīyate|
yaddvidhāpañceṣāpañcasthānatarāpunarutpattirūpavatāṃ gacchatīti tanmukha
katvāttatpratyayetyucyate|
uktā jātiḥ||
(jarāmaraṇam—12)
jautiṣadhvaṃse jarāmaraṇamiti|[154]
yathoktaṃ ca nirdeśo bhavati |
ato jarānirdeśārthamāha—jarā katameti |
yacchālikyaṃ pālityamiti vistareṇa ṣāṭdraśabhiḥ padaiḥ savākāravipariṇāmalakṣaṇā paribhedavarā dṛśyate |
rūpavipariṇāmalakṣaṇā keśavināśanebhyate | keśavināśaḥ yadāha—khālityamṛ |
keśavaiguṇyate: yadāha—pālityamṛ |
yukte keśavaiguṇyato rūpavipariṇāma-lakṣaṇā paribhedavarā prasādyabhyāhata vipariṇāmaḥ, vipariṇāmaśca jareti |
keśavināśastu kathaṃ jarā ?
ucyate |
tadvānatīyakatvātṛ keśavināśasya |
na hvantareṇa vipariṇāmaṃ keśavināśo'stīti |
svabhasaṃkocanataḥ; yadāha—balībhavurateti |
na vinā svabhasaṃkocaṃ balīpracuratā bhavati |
cyutipariṣedataḥ yadāha—śīrṇeti |
māṃsaśoṇitakṣayaḥ:—yadāha śuṣyatīti |
śuṣkamānanetyarthaḥ |
saṅgrīvat(sic)śarīra-saṃsthānatikūrvataḥ:—yadāha kubjagopānasībhrakṛteti |
gopānasī kuṭāgārodahi sarvakāṣṭhopadhāraṇakāṣṭham |
sā ca kāṣṭhukavadṛ vakrā |
tadbajayā śarīraṃ bhavati |
gopālasyā(hi)ṅkā (?) gopānasīsaparai |[152]
tilakālaṅkācirabhāganeti |
bāḍhkevāsthāyāṃ gātraṃ gauraṇaṃ tilakaiścitaṃ bhavati balavipariṇāmalakṣaṇāṃ sadābdaitibhāvaśrutaḥ; yadvāha—khalakhaḍuṣamabhrāsakāpacatā
sthānamanavasthāyo: patāti, muhyataśca |
yadvāha—purataḥ prāgabhārakāyatā; gamanāvasthāyāṃ yadāha—daṇḍavaduṣṭamanatā
prajñāvipariṇāmalakṣaṇāṃ parikṣedaścarāṃ jaṅghayate darśanevāha—dharmadhuraṃdhmiti
dharmadho yataḥ sugatitānāṃ dharmāṇāṃ niravadyānām, durgatitānāṃ sāvadyānāmadharmādharmu
mprativiśo bhavati, kathyaṅkuśca vā jñātum | āśrayabhedopeto'syaṅkaramayādvaya parikṣedaścarāṃ
smṛtivipariṇāmalakṣaṇāṃ darśayevāha—sampratyutpiti |
pustakopaghāto maithunamalavipa riṇāmalakṣaṇāṃ darśayevāha—hāniriti |
pustakopaghāto maithunaparibhogudar tatānāṃ hāniḥ |
ābhimantryabhāvataḥ āhārarāsibhogudaravipariṇāmalakṣaṇāṃ jaṭha darśayevāha—parihāṇiriti |
vidhīyaṣulabdhivedyaiveto vināśādabdha yathākrama nirdiṣṭavipariṇāmalakṣaṇāṃ parikṣedaścarāṃ darśayeṃ vāha—indriyāṇāṃ paribhāka[153]
āyu:saṃskarāṇāṃ saraṇopakṣepeṇa āyurvipariṇāmasaṃkṣepād jarā idamāha—saṃskārāṇāṃ purāṇībhāvaḥ iti |
svairyāpacārādyasadatvena yadāha—jarjarībhāvaḥ iti |
saṃskārāṇāsyāliṅganam |[154]
saraṇaṃ kṛtamaddhiti |
yā sphutirucyamaneneti vistareṇa daśa paryāyāḥ|
tatra yasmāt sañcikāyā'cyavate tatrjikāyasamānaviśeṣaḥ sphutiḥ |
maraṇāvasāno hi skhavate |
sa skhavanaḥ |
taddrāvañcyamṛtatvaṃ yadasadṛśaścyavate |
akṣuṇo'nāmakāyasya rūpakāyādi raṅgo bhedaḥ |
kīdṛśaḥ sa ucyate ?
anuprahīṇavātaśyāmagnoyātṛptimārjaikarūpaṃ |
pradasya rūpakādīndriyāṇāṃ cakṣurādīnāmucchede'ntardhāṇaṃ |
sarveṇa sarvamucchidyate tadanīniti kṛtvā dvividhasthitihetuparigrahaṃ yathākrameṇa darśayatvāha—āyuṣo hāniḥ; uṣmaṇo hāniriti[145]
pūrvenamākṣipatyupaguptaḥ āhāraupakalpanasya ca iti dvābhyāṃ bhedo'ntadvārṇiḥ|
itaretarāśrayaṃ padārthayoḥ cyutirnirdiṣṭautā| dvābhyāmāpūrṇo dvānirūpamṇo dvāniritaretarāśrayau
cyunnatā darśitā| evaṃ hi kṣīṇopaguptaḥ kameṇa yā cyutiḥ [ yāṃ ca cyutnatāṃ ]
tadubhayaṃ maraṇamityākhyātam| yaḥ punaḥ sāvaśeṣopaguḥ sahasā miyate tasya
jīvitendriyanirodhaḥ syād yathaiva—jīvitaṃ hi mṛtyusya nirodhaḥ| jīvitendriya-
vattāṃ svakarmaṇāṃ nirodhaḥ yadvāha—svakarmaṇāṃ nirodhaṇaḥ| iti sarveṇaiva maraṇamākhyātam|
kaḥ punaḥ jīvitendriyanirodha ityāha—kālakriyā| pūrvoktavāyuḥsaṃskāroṇāmaviśeṣaśca[156]
ityarthaḥ |
uktaṃ jarāmaraṇāṅkam ||[116]
pratītyasamutpādānantaraṃ satyānukīrtani |
yathoddeśaṃ ca nirdeśa iti |
tānyākhyātukāmaḥ prajñapayati —tatra katamāni catvāryāryasatyānīti |
auryārṇayetāni satyāni tasmādāryasatyānīti evaṃ praboddhavyam |
kimanyeṣāmetāni na satyāni ?
sarveṣāmetāni satyānyaviparītatattvāt |
āryaistu yathetāni tathāṣṭāni[518]
na punarevamaye'pi |
ata āyāṇiṣaitāni satyānyucyante | na tvanāyāṇi viparītadarśanen |
"pradvārau sukhataḥ prāhustatpare duḥkhato viduḥ |
yatpare sukhataḥ prāhustadarvāṅ duḥkhato viduḥ" ||
iti gāthāvacanen |
kasmāt punaḥ satyānimeṣo'nukramaḥ—duḥkhaṃ yāvanmārgaṃ iti ? ucyate[149]
yasya yasya satyasyābhisamayaḥ pūrvaṃ tasya tasya pūrvanirdeśa iti |
yathābhisamayaṃ nirdeśākramaḥ |
kasmāt satyānāmevābhisamayaḥ ?
ucyate |
yato duḥkhe sakto duḥkhe(18b)na bādhyate, duḥkhācca mohaṃ prāpnoti |
tadevātro parīkṣyate |
paścāt ko‌'sya heturiti samudayamṛcchati ; tataḥ ko‌'sya nirodha iti nirodhamṛcchati ;
tata ko‌'sya mārga iti mārgamṛcchati |
vyādhi iṣṭā tadvidānusayamevajñānavedhaprahāṇaṃ ca |
ete'pyeva eva satyānāṃ dṛṣṭānto darśitaḥ | katham ? "caturbhiraṅgaiḥ sambandhāgato bhiṣak[160]
śaṭyahaṃ vā rājāhaṃśca bhavati, rājayogyaśca bhavati, rājānaśce ca saṃkhyā gacchati|
katamaiścaturbhiḥ? ātmacakruśalo bhavati, ātmaśradhuutyānukūśalaḥ, ātmaśrayuutyānuśalaḥ, pradhīṇasyātmabhūtyātyamyādukūśalaśca|
evameva caturbhirbhaṃgaiḥ samyagyānstathāgato'haṃta samyaksambuddho'nuttaro| bhikṣūṃ śaṭyahatuṃsaṃjñayate|
katamaiścaturbhiḥ? iha bhikṣustathāgato'haṃta samyaksambuddha itīdaṃ duḥkhamāryasatyamiti yathābhūtaṃ prajānāti|
ayaṃ duḥkhasamudayaḥ, ayaṃ duḥkhanirodhaḥ, iyaṃ duḥkhanirodhagāminī pratipadāryasatyaṃ yathābhūtaṃ prajānāti|
(duḥkhasatyam—1)
jātirduḥkhamiti saṃskāraduḥkhatā|
jarā duḥkhamiti vipariṇāmaduḥkhatā|
vyādhirduḥkhamiti duḥkhaduḥkhatā|
śokaṃ sugamam|
saṃkṣepeṇa eñcopādānaskandhā duḥkhamiti phalagatā(mi)duḥkhoghasaṃgrahāt|
nanu vedanaikadeśo duḥkham|
kathaṃ sarve pañcopādānaskandhā duḥkhamityucyante|
triṣu duḥkhatā''—duḥkhaduḥkhatā, saṃskāraduḥkhatā,[161]
vipariṇāmaduḥkhatā ca | tānmithyāyogān sarve sālambāḥ skandhā duḥkhā ucyante |
yadā sukhamapyāsti kasmād duḥkha(19 a)mevābhisaṃmucyate | sukhasyā-[162]
rupavyāt | katipayadurgāṇisudūrairapi saṃdrāve māparāśiṣyapadeśavat | ko hi vidvān pariṣkṛtaghaṭaśāṭukena gaṇḍūḥ sukhamiti vyapadiśet ? uktaṃ duḥkhamatyagam ||
(samudravasuyam‌—2 )
samudrayamatvaṃ vyākhyātukāmaḥ prastuyati—duḥkhasamudrāgomatsyaṃ kṛttveti |
samudrasyamāhuḥkhamiti samudrayaḥ | samantādrudyate'trāsātiśayaiḥ | duḥkhasamudrayo duḥkhasamudrayaḥ, duḥkhasya hetutve'pi | tṛṣṇā ponarbhavikī nandī rāgasahagateti vitaraḥ | punarbhave avagāhe janma | tatsamayojanaṃ ponarbhavikam |
tṛṣṇā | nandī kiṃcit saumanasyam | rāgastu saṃkleśalakṣaṇa iti prasiddhaḥ | tadbhedena punarbhavayojanā, yathā 'syāmi'miti prārthayate, na tu 'vaidaḥ syām‌ manuṣyo ve'[163]
bhaitreya, sā paunarbhavikī tṛṣṇā|
sā ca pravṛttaprātmabhāvabhogasaṃtvācchannamātmārthāṃ nandīrāgākhyāṃ payāpteṇa samudācāratatvadṛṣṭaprabandhaguṇaistān nandīrāgasaṃprayukteṣvucyate|
sahaśabdaścātratattvārthe|
nandī rāgāṃtaraiḥ samupaśrayate ityarthaḥ|
'maitrīsahagatastṛptisaṃbodhyaṅgāvatā'canavat|
yathā "maitrīsahagataṃ smṛtisaṃbodhyaṅgaṃ bhāvayato"iti maitrīsamanvayamiti gamyate, taiḥyā: sākṣātsahāyai smṛtisaṃbodhyaṅgasya cānāvakavāt sahakāriyogaḥ|
tathaihāpi tṛṣṇārāgayoḥ sahakāriyogo draṣṭavyasaṃmatavirodhāditi|
nandīrāgaśca tṛṣṇāyāḥ kāraṇam|
sā ca punarbhavaheturiti nandīrāgasaṃprayukteṣvucyate|
tattvatrābhinandanīti|
arthāpatticatrabhogāsaṃbhāvanārthatvāt|
bhedena punarbhava-yārthyaṃ vākyaśeṣā tṛṇoktā, yathā 'devaḥ syāṃ manuṣyo ve'iti vākyaśeṣaṃ|
tandayatra bhagavatā evaṃ kramodeśo'pi samuddeśasaṃkhyuktam|
kathaṃ kathaṃ ca tṛṣṇā cet? aśīghrapidhā saṃskārāṇāṃ heturamisaṃśaye, āśayāmidhādīnāṃ[164]
hetubhāvāt | hetuśca samudaya: | punaścoktam—"yadvadhya bhikṣavaḥ pañcavījajātānyamba-ṇaḍāmyacchinnapraṇayutīnyavātātapahatāni na vā nisāraṇi pṛthivyāṃ sukhaśayitāni pṛthivīdhātuśca bhavati, abdhātuśca bhavati, tejodhātuśca bhavati | evaṃ vāni bījajātāni vṛddhiṃ virūḍhiṃ vipulatāmāpadyante | iti bhikṣava upamaiṣā kṛtā yāvadadhyāśeya vijñaptaye" | ayaṃ nidarśārtho dṛṣṭavyaḥ | "pañcabījajātānī"ti sopādānasya vijñānasyetadupacayavacanam | "pṛthivīdhātu"riti catasṛṇāṃ vijñānasthi-[165]
tīnāmeva tridharmatvam |
bojaṃ guruṣṭetuḥ |
atha hetuḥ sa samudrayaḥ |
vijñānāsthi-tayo'pi pratiṣṭhahetuḥ |
yat eva kramāvināhanopagami hetutvatvajanan samudrayasaṃkhairva siddhayet ‌, kasmādviha
dūre tṛṣṇīva samudrayasamudrayakam ?
araheteṣu |
pradhānahetumānādeyamupagetem |
tathā[166]
hi tṛṣṇāsidhyarthimidaṃ karma punarbhavāṅkurasamartham, na kevalaṃ karma|
āśayasya punarbhavahetutvāt sthāyinivṛttau mūle tṛṣṇāti nirodhasahāyam|
avidyā karma ca sāmānyakāraṇam moharahitasyā'nuśayasyābhāvāt|
tathā na dvādaśau karmaṇi punarbhavavedanīyāni trāṇi vairāgyeṇa kāni na vidyante|
tṛṣṇā|abhāvāt tu punarbhavānivṛttau naiva samarthāni|
ata evoktam—"mūle bhavasyānuśayā:" iti|
uktaṃ samyudyasūtram||
(nirodhasahāya—3)
tadanantaraṃ nirodhasatyamāha—duḥkhanirodha āryasatyaṃ kathamiti|
duḥkhasya nirodho duḥkhanirodhaḥ|
sārvasya vastutaḥ prahāṇāniṣṭyeti śiṣṭadeśanā|
tasyā evaṃ tṛṣṇāyā dūrādiḥ|
atraivataḥ prahāṇaniruddheṣu mithyādṛṣṭeṣu yāvadanuttaraṃ kṣayajñānaṃ pravṛttāni|[137]
kṣepo nirdeśaḥ|
kutaḥ ? sarvavisthāpakaprahāṇastadarśanāt|
sarvavisthāpakāṇāṃ vinodavakṣya yadāha—pratiniḥsargaṃ iti|
pratiniḥcchupramne vinodamna icchayaiḥ|
vinodanaṃ punarutpatteśca kleśasya vaimukhyabhāvena kṣepaḥ|
viṣkambhaṇārthaśca yadāha—vyantībhāva iti|
viṣkambhitā nirvedhabhāgīyādināṃ|
vyantībhāvo vyabhāva ityarthaḥ|
darśanamārgārthaśca yadāha—kṣaya iti|
kṣilapraheyayenairva darśanaheyānāṃ prahāṇāt|
bhāvanāmārgārthaśca yadāha—virāga iti|
rāgādeścaprakṣīṇat|
aśeṣamārgārthaśca yadāha—nirodha iti|
niravaśeṣāpunapraprahāṇāt|[116]
vyāvaraṇavimānasthaṃ yadāha—cyupagama iti|
aviruddhacyupāvaraṇasya vigamādatyarthāmo cyupagama||
upeṣivimānasthaṃ yadāha—acyudgama iti|
pukkusābhigamane sarvotkṛṣṭadvāṇāmato na dṛṣṭihi syāt|
anye tvanyathārthe kaipācit padānāṃ kathayanti|
pratigiḥicchante puratisarge prati sati sarvonupūrvayāvama iti prativiṃ:sargo nirgamam|
viśeṣaṇato vyaktaḥ pradvairavasānam|
addhyano vyasanobhavatīti vyadyano'bhāvo nirgamabhāva ityartho|
pañcopādānaskandhalakṣaṇasya vastunaḥ prahāṇaṃ nirodhaḥ|
viśeṣeṇa dvamaḥ sarvopadravasthaivasimpū prāpte saṃtīti cyupagamaḥ|
sarve'ya saṃskṛta( ) kṣyāsaṃskṛtasya sārvasthānātvasyāsāvasyamāvānudgumaḥ|

uddeśanirdeśaśca pūrvavadeva|[169]

uktaṃ nirodhasattvam ||
tadanantaraṃ mārgasattvam |
tadabhidheyāha—duḥkhanirodhagāminī pratipadityādi |
duḥkhasya nirodho duḥkhanirodhaḥ, nirvāṇam |
tadgamanaṃ prāptau śobhamatyā iti duḥkhanirodhagāminī pratipat, āryamārgaḥ |
mārga eva hi pratipadityucyate, nirvāṇapratipadyanāt |
āryodyaṅka iti vistaraḥ |
ārya ityanāryaḥ |
mārga iti mārgaprasthena somino nirvāṇamiti mārgaḥ |
śaṣṭhākulaṃ kathamityāha—tadyathā samyagdṛṣṭiryāvatsamyaksamādhiriti |
eviśya niṣkampo mārgo darśitaḥ |
śrotratvamārgasvāt samyagvākkarmāntānajīvāḥ śrotrakramaḥ |
samyaksamādhiḥ samyaksmṛtiśca samādhiskandhaḥ |
saṃsārottarutyarthāt smṛtiḥ samādhiskandha ityucyate |
tathāhi upasthitasya smṛtervikṣepācittaṃ samādhīyata iti |
samyagdṛṣṭiḥsaṃkalpavyāyāmāḥ prajñāskandhaḥ |
saṃkalpavyāyāmo na prajñāsvabhāvo, tayoryaṃśaktaṃ vivakṣītorthasmākṣāt |
tatyāstu prajñāyā anuguṇatvādi taducyaparihau |
uktāni catvāryāryasatyāni ||[116]
ārṣesaṃsthānantaramindriyāṇyukānīyāha—katamāni dvādaśatirindra-yāṇī'ti|
indriyatītīndriyāṇi, svaviṣaye ādhipatyaṃ kurvantītyartha: |
cakṣurindriyamiti vistaraḥ| karṇendriyasya cakṣurindriyam |
cakṣurādīnāṃ kāyaparyantānāṃ svasya svasyāyaikopakramāvāviṣayam, (21 a) manasaḥ punaḥ sarvārtha-[101]
pañcame | strīve strīndriyasya, puṃstve puruṣendriyasya |
nikāyasabhāgasthitau jīvitendriyasya, saṃjñau pañcānāṃ vedanānām |
tathāhi "sukhāyāṃ rāgo'nuśete duḥkhāyāṃ dveṣaḥ, aduḥkhāsukhāyāmavidyeti"yuktam |[152]
pañcānām | tathāhi vaiśeṣikarmaṇimite: keṣāñca vikramante, nirvedhabhāgīyakā
mārgāṅgābāhyate | darśanaheyakleśaprahāṇaṃ pratyākhyānagāmindriyasya | bhāvanāheyakle
prahāṇaṃ pratyākhyānindriyasya | eṣadharmasulavihāraṃ pratyākhyātāvīnindriyasya, vimuktipha
sulasaṃvedyanāvaṃ |[173]
tatra cakṣurādīndriyāṇānāṃ kāmadhāto rūpadhātau ca sambhavo na cārūpyadhātuṣu|
tatra rūpadhāvāt, teṣāṃ ca rūpadhāvanabhāvāt|
strīpuruṣendriyadvaye: kāmadhātāveva sambhavaḥ|
manojīvitendriyadvayostvatrāpi bhāvayu sambhavaḥ|
kāmadhāto pañcavijñānakāyikasamātaṃ cetitaṃ duḥkhendriyam|[174]
rāmasya mānasaprasāde veditaṃ dvaurmanasyendriyam |
kāmacorāmasya dviśalyā vedanokā yūte |
kāpikaṃ duḥkhaṃ cetasikaṃ ca dvaurmanaskam |
kāpikaṃ duḥkhamiti vacanāt |
kāmaprayo pañcavijñānakāyikaṃ sātaṃ veditam, prathame ca dhyāne trivijñānakāyikaṃ sukhendriyam |
mānasī ca sātā vedanā tṛtīye dhyāne sukhendriyam |
mānasī ca sātā vedanā kāmadhātau, prathame dhyāne dvitīye ca saumanasyendriyam |
madhyā tu vedanā'ḍuḥkhāsukhā kāyiko caitasikī cāvisamprayogendriyam, avikalpakatvāt |
tat triṣvapi dhātuṣusti |
kathaṃ jñāyate ?

dvitīye dhyāne'dhyātmasaṃprasādaḥ pratipakṣātmam |
tena vitarkavicārapratipakṣaṇāt |
prītisukhaṃ cānuśaṃsātmam |
vivekakāmatā pūrvavat |
tṛtīye dhyāne upekṣāsmṛtisaṃmanve ca pratipakṣātmam |
taiḥ prītipratipakṣaṇāt |
sukhamanuśaṃsātmam |
vivekakāmatā pūrvavat |
caturthe dhyāne upekṣāpariśuddhiḥ smṛtipariśuddhiśca pratipakṣātmam |
tāsyāṃ dhyāneṣu sukhapratipakṣaṇāt |
aduḥkhāsukhaṃ vedanānuśaṃsātmam |
vivekakāmatā tadubhayasattriśrayātmemiti |[116]
yathā kāmapratisaṃyuktānāmanugrāhyāṇāṃ rūpapratisaṃyuktaṃ jñānaṃ pratipadya, tathā rūpapratisaṃyuktānāmanugrāhyāṇāmākhyeyapratisaṃyuktaṃ jñānaṃ pratipadya ityāha—sarveṣāṃ rūpasaṃjñānādi|
odārikarūpavibhāgāt sarveṣāṃ iṣyakam|
tadbhaya tu tatra rūpaṃ vidyata ityeva|
tatra|
'khyāṇāṃ niḥsvaraṇamākhyeya' iti sūtre[190]

aratibahulānāṃ tatprahaṇāya muditā |

kathamādikarmiko meghāṃ pradhuñjate ?

smṛtisamprayogaviśuddha ityāha—yathādraṣṭamupaśrutānubhūtāni|[223]
kāmādyābhidheyo'nukramaḥ kāyasmratyupasthānaṃ ko'nukra dharmasmratyupasthānamiti ?
ucyate | yathoktavistaḥ karma | karmādvimukhyatiḥ ? yadedātmikaṃ tasya tasya pūrvaṃ darśanāt | yato vā kāmarāgasya kāye'bhidvānam | sa ca caittyanābhiśavāt | sa cittasyādyanasaṃvedaḥ | tatuguḥ nairaśyaprahāṇāt |
yathākramameva caiṣāṃ nāma | tathā hi kāyasmratyupasthānasya kāya ālambanam |
evaṃ vedanādīnāmapi yojyam | vyākhyāyatāni smratyupasthānāni ||[214]
tadanantaraṃ samyakprahāṇānyucyante | tāthāha—tatra kṛtamāni catvāri samyakprahāṇānīti | samyakprāyahmanānīti pāyaścittīti pradhānānīti | kṛ(28a)[225]
ucchvāsānāṃ pāpakānāmiti vistaraḥ | pāpakānāṃ rāgādīnaṃ sambhāva-
saṃprayogāsusthānānām | prajñāpāyasamuddhācāravaśīkaraṇatayā | chandaṃ janayati |
chando hi dīrghakṣamataṃ ityādau teṣāṃ prahāṇaṃ katuṃ kausītyullekhaṇaṃ chandamūlādayati |
śamathamiti tamogaṃ mahāyānaṃ tenāśupatyaparigrahādvaṃ vyākhyaṇḍuktate | asya cobhayapari-
grahasya bahulīkāraṭṭīkārasthau vīryamārabhate | tatra bahulīkāraḥ sātatyakriyayā[216]
idākāraḥ | saṅkṛtya kriyayā samāhitamanoge layabhāvanācittaṃ praṇuṅkti autsukyabhāvanācittaṃ pravaśayati |
anusmṛtyavākyati | keṣām ? teṣāmeva pāṭakānāmanusmaraṇānusmāve
atyantānurakticittenodyatānaṃ | chandaṃ janayatīti pūrvavat | tathā kuśalānāmanusmara
ṇānāmahṛdaye | chandamiti pūrvavat | teṣāmevānusmaryāṇāṃ kuśalānāṃ śraddhā
dīnāṃ hi yattaye | chandaṃ janayatīti pūrvavat |
athavā śīrṣaprayogotpannaḥ chandaṃ janayatīti | prayogadvāramāva
vyācakṣṭte | kācinmaitrīsakumārodāyādipi bodhisattvakṣaṇād vīryamārabhate
śamathaikapuśye vaśenācittaṃ praṇuṅktīti vipaśyanaikapṛṣṭhe vaśenācittaṃ pravidadyāti[247]
te punastaduṅkuraṇau jyotsnodayāveca yathākramam |
saṅkramya vīthīmavanāvāṃ prayogamāha chandaṃ janayeti |
'kausīvimarśanādu vyāghrañcandrate | kāyaṃ cittaṃ cābhimukhī-karotītyarthaḥ |
jyotsnodyāparkaṣeṇoddu vīryamārakṣate | kathamārakṣate ? kṣīrṇa cittaṃ prakṛtyati saṃsnenārthīryamodīryaṣarṣemanāṅkārai: |
uḍḍīrya cittaṃ tatsiṣevātmane samyaggṛhṛṇīte |
dhārayati pravartayatītyarthaḥ | tathotpannānāṃ kuśalānāmapari-hāṇiḥ: sthitiḥ; pravardhenotsāho bhūyobhāvaḥ; bhaṅkavaiklavaṃ paripuririti puruṣārtha[116]
ṣaḍvargopekṣā|
eṇimadyuddhiḥ vīthīraivete caturvargaṃ dṛṣṭiradiśatā|
tadevaṃ sampūrṇapadhānāni vīthīsaṅkāntīti jñātavyaṃ (28 b) bhavati|
atha kasmācchṛṅkhalanaṃ druṇapakṣo vā prathamataḥ upanyastaḥ?
ucyate|
prakṛṣṭapadaṃ hi sukhāvabodhaṃ nānupūrvakam|
kṛṣṇapakṣo vā sukhanubuddhyate videśagamaneti||[116]
samyakprahāṇānantaraṃ catvāra ṛddhipādā: |
tān darśayannāha—tatra krameṇa catvāra ṛddhipādā iti |
sarveṣāmirjādikasya guṇasya samṛddhe: ṛddhi: |
tasyā ṛddhe: pratiṣṭhānena pādā: ṛddhipādā: |
ṛddheretatra[220]
ityarthaḥ | te punaraṣṭasaṃskāramārgeṇaivamīmāṃsālakṣaṇāḥ | tān yathākramaṃ darśayannāha-
iha bhikṣavo bhikṣuḥ chandasamādhipradhāṇa ityādi |
chandahetukaḥ samādhiśchandasamādhiḥ | chandamadhipatītyanupūrvata ityarthaḥ |
tatra chandasamādhiryenādhipatyādīnībhavanānāṃ saṅkṣayeṇa pravartanataḥ spṛśati cittasya kāmatāṃ | prahāṇāya saṃskāraḥ prahāṇasaṃskāraḥ | kenasā prahāṇāya ? pañcānāṃ doṣāṇāṃ kausīdyasamoyaśyauddhatyānirvedasaṃkīrṇānām | prahāṇasaṃskāraḥ punarevaṃ labhyate pratyakṣataḥ[221]
chandaśyāmapraśvatārthaśritamutisavijayetanopeśalakṣaṇau: |
chandasamābhipraślaṇasaṃkāro |
tabhyāṃ samanvāgataṃ sahitaṃ yuktaṃ ṛddhipādaṃ bhāvayatīti niyamaṇabhāvanayā |
vivekaniśritamiti |
vivicyate prahīyate 'nena kuśa iti viveko mārgaṃ ucyate |
tatriśritaṃ tatanukramam |
tadāśrayaṇamiti vā |
virāganiśritamiti |
rāgaprahāṇaṃ virāgo nirvāṇamityartha: |
nirodhaniśritamiti |
sāvakastuvisaṃyogo nirodha: |
tadāśrayanam |
vyavasargopariṇatamiti |
vyavacchede tyajyate saṃkleśe saṃskṛtaṃ vastu tasmin satīti vyavasargoṃ visaṃyoga: |[222]
tannukūlamityarthaḥ|
(2) dvitīyamāha—vīryasamādhirityādi | tatra vīryasamādhiryadā bodha bhāramāyāya: sātatyaprayogāt sphurati cittasyaikāgratām | śeṣaṃ pūrvavat |
(3) tṛtīyamāha—cittasamādhirityādi | cittasamādhiryadā pūrvasamādhi vīryamāgrahya ci(29a)ttaṃ (?) etad cittaṃ dhārayan sphurati cittasyaikāgratām śeṣaṃ pūrvavat |
(4) caturthamāha—mīmāṃsāmādhiriti | mīmāṃsā prajñā | tat mīmāṃsāsamādhiryadālambanaṃ vicinvan sphurati cittasyaikāgratām | śeṣaṃ pūrvavadevaṃ tadevaṃ samādhyāsakāṃ ṛddhirpādā ityuktaṃ bhavati ||[16]
ṛdvipādānantarabhikṣuṇā puṇyakāni|
tānyeva—tatra kṛtapāni pañce-ndriyāṇīti|
yadutta arhadvindrayaṃ yāvat prajñendriyamiti|
śraddhā cetasa: prasāda:|
kuśaladharmeṣvabhilāṣo bhīmadyaṃ|
smṛtirāśayaniyamoṣa:|
samādhiścittasyaikāgratā|
prajñā dharmavicaya:|[224]
indriyāṇāṃ punaḥ kramaḥ |
aśramaṇo hi phalārthī bodhamāropate |
ālasya-bībhatsya smṛtirapanihite |
upasthitasmṛterviśeṣāṇibandhaṃ samādhīyate |
samāhitacitto yathāsṛtaṃ prajānāti |
saṃsārāvacarāśiti |
saṃsārāvacārītineṇa |
lokikīṣa |
loke bhavā lokikī, tādṛś |
samyagdṛṣṭe paralokātisaṃrakṣaṇārth |
kalyāṇamiti kuśalam |
paudgalamiti akuśalam |
vipākamiśrāniṣṭakuśalaṃ bratisaṃvedayiṣyāmi |
bodheṇa smṛdyānugṛhya, kuśaleṣu avyutsāhaścāprākāśādu bodheya |[225]
śeṇa na viguṇānupaiti, ālambanāsaṃmevecchuprāṇavāt spṛhet |
ekābhīkṣṇakaroti, cittekagatālambaṇavyattamādhe: |
prativiṣyaiti sākṣākkaroti, dharmavicayastvabhāva-tvāt prajñendriyasya ||[116]
indriyāntaraṃ balamityāha—karmaṇāni pañca balānīti|
śraddhāvalamityādi|
yāvat prajñābalamiti|
tānyeva śraddhādīni pañcendriyāṇi balāni balānyucyante|
kathaṃ caiṣāṃ balaṃ vakṣyam? yasmādvipakṣairaśraddhādībhirmuktiviṣayāpasavyapravṛttikāraṇairetarāsaṃyutācārabhāvāt tāni nābhibhūyante, tasmād balānyucyante|
indriyāṇi[227]
punastaddhipakṣe| pañcabhistattvātmakaḥ samudācārādanubhūyate|

vyākhyāyāti balāni||[29b]
tattannaraṃ bodhyaḍgānyuktāni|
teṣāṃ ni(29b)rdeśādetara ityādya—
tra kramaṇāni sapta bodhyaḍgānīti|
āha—smṛtisaṃpramodhyaṅgaṃ yāvadupekṣā-
saṃbodhyaṅgamiti|
kā punaratra bodhiḥ? kiṃ bodhyaṅgamiti? ucyate|
dharmapratisaṃvidya
bodhirityādyarthaṃ ca|
viśāni bodhyaṅgānyeva|
tatra bodhirasyāmitisaṃbodhaḥ (?)
sa ca pudgalaśyenaiva tiṣṭho bodhayaḥ|
āryakamaraśyendriyasyakṣaṇasaṃbuddhabodhayaḥ[229]
dharmapravicayasu bodhyātmakatvād bodhirityucyate |
teṣāṃ ca smṛtyupasthānena sahāyabhāvādakampaṃ |
smṛtyādaya: puna: ṣaṭ bodherupāyatvabadaṃśatvāni |
tatra smṛtibodhe-[230]
rāśigrāham | sthavireścena tatsādrāl̤amnāvudviśeṣāt | ṣarmabhirvicayaḥ
sthabhāvāṅgam , jñānasthabhāvetāvu bodhe | vīryaṃ niryāṇāṅgam , tena praśrajanamasi-
samatikramāt | prītistukhasokharam , tathā kāyacittasukhahetut | praśrabdhisamā-
dhyupekṣā asaṃkleśāṅgam , teṣāṃ kleśapratipakṣatvāt | saṃskāropekṣā cāla-
grahate na vedanopekṣā, prītivacanāt , prītistu saumanasyasvabhāvatvāt | ekasmi-
kṣicacetakadhāpi ca vedanādyupadraprakṣayāsambhavāt | tatsātsaṃskāropekṣevetyupekṣāṅgamiti |
bodhyāṅgalakṣaṇāvu bhāvanāmārgaṃ bodhyaṅgānītyucyante | ucyate vīryasyaṅgāni ||[116]
bodhyaṅgānantaraṃ mārgāṅgānyucyante| tānyāha—tatra katama āryoṣṭāṅgo mārga iti|
āha—samyagdṛṣṭiryāvat samyaksamādhiriti|
atra ca mithyādṛṣṭipratipakṣeṇa yāvanmithyāsamādhipratipakṣeṇa samyagdṛṣṭyādīnāṃ mārgāṅgānāṃ yathākramaṃ vyavasthā|
āryamārgaṃ ca samāpannasyaikasminkṣaṇe yugapadaṣṭau mārgāṅgāni bhavanti|
bhavantu tāvatsamyagdṛṣṭyādayaḥ|
samyagvākkarmāntajīvāḥ kathaṃ bhavanti samāpannasya?[232]
kiṃso tatsāmarthyāyāṃ bāhyaṃ bhāvayet, kiṃyāṃ vā karoti, cīvarādīn vā paśyate na hi|
kathaṃ tarhi tatsāmarthyāyāṃ vyavasthāpyante? ucyate|
tanmārgasamāpannamānasavānityeti pratilabhate|
athavā: pratilambhādū vyutthito'saṃpradhāgāmiṣu pravartate(30a)teṣu na punasthitāvāgāmiṣu pravartate|
anye nirviśeṣenaiva kopacāraṃ kṛtvā'bhiśete tatrāsyaiva kiṃyate|
sautrāntikānāṃ punarmārgamārgasamāpattaya: yogino ninādhyavibuddhyā arthamārge'nuluptamārgamākhyā[233]
pratilambhate, yasya pratilambhād vyucchino'pi na punarniṣyāgamanādivu pravartate |
saṃprayogāgamādiṣu ca pravartate |
ato nimittanaimittikopacārāt kṛtsnā'ṣṭau mārgāṅgāni
vyavasthāpyante |
na tu svarūpatasteṣāṃ bhāva iti ||ṣoḍaśākārā ānāpānasmṛtiriti |
āgamīyanta ityāgān:, ye vāyavaḥ praveśyante |
apagamīyanta ityapānāḥ, ye vāyavo niṣkāsyante |
tadubhayānugā smṛtirānāpānasmṛtiḥ |
saiṣā prajñāsmārthāḥ |
smṛtivarcanaṃ tu smṛtivedanopalakṣaṇaṃ prajñāyāḥ prajñatve |
tatrāpi tu evaṃ ānāpānasmṛtimabhisamabhikrānte yogināḥ[235]
kalyāṇamitrārāpakṣena bhavitavyam , taṇapravicuddhatvāt bhāvanāvādasya |
tathā viśuddhaśīlena śrutacintāmahitena kāryacittavyagrakṛhena ca bhavitavyam |
evaṃ hi sā bhāvanā saṃpadyate nānyatheti |
tān ghoḍśākārāniha bhinnurityādinā nirdiśati |
sphuta āśrayam sphuta āśrayāmīti yathāsṛtaṃ prajānāti |
sphutaḥ praśvasan sphutaḥ praśvāsāmīti yathāsṛtaṃ prajānātītyevaṃrūpaḥ, sarveṣāmaṃśānānāṃ sāmānyopakṣaṇā-kāratvāt |
śeṣo'sya nirdeśo dīrghahrasvādyusaṃlakṣitā viśeṣopakṣaṇākāratvāt |
tatra sphutaḥ sphutimatispaṣṭaḥ | prajānātīti | tattvaiva bhāvanāyāṃ pravṛttasya bhāvanābhyāsavikamā gatiḥ saṃvivṛte |
(1) dīrghamāśvasāmītyādi | dīrghamityartham |
(2) tahipratete hṛsvam | prajānātīti ādhyātmaprajñāsā gatiḥ saṃlakṣyate |
(3) sarvakāyasaṃskāraprativedyo ceti | sarvaṃ ca te kāyasaṃskāra-rūvaite sarvakā(30b)yasaṃskārāḥ | te cādhyātmaprajñāyāḥ | tāttvikasaṃvedana-śīlaḥ | tadanuyogāt | śātavyakiṃcayā ete sarvakāyaprajñaptiva iti saṃvedanāt |
(4) prītiprativedyo ceti | prītipratisaṃvedanaṃ prathamadvitīyayordhyāna-[236]
samāsaścau saumanasyasaṃvedanāt | prītiśca saumanasyasamākhyānaṃ |
(5) śuṣkapratisaṃvedī leśaṃ | śuṣkaṃ pratisaṃvedayan tat prathamadvitīya-
yo'dhyānayoḥ praveśiṣyadyuktam , tṛtīyavyānasamāpattau mānasaṃ śuṣkam | uṣekṣāsukhayo-
rnirāhārānāpānastuteḥ, kathaṃ prītiṃ sukhaṃ ca pratisaṃvedayan āśvasatīti ? ucyate |
naiṣa doṣaḥ | bāhyā''lambanau prītiṃ viśuddhaṃ prītisukhaṃ ca yathyeṣamāṇa āśvasa-
tītisūkṣmapratisaṃvedītyucyate | tannaivaṃ sati ātmānamanuddharediti bhāvaḥ syāt ?
syāt | prayogāśrayavyātmaprabhañjastvevāt , āyyu vā svāśrayanaṣṭena grahaṇāt |
(6) pratipraśrabhyamāṇaṃ kāyasaṃskārānimitta | praśāmena praśrabhyamāṇa

(10) abhipramodayan iti chayoṃpramuhanihānāmanāt| mudrā yojaya-tīti abhipramodayan| tathā hi styānamiddhayogāddhīnaṃ bhavati| audatyaṃyogāt mṛdumadhyam| evaṃ viṣamatā̭nimuktena ruṃḍā ucyaṃte, tasmādabhipramodayannirucyate| tatur kasyacidapra prasṛtaṃnīyam (sic) nimittasyopasaṃhārāt| śṷddhatā-sahagataṃ vā pratisaṃvedya abhipramodayannirucyate|

apare tvāhuḥ | dīrghaṃ vā hṛsvaṃ vā śākyasabhiti | prayogakramasanikarṣācchaktikaṃ dīrgham | prakṛtībhūtaṃ ca hṛsvam | prajñānātīkṣṇyasaṃmohakinvāṃ darśayati | ādikarmikasya hi pratyavekṣaṇamaviyuktākṣāsamādhāroṣu sthitimato viharataste suspaṣṭā yānti | suspaṣṭatvād prahāṇaṃ tīvramacchanti | tasmin samaye tena yoginā dīrghaṃ kṛtvā copakṣitavyaṃ yāvannimitaṃ saṃvidyate | nimitte ca sthite prākkālikamevāsitavyam | idamatrāsato'ṅgikā | dīrghaṃ pūrvavadeva | atra ca yatra yatrāśvāsapraśvāsānatiriktamāśrayaṇaṃ tatra tatrāśvāsaṃ nasikāntarviṃśatyaṅgulayā[116]

loteśāparataṃ aṣṭāni, loteśāpanikampamānāvedāni hetusaṃtānīyathaḥ |
tāni ca buddhe dharme saṃghe cāvatrapyabhīraṃ śārīrakājñāni ca śīlabhraṃśanīti |
vāraṃ yathākrameṇāha—( 1 ) buddhe'rjekṣayaprasādeneti vistaraḥ |
buddhaśāsyādikau aśeṣāṃ dharmāḥ | buddhakarasaṃhṛ buddhaḥ | teṣu prasādaḥ rāgādikaścittatāyāṃ rajaḥ |
buddhe urjekṣayaprasādaḥ |[241]
avedayaprasādaṃ iti ko(31 b )'rthaḥ ? yathāsūtaṃ satyānyatvabudhya saṃprayogo'tretyaprasādaḥ | sa ca śāstrādhigamaprakāle vyavasthāpyate |
yathā vyatiśayaḥ saṃmukhīkaroti tathā nirdiśati—ityapi se bhagavānityādi vistaraḥ |
ebhiśca padairyathākramaṃ puṇyamāhātmyamabhidhīyatam | vinayaśraddhācchāndrānusaraṇam
vastusvabhāvapṛṣṭacchakṣaṇā, śrenaṃ hetunā yathā sa bhagavān bhavyāṃbhavyān sattvānavalokya
bhavyān vinayati, taśca śāstrasya śreṣṭhu pratiṣṭhitaṃ tattvābhidhīyatām |[242]
bhagavānityanena vivakṣabhūtaṃ darśayati, bhagavāniti kṛtvā nairūktena vicintya bhagavāneva|
kaḥ punaratra śāstṛtvasāmagrī vivakṣuḥ| deśavuddhamataḥ| tadvṛttena prathamataḥ śāstṛtvasāmagrī 'bhedhat|
tathāgato'ddevu samprasambuddha iti| punaḥ pude śāstṛtva sāmagrīṃ darśayati|
sā ca śāstṛtvasāmagrī dvividhā| vaktṛtvalakṣaṇā pratipattṛlakṣaṇā ca|
vaktṛtvamaviparītopadeśakartṛtvaṃ yena bhagavān tathāgata ityucyate, tenaivāviparītaṃ dharmaṃ gṛhṇatīti kṛtvā|
pratipattasaṃpadaṃ prahāṇasaṃpadaṃ jñānasaṃpadaṃ|
bhagavato'tra liṅgamucyate, arīn hatavāniti kṛtvā nairuktena vicintya| arayo vikleśāḥ, sarvakleśabhayopadravārthena|
jñānasampadā samprasambuddha ityucyate,[253]
samyagviparītaṃ sammāsād dharmān buddhabodhiti druktvā |
etāvatā ca śāstrasya samyak paripūrṇo bhavati-aviparītalakṣaṇadeśakatvena, sarvajñeyasamūhajñānena, sarvadharmāsammohena ca |
etacca vayaṃ bhagavatyevopalabhyate |
ato bhagavatyevaiṣato sarvākāraṃ śāstralakṣaṇaṃ prāpta iti nirdiṣṭate |
vidyācaraṇasampanna ityanena tasyāḥ śāstralakṣaṇaḥ prāptihetutvaṃ darśayati |
vidyācaraṇasādhīyastro mārgaḥ |

dvedhe'pyasyāti caraṇam, na dvayaprakṛtacaraṇo vā gantuṃ samartho bharvatīti druktvā |
tito vā vidyā "vidyācaraṇam" |
abhiprajñāvidyā vidyā, adhiśīlasamādhiśca ca śikṣe caraṇam, prajñāyāḥ pūrvabhūmikatvāt" t pṛṣṭasya"raṇaṃ caraṇamiti druktvā |

[ iti ] vidyācaraṇaśabdena tisraḥ śikṣā nirdiśyante"[244]

aitañcāturvidhyaṃ bāhyāśritadṛṣṭiyo vikṣepaṇārthaṃ |
coddhā hi na chandu gatāḥ purāvidyitāḥ |

evaṃ vidyānuśaṇa-sambhavā chandu niḥśeṣamaikṣasthā śāstrajñatvasaṃpadaṃ prāptasya bhagavataḥ śāstrajñarkauśalyadidṛkṣucaraḥ prasiddhadṛśyasāraraṇirūpetārthaṃ padārthāṃ darśayati |
tadguṇaḥ śāstrajñarkaṃ dvividham |[325]

lokaṃ caitoniṃ lokavid |

svarge mokṣe ca pratiṣṭhāyogyam, kasyānavaropitāni kuśalamūlānyavyavaropayedyam, kasyāvaropitāni paripācayedyam, kasya paripakvāni parimocayediti |
ataeva: pṛṣṭhatadṛṣṭaśāstrādhikṛtena bhavyāvinayaṃ darśayati |
bhavyābhavyalokānavalokya bhagavān vai pṛṣṭadṛṣṭyā damanārhaṃ damayituṃ vā samprakāśād vinayati, sārathiryānamivānāt |
damanaṃ hi sārathirbhāvaḥ |

avinayān punaḥ pratyayabalena hīnāvayāṃ duḥkhasaṃkṣayāte pratiṣṭhāpayati |

bhaviṣyadvā vipaśyaṃ śāstṛtavamarthaṃ yo hi veda saḥ |
