idaṃ ca samyaksaṃśabdakaviṣayaṃ sthānīyāprajñānalakṣam |[263]
ityavacāraṇāt |
asya devamanuḥ phalamāsādayiṣyati, śamanuṣyamūtaṃ ityavacāraṇāt |
hetuvo nijadāsa ityarthaḥ |
anena nijottaraje viṣṭhṛtaśatanetreniti |
īdṛśī vā sāmagrī matodyevaisya kamotpatte,
īdṛśī matodyevaisyamiti yathāmūtaṃ prajānāti |
vastutaḥ iti draṣṭavya ityarthaḥ |
yasya karmaṇo yadṛ dṛṣṭaṃ vipaśyati rūpaṃ gandho rasa ityevamādi |
smṛtimato vā jñānaṃ bahujñānam |
idaṃ karmaivaṃsamācaritaṃ sākalyena yadṛ jñānam |
vipākaśceti |
asya karmaṇo vipāko'nubhūya vipaśyatyasya nānadayam |
arddhayatastu kāle vipākānubhavo bhaviṣyatyasyetyavaniti |
idaṃ sākṣāttaṃ vipākaṃ karmaivaṃ tisṛbodhaṃ vipākaṃ vipaśyatīti |
paramasūkṣmatitiraṃśāya na vipākato yathāmūtaṃ prajānāti |
samātasato'yamasya karmaṇo vipāko'tra bhūsavayamasya karmaṇa iti |
yadvipākaviśānkairallālanamadhyādattaṃ taskairaijñānaṃ vipākajñānavaśyam |[264]
nābhin |
tatra hīnapraṇītādiṣu rūpinonānābhiyuktiḥ |
rūpirabhiyuktiriti payāyau |
sattvānāṃ rūpivividhye vibhāgajñānamadhyāhataṃ nānābhiyuktijñānamadhyam |
etaduktaṃ bhavati |
anyonyāśritaḥ sattvānāṃ viśeṣogatā ca yadvaśāddravyati tāṃ bhagavāneva jānātīti |
(4) anekaśyādakaṃ lokamityādi |
bhinnaśyādakaviyasaṅghai: |
tatra dhātuḥ sattvānāṃ pūrvādhyāyavasānasamudghātābhiyuktyevaviśeṣā: |
tadādyamanamadhyāhṛtaṃ jñānamevamuktam |
etadeva dvitīyena padena (37b) spaṣṭīkaroti—nānādhātu-kṛmiti |
idaṃ nānādhātukṛjñānamadhyam |
(5) parasparasvanāmasiddhiyugapādvṛttāmiti vistaraḥ |[265]
śraddhādīni | purāṇakukathāni | āparāṇi hīnāni | madhyāni hīnāni adhaiya parāṇi, utkṛṣṭamadhyāparāṇi ca prathamupadiṣṭāni smṛte |
śraddhādīnāmindriyāṇāṃ yathāvacanaprabhedenajñānaṃ ṣaṅuguṇyavibhāgamedenāpyohaṃstadvindriyaparāprājñāna-baṅam |
tathā coktam—
"parāṇastamaśūrṇānāṃ ṣaṅuguṇyavibhāgaśaḥ |
aṃśāṃśabhedeno'nantarya na svadṛśo'ṅgaṅgahṛtiḥ" ||
iti |
(6) sarveṃnagāminī pratipadyādi | tatra bahavaḥ pratipadaḥ |
nirodhagāminī devagāminī yāvatkarmagāminī | tatra yā devāditigāmibhyaḥ pratipadastā hetuḥ, pratipadyante tāmiṃrdeyatītiti kṛtvā |
nirodhagāminī bhūtikarmārthaṃ, visaṃyoga-prāpakatvādiśeṣe pratipadyante'nvayeti kṛtvā |
samāyataḥ satkāyasamudrayagāminī satkāya-nirodhagāminī ceti dvidhā |
tatra satkāyaḥ yathopādānasaṃbhavā: | samudrayastadutpādako hetuḥ |
satkāyanirodho visaṃyogaḥ | tatra sarven gantuṃ śīghramaryā: seyaṃ[226]
sarvajñagāminīṃ pratipad |
tatra jñānam |
tadevāṣṭādaśadhābaddham |
dhyānavimokṣasamādhisamāpattīnāṃ saṃkṣepavyagradānacaryāsthāneṣu yathābhūtaṃ prajñānātītyādi |
ya eṣa dhyānādiṣvabhisamaviśeṣaḥ sa saṃkṣepaḥ |
ya eṣa (sic) viśuddhisandarbhamudānam |
yaścaivaṃ punaḥ pāṭhaḥ—indriyavaśago'ṣṭaṅgadhyānavimokṣasamādhisamāpattīnāmiti |[267]
śraddhādīni | tānyeva nirjitaviṣayasyeva vṛttāni | godhastūṇāni bharmasaṃvicchādīni |
dhyānāni pidhapracchanādīni | vimokṣā aṣṭau rūpī rūpāṇi paśyatītyevamādayaḥ |
samādhayaḥ śūnyatādayaḥ | | samāpattinirodharmasamāpattikāḥ | tairnirodhādibhiḥ
dhyānādibhiḥ ca hānasthitiviśeṣanirbaṃdhabhāgīyādibhedātpunaḥṣaḍviṃśatiḥ |
samāpattiduḥkhahetuṣu yadabhyāhataṃ jñānaṃ tad dhyānānuttarajanakam |
punaraparamityādi | sākāramiti | ya uktacātukāro jātiḥ
dīrgho hṛsvo vāmana iti vā| tenākāreṇa sākāram | | sodeśamiti| upapadaviśeṣa
ucyate uddeśaśabdena | tena soddeśam | | tathā ca vakṣyati—[268]
'kasminnuddhāre tvayā'haṃ dṛṣṭaḥ ?' kasmin sthāna ityarthaḥ | satrimitragiri saṃhetukam | athaikākṣipraśnekavākyam | pūrvadigvātaṃ pūrvajanmanivāsam | evadeva spaṣṭayati—prakāmapi jātismṛtyādinā | evaduktaṃ bhavati | parevāmāsānadhyā tītajanmaparamparānusaraṇajñānamapyāhavaṃ pūrvanivāsānusmṛtijñānavacca | pūrvanivāsam cedaṃ jñāteru |
(9) punaraparāgnithādi | dvivedena cakṣurbhūte | adhamaviṣayavācakaśuddhā mānuṣakeṇāpi cakṣuṣā dūrasthaṃ rūpaṃ gṛhyate, na tu deśādiviśeṣapramitiḥ | atra āha—dvivedeneti | dvini bhavaḥ dvivyaḥ | sa copapatikṣaye'pi daivanāṃ syāt | na tvasāvihyucyate | ityāha—divyukte neti | abhijñāmayanetyarthaḥ | nanakṣa dvivya iva divyam | nanu dvinimatrādanijñāmayasya ko viśeṣaḥ ? ucyate | diviṣu svargamikasamayermilike ca rūpaṃ paśyati nodhvarmūmikam | abhijñāmayena tu sarvataḥ | tatra laukikabauterāgaṇau tu abhijñāmayo yasya thāvaro viṣayaḥ | śrāvakapratyeka buddhamayakṣetruddhānāṃ svanisartsakāriṇaṃ yathākramaṃ sāhasradvisāhasratrisāhasramahāsāhasra lokadhātuviṣayaḥ| abhisaṃskāreṇa tu yathākramaṃ dvitricatuḥsahasro'saṅkhyeyo jambudvīpa[269]
daiśikarkaṃ iti|
daśānāṃ svanāmasūtritasandhijñānasamudāhṛtaṃ cyutyuparṇītajñānavacanam|
aparāṃstāttvajñānaṃ stenaiv jñānam|
(10) ātmavaśyamādiyādi|
ātmavaśyaṇāṃ kṣaiśanāṃ śramāt prahāṇo-dvāsaṃvāṃ cetovidhṛtti cetaso maitrīya śraddhayā cārthaya majñāneti|
ātmavaśya-viṣaye yadadvāhṛtaṃ jñānaṃ tadātmavaśaprajñānavacanam|
iśati bhikṣuva ityādi nigamanīyam|[270]
idaṃ ca daśāvidhaṃ jñānaṃ tathāgatānāṃ vineyaparīkṣāyāmupayujyate :-
(1) sthānāsthānajñānena vineyānāṃ sadhyāmadhyatāṃ parīkṣate |
(2) karmasvakajñānena kamāvaraṇam |
(3) ghyānādijñānenātmāditvam |
(4) indriyajñānena cadhavadānavaśyaktiṃ jānīte |
(5) abhimuktijñānena prayogam, yasya yena prayogeṇa viśeṣaṇādhigamaḥ |
(6) dhātujñānenāśayam |
(7) sarvatragāminījñānena hitānuhitapravartanivāraṇau sampravibhaje |
(8) pūrvanivāsajñānena sammudācāraṃ jānīte |
(9) divyena cakṣuṣā pratisaṃdhim |
(10) āśravakṣayajñānena vimuktim |
anyatare vikalpo hi na śaktaḥ sakalaṃ cāstu kāryaṃ sampādayitum ||[116]
catvārīmāni catvāri vaiśāradyāni|
tānyāha—tatra kṣatamāni bhikṣava ityādi|
vaiśāradyānīti|
viśāradasya bhāvo vaiśāradyam|
samāsarthasākulyaṃva nirbhayatetyarthaḥ|
(1) samyaksaṃbuddha ityātmānaṃ pratijānīte ityanena sarvathāmisaṃbodhe kathayati|
"ime svathā dharmā nābhisaṃbuddhā" iti sarvathāsaṃbodhoktyā[272]
kaścino'nena, taṃ pratyāha—nibhṛtamapi na samyupeyāmi|
idaṃ prathamam|
( 2 ) ye vā punarīśā antarāriṣṭakā dharmā ākhyātaḥ iti|
abhigamanasvarūpakāḥ| tadyathā ānandajyatirlūpaṇāḥ|
tāṃśca pratisaṃkhyānendhuti|
prayoganīlūṣṭakaṃpīyathānonekatantraya dravyolabāṇāṃ vā ādisaṃmānasya nālambanīrāponiti|[273]
vaiśāradyasamabhimokṣato sevyamānā: kumbhenīti karatvapic tyādisaṅgraha—
niśitsamṛtiṃ vistara: |
idaṃ cātsātmikamamabhisambodhe vaiśāradyasuktam |
yā vā punarbhayā śrāvakāṇāmityādi |
prativiśiṣṭādhīmargai: |
āryo hratokta: |
naiyāṇikoti |
niryāṇāya prabhavati nairyāṇiko |
tatkarmapreti |
āryamārgasaṃyuktīkaraṇaṃ |
tacchabdenāryamārgābhisambandhaḥ |
ne sa mārgaṃ ityādi subodhaḥ |
idaṃ niryāṇamārgābhisambodhe'kṣaraṇaṃ vaiśāradyasuktam |
śrīṇāvacṛnasaṃyuktyādi |
prahīṇakleśasya sataḥ |
ime āśravāḥ kṣīṇā na prahīṇā iti |
pūrvavadeva |
anena prahīṇāsaṃvabodhe'kṣaraṇaṃ vaiśāradyasuktam |
atra ca prathamavaiśāradyaviśeṣa eva rupāṇi vaiśāradyāni |
vineyaviśeṣāpekṣayā bhagavatā caturdhā vyavasthāpitāni |
eṣāṃ ca baddhapaddhatyānukrāntaviśeyam,[274]
etāneva: svarūpasampattikamajñāne'ṣacavacanīyo'bhamān |
pūrveṇa hi veśāradyenātmajñānasamuddrajñāsitā, dvitīyena prahāṇasampat |
jñānaprahāṇasampadā ca samudraḥ svarūpamucyate|
etasmādevamidānīmuktaṃ | (sic) asamagopanivāraṇaṃ sanmārgopadeśaśca tatra tathāpratīpavidyāntarāthikaṣayepravṛttanetāt |
śiṣyaḥ: prahāṇasampadyupāyapratipattirbhavati |
tadyuktiṣṭe vaiṣṇāṅgakadharmasamyagbhaviṣyantstu jñānasampadyāyapratipattibhevati |
etāvatī ca śāstrasampat svaprayojanāthasamarthaṃ jñānaprahāṇasampatsamarthagītārthaḥ naccātyeva vaiśāradyāṇi |
tatra jñānaviśeṣa eva kaścidbh vaktavyamate kaścidverasme |
baddhaveśāradyabodhitane kaḥ prativiśeṣaḥ ?
ucyate | anantagrahaṇākāraṃ hi jñānavakṣm, arthakoṣṭīkaraṇaṃ vaiśāradyamiti ||[116]
(24) cattāraḥ pratisaṃvidaḥ
vaiśāradyajñānantaraṃ pratisaṃvidvinirdeśādāha—tatra katamāścatasraḥ pratisaṃvidaḥ
iti vistaraḥ |
(1) dharmeṣveṣu paramārthajñānamiti |
bhagavaṇāṃ paramārthe jñānamadhyāhṛtampratisaṃvid |
svasāmānyalakṣaṇānicchayā |
vivaraṇapravṛtti tasya na vyākhyeyā-[276]
tāni, vistarabhayātyathāt |
(2) dharmaskandhasaṃgrahiḥ | yaṅgharmaskandhebhyo vistaraḥ |
dharmaśabde nārthaṃ deśanādharmaḥ pratisṛjyate |
anekārtho hi dharmaśabdaḥ |
tadyathā—"dharmaskandho bhikṣavo deśayiṣyāmyādito kalyāṇam"iti deśanādharmaḥ |
tathā "dharma[277]
katama: ? āyodhyāṅgo mārga" iti pratipattipathaḥ | tathā "dharmaṃ śaraṇaṃ gacchāmī"ti phalabhogo nirodho'nut |
yatsaṃbandheṣu kuśalākuśalādiṣu anubandhaḥ: kuśalāyavacanena tattvayete* |
(3) niḥśaktipratisandhitṛ | niḥśaktirni( 38a )rvedanam |
yatsaṃvedaneṣu devādirateṣu anubandheścattvathodhyate |[278]
(4) pratimānamū nirsaṃjñita |
pratimā pratimodgam |
yuktamuktānilānitā |
yuktamurthaithamanyatham, yuktamasaṃtakam |
taddvābhau yuktamuktānilānitamiti vidyaṃ chugamatvānoktamiti ||[116]
pratisaṃvidantaramāveṇikā nirdiṣṭāḥ|
tānāha—tatra katame bhikṣava-stvāgatasyāveṇikā iti vistaraḥ|
vaiṇikībhīmāḥ|
na vaiṇiraveṇiḥ|
aveṇyāṃ sarvī āveṇikāḥ, asādhāraṇāḥ|
tathāgatasyaiva nānyasyetyarthaḥ|
asādhāraṇaṃ dvāveṇikṛmiti|[260]
nāsti tathāgatasya skhalitamiti|
kāyavāṅmanaḥprayatnānāṃ tatsambandhānāṃ prahāṇāt|
aprameye tu prāptakāle nāṃ kāyādiskhalitamuktaṃ bhavetsambandhāhārataḥ|
tathā ca saṅketapūrṇe upasthāyamāne śrutavai|
āyāryapilindavatsasya gaṅgāvuvasīmudratamiti|[281]
(2) racitaṃ sahasā vāṅmakriyo |
(3) gruvitasyṛtiteti | gruvitasyṛtobhego gruvitasyṛtitaḥ | sā nāsti |
(4) nāstyasamāhitacittalipi | nityasamāhitatvāt |
caran samāhito nāmasiddhānāḥ samāhitaḥ |
svapan samāhito nāmo niṣaṇṇo'pi samāhitaḥ ||
iti gāthābhidhānāt |
(5) nāsti nānātvasaṃjñeti | sukhaduḥkhāduḥkhāsukhavedanīyeṣu rāma-[383]
dvaiṣamohato yathākramaṃ pravṛttiḥ sā nānātvasaṃjñā | sā ca nāsti tathāgata-
syetyarthaḥ |
(13)-(15) kāraṇāntarāpekṣaṃ yadutpadyate tatsamutpadyata ityucyate | yadyunaḥ
kāraṇāntarānapekṣaṃ pravartate tadasamutpadyata ityucyate | ata āha—arthaṃ jñaptimiti |
yat kañcit pravartate kañcit pravartate tat pratihatamityucyate | yat punarapra-
vibandhaprabhāvāt' sarvatra pravartate tadapratihatamityucyate | tadevamāha—aprati-
hatamiti | jñānadarśanamiti | jñānameva prathamaśabdasyārthadarśanaṃ ca |
(16)-(18) sarvaṃ kāyādikarma jñānapūrvaṃkarma yadupasthitasmṛtisaṃpra-
jñāvataḥ ||[116]
lakṣaṇamabhidhattedāha—tatra kramemāni dvātriṃśallakṣaṇonīti|
tanu codahaśkāle lakṣaṇānyuddiṣṭāni|
kimarthaṃ punaḥ pṛthak kṛtvā uddeśaḥ kriyate?
ucyate| pūrvaṃ sāmānyalakṣaṇoktam, idānīṃ viśeṣalakṣaṇeti|
bahusamudāyabaṃdhe vā vyāhisatvādya idoddeśo lakṣyate|[284]
samayabhāvino'rthaṃ, tattāvadhānāṃ duṣṭodgrāhaṇārthaṃ ca |
udughṛṣṭacittānāṃ saṃkṣiptaśravīnāmanugrahārthaṃ punaruddeśaḥ kṛtaḥ |
vipulvijñānāṃ vistarasnehināmanugrahārthaṃ tadidaṃ vistareṇeti asmatpuruvaiḥ |
cakravartino'pi lakṣaṇāni bhavanti, na tu yathā bhagavato devādhidevasya |
pūrṇetaraṇīyāha—tathāgatasyeti |
sattvāntarasyaivacchedenāvadhāraṇārthamāha—dvātriṃśallakṣaṇānīti |
dvātriṃśadeva, nādhikāni nānyūnatarāṇi |
mahāpuruṣamahālakṣaṇasaṃjñānihetumāha—mahāpuruṣalakṣaṇānīti |
ma'dhyakramādiguṇānissallakṣaṇamāhmaśrayapratiṣṭhati mahāpuruṣaḥ |
tasya lakṣaṇāni mahāpuruṣalakṣaṇāni |[265]
lakṣyate bhāvinī phalaniṣpattiriti lakṣaṇāni | tānyahiṃsāgrāha—yadūtaṃ suprati-
ṣṭhitapādeti | nirdeśo vyākhyāyate :—
(1) supratiṣṭhitapādatā tathāgatasyeti | supratiṣṭhitapādamāhuḥ supratiṣṭhita-
pādatā | padmasūtratrāṇi bhāvayatyayo buddhimattvaḥ | yadūdṛśāssamavakrāntānukramadvāñca
bhagavān pṛthvyāṃ sarvaṃ pṛṣṭhīṃ spṛśati | māddvādrānauṣadhaiḥ pādatalayornigotvapāda-
pariṇāhau udgatāhau bhavatītyanye | kasya kṛteṇo nipātena lakṣaṇametaditi
lakṣaṇaśīrṣobhyadhagattajñānisaṃbharāṇāṃ śrautūṇāṃ sarvaviṣayopārtha paryeṣamāṇānāṃ karmekṣaṇaṃ-[266]
viṣādahetupāhe—pūrvaṃ ṭahamabhādānateyeti |
pūrvaṃ pūrvismin kāle govissya vasthāyām |
ṭahamabhādānateyeti |
sātatyasaṃskṛtakuśalasyabhogābhayamajanateyetyarthaḥ|
bhikṣāvu ṭuṭṭahetyevaṃ vā |
(2) atha(39a)stātapādatalayoścakrāṅkupādataleyeti |
yorapatsāva sanābhi sanemi sāraṃ cakraṃ bhagavataḥ sarvākāravaraṇīyaṃ sarvākāravaripūrṇaṃ tathā dantamayaṃ cakraṃ yuktiliṅganā paṭhitam |
yatra ca bhagavān padbhyāṃ mahī māmarśa tatra taspratibimbakaṃ cakra(kā)mudgate pṛthivyāmiti |[287]
mātreṇa bhagavān pṛthivīmaheśvarān gacchati | pādatalasparśanirghiṃṣṭakramābhidhānācaṃ udaybhavate iti |
viśiṣṭahetumāha—trividhadānopacayena nirvṛtvalabhate |
dānāviśāpuṇyaprasyāsamṛddhānopacayena prakarṣaṇonnataṃ nirvṛttaṃ janitaniḥśreyasaṃ |
na ca pādatalasyeva cakraṃ kiṃ tarhi karatale'pīti cakraṃ saṃbhavate | tayoḥ prakarṣalābhanirdeśaḥ |
(3) āgamanagamaṇyutsaṅgāditi | pādayoḥ pāṇyorbhaigavataḥ āyāvā[266]
bhavati | bahuramatīsopalikhatā | pādopari māsopalikhiśca | karmanirdeśaḥ spaṣṭaḥ |
(4) dīrghāṅgulikaretiṃ | indranāmanusucūraṇobhikrantakaracaraṇo na rajate ityāha-dīrghāṅguṣṭhalakṣitā | sudvatānāmanurūpeṇānyaḥ spaṣṭaguñjalikhatā dīrgha bhavanti | karmanirdeśaḥ spaṣṭaḥ |
(5) jālacchastapādeti | hastayoḥ prathaṅguliyugo haritarasunucama patihṛdayati | tenasa nābhito padmāda uparisphuṭāyau nāvāva ṭharitasuguṇagatiṣṭhā citra vicitra hoti | pratharakāyau ca duṅgati, suvena pādā parivartanti | pūrato pi pañcahuto pi kamaparasena pi ṭhatvā parasatātaṃ pādatāti pañcadhāmitra, na hayītaṃ citra pañcuto veva |[289]
bandhyāhito hastasaṃjñāṃ cakāre pratyaṅguliṣu līyate|
vivṛte tu haste vivṛtaṃ bhavatīti|
tathā herarājasya aṅgulīṃ kṛṇāyogopetahārahavidhiḥ|
evaṃ pādatalayoḥ pratyaṅguliṣārthaṃ iti pūrvabhojanīyamiti|
karmanirdeśaḥ|
pūrve paraparicārabhodyanentarāceti|
yugmābhilatvāt paiśunyacaritaiḥ pūrvasmin janmeti parasattvapariśā-ṣodakapātayā veditavyam|

pūrvoktameva śiṣyamukhenopatiṣṭhathaithaṃ smārayannāha—yaduktamādau kalyāṇamiti vistaraḥ|
tatrādau kalyāṇamityanena prātimokṣasthānīnānāvasadīralakṣaṇā śikṣā śodhitā|
tataśca samyakkarmaphalavibhāgaṃ kurvatā prāṇātipātāviratirūpacanānujñakā prātimokṣasaṃvarolakṣaṇā|
dhyānasamāpattinirdeśena dhyānasaṃvaralakṣaṇā|
tathārthakāmaśīlanirdeśena anāsavasaṃvaralakṣaṇā|
ityevaṃ trividho'piśīlaśikṣā śodhitā|
madhye kalyāṇamityanena adhicittaśikṣā|
sā caiva samādhibhāvanānirdeśāt|
adhiprajñā tu pariśiṣṭena sūtranirdeśeneti|
tatpramāṇamiti—idaṃ pratyuktaṃ mayeti|
iti hi bhikṣavo yūyuta śāstā "ācakaṇāmiti vistaraḥ|
avitatathopadeśakatvād bhagavān śāstā"|[310]
veraḥ śrāmyan iti śrāvikāḥ |
śrotrendriyāṇugā ityarthaḥ |

ṛṣabhaḥ śrāvakapratyekabuddhāḥ |
teṣāmatsādhāraṇamahākaruṇāvipuṇagahattvena bhagavān maharṣīrityucyate |
vivekadṛḍhavaśaddha bhāvanā saṃpadyate nānyatretyāha—
etāni bhikṣava ityādi |
vivekadṛḍhatva—kāyavivekabhikṣuvivekaśca tatra kāyaṃ |
viveko natasaṃsargavarjanam |
yathoddeśamahāī—imāni bhikṣavo'raṇyāyatanānīti vistaraḥ |[311]
sevatīti | cittepekaikaḥ punaruktaśabdavivekairvivecanīyam | yetsaviśeṣayannāha—sparśayat
